### [1] [Quantum Hall Fabry-Pérot Interferometry in Monolayer WSe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14633)
*Himadri Chakraborti,Charles L. Tschirhart,James Fang,Saisab Bhowmik,Chengrui Yu,Shujian Cao,William Caro,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Jie Shan,Kin Fai Mak*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在单层WSe₂中首次实现栅定义的量子霍尔Fabry-Pérot干涉仪，观测到稳健的Aharonov-Bohm干涉，并测得边缘速度与弱体-边耦合参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子霍尔Fabry-Pérot干涉仪是探测相干边缘输运的有力手段，但现有范德华材料平台存在多重自旋与谷简并，使干涉图像解释复杂。单层WSe₂因强自旋轨道耦合与自旋-谷锁定消除了这些简并，形成本征单分量朗道能级，从而提供干净、味分辨的干涉平台。

Method: 在单层WSe₂中制备首个栅定义量子霍尔Fabry-Pérot干涉仪；在单一器件中连续调节腔尺寸；通过温度与偏压依赖测量提取边缘输运参数，并结合静电学估算体-边耦合。

Result: 观察到跨越干涉仪的量子霍尔边缘通道的稳健Aharonov-Bohm干涉；AB区域占优与静电学估算的弱体-边耦合（Δ≈0.034）一致；温度与偏压测量一致给出边缘速度 v≈(4.9±0.2)×10⁴ m/s。

Conclusion: 单层WSe₂被确立为清洁、味分辨的量子霍尔干涉平台，为研究单分量分数量子霍尔态及过渡金属硫族化合物中任意子统计的干涉探测奠定基础。

Abstract: Electronic Fabry-Pérot interferometers provide a powerful probe of coherent quantum Hall edge transport, yet the presence of multiple spin and valley degrees of freedom in existing van der Waals material platforms can complicate their interpretation. Monolayer WSe$_2$ offers a distinct opportunity because strong spin-orbit coupling and spin-valley locking lift these degeneracies, producing intrinsically single-component Landau levels. Here we realize the first gate-defined quantum Hall Fabry-Pérot interferometer in monolayer WSe$_2$. By continuously tuning the cavity size in a single device, we observe robust Aharonov-Bohm (AB) interference of quantum Hall edge channels across the interferometer; the observed predominance in the AB regime is consistent with a weak bulk-edge coupling ($Δ\approx 0.034$) estimated by electrostatics. Moreover, temperature and bias dependence measurements consistently yield an edge velocity $v \approx (4.9 \pm 0.2) \times 10^4\,\mathrm{m\,s^{-1}}$. Our results establish monolayer WSe$_2$ as a clean, flavor-resolved platform for quantum Hall interferometry and provide a foundation for future studies of single-component fractional quantum Hall states and interferometric probes of anyonic statistics in transition-metal dichalcogenides.

</details>


### [2] [A discrete realization of the boundary sine-Gordon model and the Schmid transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14637)
*Nicolas Paris,Oscar Bouverot-Dupuis,Christophe Mora*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文构造了可精确映射到边界sine-Gordon（bsG）模型的离散模型，用团簇Monte Carlo高效模拟，给出了弱-强隧穿之间的完整普适交叉行为，明确了Schmid转变的存在性与性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: bsG模型（等价于电阻分流Josephson结）的相图近期存在争议，亟需在大范围参数下无歧义地确定相图结构与转变性质。

Method: 引入一个与bsG模型性质严格等价的离散模型，采用团簇Monte Carlo算法高效模拟；建立模型的自对偶性；与共形场论预言作定量比对，并与BKT转变作对比分析。

Result: 计算了整个参数范围内弱-强隧穿区域的普适交叉；明确识别出Schmid转变对应的竖直线临界不动点，沿该线与CFT预言高度吻合；厘清了Schmid转变与BKT转变的本质差别。

Conclusion: 结果确定性地证实了Schmid转变的存在及其性质，验证了Schmid-Bulgadaev图像的正确性。

Abstract: The phase diagram of the boundary sine-Gordon model (bsG), or equivalently of the resistively shunted Josephson junction, has been the subject of recent debate. We introduce a discrete model that reproduces exactly the properties of the bsG model and can be simulated extremely efficiently using cluster Monte Carlo algorithms. This enables us to explore a broad range of parameters and compute the complete universal crossover between the weak- and strong-tunneling regimes. We establish the self-duality of the model and unambiguously identify the vertical line of critical fixed points characteristic of the Schmid transition, recovering the conformal field theory predictions along this line with high accuracy. We further clarify the essential distinctions between the Schmid and Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless transitions. Our results definitively establish the existence and nature of the Schmid transition and demonstrate the validity of the Schmid-Bulgadaev scenario.

</details>


### [3] [Controlling the Vacuum: Tunable Magnonic Casimir Effect in Magnetic Moiré Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14809)
*Alejandro Roldan,Dante Navarrete,Mario A. Castro,Sebastián Allende,Roberto E. Troncoso,Alvaro S. Nunez*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 证明莫尔结构的磁自由度量子涨落可通过调节莫尔晶格来调控；基于Bistritzer-MacDonald方法，磁振子真空能与磁振子Casimir力成为莫尔图案参数的函数，可实现以真空涨落为工作介质的原型热机，提供一类新型磁-机械效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 莫尔结构中的量子涨落是否可被外部几何参数（莫尔晶格）主动调控尚不清楚，而这一调控若能实现，则可把真空涨落作为热机工作介质，产生新的磁-机械效应。

Method: 采用Bistritzer-MacDonald（连续介质）方法处理莫尔结构中的磁自由度，以"莫尔梯"（moiré Ladder）为具体范例，计算磁振子真空能与磁振子Casimir力随莫尔图案参数（如扭转角、晶格失配）的变化规律，并据此设计原型热机循环。

Result: 磁振子真空能与磁振子Casimir力均为莫尔图案参数的函数，可通过调节莫尔晶格加以调谐；由此构建的原型热机能够以真空涨落为工作介质运行。

Conclusion: 该效应被预期为莫尔结构的普适性质，与所涉激发的物理本质无关；虽以莫尔梯为例计算，但方法可直截了当地推广到任意莫尔结构。

Abstract: We prove that the quantum fluctuations of the magnetic degrees of freedom of moiré structures can be tuned by adjusting the moiré lattice. Using the Bistritzer-MacDonald approach, we explore the consequences of this fact for the magnonic vacuum energy and the magnonic Casimir force, which become functions of the moiré pattern parameters. This allows a prototypical engine to be run with the vacuum fluctuations as a working agent and, in that way, to furnish a novel type of magneto-mechanical effect. Our calculations focus on the moiré Ladder as an example but can be generalized to any moiré structure in a straightforward fashion. We anticipate that our results belong to a generic property of moiré structures, regardless of the physical nature of the excitations at hand.

</details>


### [4] [Interplay of the Fano-Andreev Effect and Nonlinear Screening in Electric and Thermoelectric Transport through a T-Shaped Double Quantum Dot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14991)
*Sachin Verma*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文理论研究T型双量子点与正常/超导电极耦合体系中的弱非线性电输运与热电输运，将非线性屏蔽用特征势形式化处理，揭示了Fano-Andreev效应与非线性屏蔽共同导致的偏压不对称Andreev电导、受抑制的准粒子热电响应以及亚能隙交叉热电响应的符号调控机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: T型双量子点中的Fano-Andreev效应已被广泛研究，但非线性屏蔽（非平衡电荷再分布导致的能级重整化）对Andreev输运与热电输运的影响尚缺乏系统理论刻画，尤其缺乏对偏压不对称性和交叉热电响应符号来源的机理分析。

Method: 采用特征势（characteristic-potential）形式化方法处理非线性屏蔽：以内部静电势描述与电极耦合的中心量子点上非平衡电荷再分布；侧耦合量子点引入Fano-Andreev效应；分别在纯电压偏置、纯温度偏置以及二者同时施加的条件下计算弱非线性Andreev微分电导、热电压与非线性Seebeck系数及亚能隙交叉热电响应。

Result: 电压偏置下，屏蔽诱导的能级重整化产生显著的Andreev微分电导偏压不对称性；纯热偏置下，增强点间耦合会削弱能级重整化并抑制准粒子热电响应，使热电压与非线性Seebeck系数降低；电压与温度偏置同时存在时，线性区之外出现有限的亚能隙交叉热电响应，其符号由能量依赖的输运贡献（受Fano-Andreev效应修正）与非线性屏蔽贡献之间的竞争决定，从而决定热偏置是增强还是抑制低偏压Andreev电流。

Conclusion: Fano-Andreev效应与非线性屏蔽的协同作用是决定该类超导-量子点混合结构非线性电、热电输运性质的关键，通过调节点间耦合与偏置条件可有效调控Andreev电导的不对称性及交叉热电响应的方向与大小。

Abstract: We present a theoretical study of weakly nonlinear electric and thermoelectric transport through a T-shaped double quantum dot coupled to normal and superconducting leads. Nonlinear screening is treated within the characteristic-potential formalism, in which an internal electrostatic potential accounts for nonequilibrium charge redistribution on the lead-coupled central dot. The side-coupled dot induces the Fano-Andreev effect, which, together with nonlinear screening, significantly modifies the transport properties. Under voltage bias, the screening-induced level renormalization produces a pronounced bias asymmetry in the Andreev differential conductance. In response to a pure thermal bias, increasing the interdot coupling weakens the level renormalization and suppresses the quasiparticle thermoelectric response, leading to reduced thermovoltage and nonlinear Seebeck coefficient. When voltage and thermal biases are applied simultaneously, a finite subgap cross thermoelectric response emerges beyond the linear regime. The sign of this response is governed by the competition between the energy-dependent transport contribution, modified by the Fano-Andreev effect, and the nonlinear screening contribution, thereby determining whether a thermal bias enhances or suppresses the low-bias Andreev current.

</details>


### [5] [Momentum-Space Mismatch Enables Non-Hermitian Valley Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15036)
*Kai Shao*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出"动量空间失配"作为实现节点选择性非厄米趋肤效应(NHSE)的普适机制，从而获得近100%的能谷极化输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有能谷滤波器多针对特定材料或晶格几何设计，缺乏通用性，作者希望找到一种不依赖于具体材料/晶格的共同微观要素来实现能谷选择输运。

Method: 将体系的带接触节点(狄拉克/能谷节点)与耗散库耦合，利用二者在动量空间的失配，将其编码进推迟自能，产生节点依赖的耗散与对比鲜明的准粒子寿命；由此构造的有效非厄米哈密顿量在两个节点处具有相反的点隙谱绕数，产生节点分辨的NHSE。在最小一维二聚链以及含真实狄拉克能谷的二维体系(π-通量矩形与蜂窝晶格)中验证。

Result: 各体系输运计算均给出接近100%的能谷极化，且可栅压调控，并对无序和边缘缺陷具有鲁棒性；两节点呈现强烈不对称的耗散率(一个节点快速衰减，另一个可存活于器件中)，输出近乎谷极化的电流。

Conclusion: 动量空间失配是一种实现非厄米能谷滤波的通用途径，将NHSE与能谷自由度结合，提供了材料无关的能谷选择性输运方案。

Abstract: Valley-selective transport is central to valleytronics, but existing valley filters are largely tailored to specific materials or lattice geometries, motivating the search for a common microscopic ingredient. Here we identify the momentum-space mismatch between the band-touching nodes of a system and its dissipative reservoir as a route to node-selective non-Hermitian skin effects (NHSE). When the two are coupled, the mismatch is encoded in the retarded self-energy, producing node-dependent dissipation and contrasting quasiparticle lifetimes. The resulting effective non-Hermitian Hamiltonian exhibits opposite point-gap spectral winding at the two nodes, giving rise to a node-resolved NHSE with strongly asymmetric dissipation rates: one node decays rapidly into the reservoir while the other survives over the device, yielding a nearly valley-polarized outgoing current. We demonstrate this mechanism in a minimal one-dimensional dimer chain and in two-dimensional systems hosting genuine Dirac valleys, including $π$-flux rectangular and honeycomb lattices. In all cases, transport calculations yield nearly 100\% valley polarization, while remaining gate-tunable and robust against disorder and edge imperfections. Our results show that momentum-space mismatch provides a route to non-Hermitian valley filtering.

</details>


### [6] [Magnon Chirality Selection at Equal Linewidths in Open Collinear Magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15113)
*Dinghui Wang,Lulu Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 耗散本身可在等线宽条件下选择磁振子手性：通过在共线模式网络中设计空间关联的损耗与相干交换，使相反角动量获得不同存活概率，未极化混合态可被耗散"纯化"出26.9%极化（剩留29.9%布居），等谱厄米对照为零；结合自旋与晶体旋转可翻转信号并交换强损耗下的奇异等值线，并用非厄米共线自旋-空间小群对极点配对分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常认为手性选择依赖共振线宽差（圆二色性/非互易吸收），但作者质疑：当所有共振线宽相同时，损耗是否仍能选择手性？动机是证明纯耗散（无增益、被动系统）即可在手性上产生非对称，并为"极点约束"与"制备、本征矢、物理库约束"的可观测量区分提供框架。

Method: 构造被动（无增益）共线模式网络，令空间关联的损耗与相干交换对相反角动量赋予不同的存活概率；以等谱的厄米（normal）系统作对照；用自旋旋转加晶体旋转考察信号翻转与奇异等值线（exceptional contours）的交换；借助非厄米共线自旋-空间小群在"手性×复频率"空间上的作用，对允许的极点配对进行系统分类；并给出第二种实现，在总绕数为零的情况下于相对的两个对角边界分离共轭手性扇区。

Result: 初始未极化混合态经该网络达到26.9%极化，同时保留29.9%的布居；等谱厄米对照无任何极化。自旋与晶体联合旋转可反转信号，并在更强损耗处交换所达到的奇异等值线。第二种实现即使在总绕数为零时，仍在相反的对角边界分离共轭手性扇区。

Conclusion: 损耗在无任何线宽差异时即可作为手性选择的资源。两类读出量可区分关于极点的约束与关于制备、本征矢及物理库的约束，从而为耗散驱动的手性工程提供可判别的实验/理论判据。

Abstract: Loss can select one magnon chirality even when all resonances have the same linewidth. We construct a passive collinear mode network in which spatially correlated loss and coherent exchange give opposite angular momenta different survival probabilities. An initially unpolarized mixture reaches 26.9\% polarization with 29.9\% of the population remaining; an isospectral normal control gives none. A combined spin and crystal rotation reverses the signal and exchanges the exceptional contours reached at stronger loss. We organize the allowed pole pairings by the action of a non-Hermitian collinear spin-space little group on chirality and complex frequency. A second realization separates conjugate chirality sectors at opposite diagonal edges despite zero total winding. The two readouts distinguish constraints on poles from constraints on preparations, eigenvectors, and physical reservoirs.

</details>


### [7] [Magnetically Compensated Spin-Polarized Edge States in Coplanar Magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15134)
*Dinghui Wang,Lulu Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出共面交换磁体中边界释放自旋极化态的对称性判据：允许的偶宇称边缘自旋空间等于允许的均匀磁化空间，从而使对称性保护的磁补偿必然选择垂直于磁矩平面的奇自旋织构，并给出六类极大扇区、材料实例及互补的电学自旋响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁补偿（自旋简并）层通常被认为不表现自旋极化电子态，但边界可能打破这一限制。作者希望从对称性角度建立"磁补偿—边界自旋织构—电学读出"的统一框架，明确何种边界能释放自旋极化态及其自旋取向规律。

Method: 以共面交换磁体为对象，通过对称性分析推导边界自旋空间的判据（偶宇称边缘自旋空间=均匀磁化空间），并据此对可能的边缘自旋织构进行分类，得到六个极大扇区；再用同一交换模型的互相垂直切割分别实现奇、偶扇区，并以FeSe自旋涡旋与NiI₂周期四螺旋的Wannier模型作为材料实现，计算半无限边界的电学响应。

Result: 发现对称性保护的补偿会强制产生垂直于磁矩平面的奇自旋织构；六个极大扇区完整刻画可能的边缘自旋织构；正交切割可实现奇、偶两类扇区；FeSe涡旋与NiI₂周期四螺旋模型给出材料实例。两类边界呈现互补电学响应：奇边界为垂直自旋累积，偶边界为面内自旋流响应。

Conclusion: 边界对称性把磁补偿与平衡态自旋织构及电学自旋读出联系起来，为在补偿磁体中实现边界诱导的自旋极化和自旋-电荷转换提供了对称性设计原则。

Abstract: A boundary can release spin-polarized electronic states from a magnetically compensated, spin-degenerate layer. We establish the symmetry criterion governing this release in coplanar exchange magnets: the allowed even-parity edge-spin space equals the allowed uniform-magnetization space. Symmetry-protected compensation therefore selects an odd spin texture perpendicular to the moment plane. Six maximal sectors organize the possible edge textures. Orthogonal cuts of one exchange model realize odd and even sectors, while FeSe spin-vortex and NiI$_2$ period-four spiral Wannier models provide material realizations. Their semi-infinite boundaries exhibit complementary electrical responses: perpendicular spin accumulation at the odd edge and in-plane spin-current response at the even edge. Boundary symmetry thus connects magnetic compensation to equilibrium spin textures and electrical spin readout.

</details>


### [8] [Quantum-Metric-Induced Distinct Optoelectronic Responses in Triple $M$ Valleys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15145)
*Yongliang Song,Shuxian Sun,Zhichao Zhou,Xiao Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出利用量子度规诱导的光电响应区分三重M谷，在反铁磁MoI3双层中实现非线性谷霍尔效应与光激发谷极化霍尔输运，将谷电子学从二元K±谷拓展到三元M谷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有谷电子学主要聚焦于光电性质对比鲜明的K±谷，而M谷缺乏可靠调控手段，导致大量M谷材料长期未被开发利用。作者希望为M谷提供可操纵的自由度，扩展谷电子学的状态维度。

Method: 采用紧束缚模型计算，通过调节电场取向选择任意一对M谷产生的反向非线性霍尔电流，实现非线性谷霍尔效应；利用线偏振光实现单个M谷的优先激发，产生光激发谷极化霍尔输运；结合第一性原理计算在反铁磁MoI3双层中验证量子度规诱导的光电响应。

Result: 三种M谷可通过量子度规诱导的光电响应加以区分；电场取向调控可实现任意M谷对的非线性谷霍尔效应；线偏振光可实现单谷选择性激发；第一性原理证实反铁磁MoI3双层具有显著的非线性谷霍尔电导，具备输运测量可行性。

Conclusion: 研究将谷电子学的研究对象从传统二元K±谷推广至三元M谷，为设计具有多量子态、高可调性的先进光电器件提供了新途径。

Abstract: While $K_{\pm}$ valleys with contrasting optoelectronic properties have been the main focus of valleytronics, the lack of reliable manipulation methods for $M$ valleys leaves numerous $M$-valley materials untapped. Here, we discover that three flavors of $M$ valleys become distinguishable via quantum-metric-induced optoelectronic responses. The tight-binding calculations demonstrate that by tuning the electric-field orientation, opposite nonlinear Hall currents from any pair of $M$ valleys can be chosen to realize the nonlinear valley Hall effect. Moreover, linearly polarized light allows for preferential excitation of individual $M$ valleys, driving photoexcited valley-polarized Hall transport. Near a chiral region, these forces deflect the skyrmion transversely in a direction that reverses between enantiomers, which provides a direct signature of the chiral interaction. This dual-sensor architecture combines molecular recognition, high analytical sensitivity and matrix-aware electrical signal correction, providing a potential pathway towards more reliable FET-based biomarker detection in complex biological fluids. The approach may further enable multiplexed detection of cardiac biomarkers through incorporation of target-specific aptamers, offering potential for broader assessment of cardiac stress and myocardial injury risk.

The resulting contrast converts the band-effective ferroelectric layer asymmetry into a shift of the $K$ edge, and so displaces the $K$--$Γ$ landscape: a finite-field fit that does not impose the rule leaves the $K$ coupling consistent with zero, and the shift reaches \polshiftdft~meV, the most that asymmetry can give a single valley. Reversing the polarization mirrors the valley landscape in displacement field, so the two registries have equal and opposite differential transconductance $g_d$ at zero field. In the ideal symmetric limit a second parity holds about each registry's own compensation field and places a zero of $g_d$ there. The valley-dependent screening calculated here displaces that zero by only \gdnulllow\% at the read point, so device asymmetry limits the accuracy. A flat band becomes singular when its eigenvectors hold immovable discontinuities at band crossings. Such singularity in non-Hermitian systems is sensitive to boundary conditions and unprotected by band flatness. We present a geometric approach to construct this boundary skin mode from a macroscopic sum of compact localized skin modes and trace its topological origin to two linearly independent noncontractible loop states on a non-Bloch torus. Breaking exact flatness, we further realize nearly flat non-Bloch Chern bands.

Furthermore, we derive an analytical criterion for the existence of hydrodynamic transport, showing that a finite hydrodynamic window can be supported only when the effective thermal diffusivity exceeds the phononic momentum diffusivity. Abstract: We report the growth and transport properties of GaAs1-xSbx/InAs core-shell nanowires. Accordingly, both the nanowire crystal quality and device performance improved significantly. Increasing x from 0.15 to 0.35, the density of crystalline defects, such as stacking faults in the InAs shell, decreases by half. Moreover, the field-effect mobility increases from 3850 cm2/Vs to 4800 cm2/Vs, consistent with the reduction of the lattice mismatch. The incorporation of Sb makes the nanowire easier to pinch off via the gate voltage, enhancing the electric field tunability over the nanowire. Magneto-conductance measurements reveal Aharonov-Bohm-like oscillations, suggesting a phase-coherent tubular InAs shell. Moreover, the observation of quasi-ballistic transport, quantum dots, and Kondo correlations demonstrates the coherent quantum transport capability of the MBE-grown InSb nanowires. We then review the linear dynamics of inertial spin waves, highlighting the coexistence of GHz precessional and THz nutational branches, the inertial redshift of precessional modes, spectral linewidths, and the effects of exchange, magnetostatic interactions, confinement, curvature and torsion. Finally, we address frequency- and time-domain micromagnetic simulation techniques for spatially nonuniform inertial dynamics, including geometric midpoint integrators preserving the fundamental constraints and energy properties of the continuous dynamics. Both as-cast devices exhibit clear MR responses, with a stronger absolute response observed for the transferred-electrode device. After in situ annealing, the resistance of both device types increases substantially, and no reproducible MR response is resolved, despite the enhanced structural order observed in companion films and an approximately 36-fold increase in the effective vertical conductivity extracted from electron-only devices. Instead, annealing-induced changes in interfacial contact, contact homogeneity, and local current distribution can become dominant in determining the complete-device response. These results emphasize the critical role of spinterface stability and interface engineering in achieving reliable and thermally robust OSV operation.

Abstract: Two-dimensional electron gases in terahertz cavities are among the most mature platforms for ultrastrong light--matter coupling, yet their collective response is captured by the linear Hopfield model, which has an exact classical correspondence. We show that the intrinsic band nonparabolicity of the electrons, recently revealed through nonlinear diamagnetism, turns this system into a quantum Kerr oscillator that squeezes terahertz light. Using a non-rotating-wave theory with ultrastrong-coupling-consistent input--output, an ab initio cavity bath, and a self-consistent model of electron heating, we predict output squeezing of 1.8--2.4\,dB with present GaAs samples in weakly confined cavities with quality factors of order $10^2$, and up to $\approx9$\,dB with modest improvements in mobility or cavity frequency. The spin current decomposes into an achiral part, driven by the interfacial moment, and a chiral part, driven by the Rashba spin flip. Under increasing lead spin asymmetry, the achiral part grows linearly while the chiral part follows the geometric-mean suppression, so the two sectors dissociate; they also peak at different coupling scales along the lead broadening. The coupling and bias optima are operating points of the reference configuration, not universal scaling laws. A structural zero appears at $λ_R\sin(φ/2)=t_{\mathrm{mol}}$ in the collinear-lead configuration, where the chiral signal collapses to machine precision universally across chain lengths, frequency resolutions, and twist angles; the zero originates from the rank-one singular bond matrix, whose null eigenvector traps an electron in a spin-superposition dark state that does not hop. With non-collinear ferromagnetic leads, the zero is broken in a controlled way: the residual is generically linear in the transverse polarization, arising from a single coherent spin flip at a boundary, and becomes quadratic at a tuned cancellation angle where two boundary contributions interfere destructively, with the scaling exponent jumping sharply from one to two. These results establish the spinterface model as a transport platform with a two-axis dissociation structure, non-scaling operating optima, and an exact structural zero whose non-collinear deformation is a clean linear-plus-quadratic hierarchy.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 证明 $Q$ 计数无反射态：当复能量平面下半平面存在奇数个无反射态时 $Q=-1$。在有限线中，$Q=-1$ 意味着两个马约拉纳模的分裂小于其与接触的耦合。等接触时，$Q$ 的每一次变号（包括体拓扑转变）都是一个奇异点，在此两个无反射态合并且透射最大平坦。零温下，$Q=-1$ 当且仅当接触处测得的零偏压电导超过 $e^2/h$。

Abstract: Majorana modes, zero-energy excitations of superconducting wires that are their own antiparticles, could store quantum information protected from local noise. In transport experiments, however, ordinary bound states mimic their signature, a conductance peak at zero voltage, and whether Majorana modes have been observed remains debated. A sign, $Q$, of the electron reflection at zero energy marks the topological phase of a long wire, but what $Q$ certifies in a finite device has been unclear. Here we show for the first time that $Q$ counts reflectionless states, waves that enter through one contact and leave entirely through the other: $Q=-1$ when an odd number of them lie in the lower half of the complex energy plane. In a finite wire this means that the two Majorana modes are split by less than their coupling to the contacts. With equal contacts, every sign change of $Q$, including the bulk topological transition, is an exceptional point at which two such states merge and transmission is maximally flat. At zero temperature, $Q=-1$ exactly when the zero-bias conductance measured at a contact exceeds $e^2/h$. These results show what transport can certify about a Majorana device and how to read the invariant from conductance.

Abstract: Developments in single-molecule imaging and sensing techniques have enabled direct observation of biomolecular dynamics at interfaces, which has important implications for the fields of membrane biophysics, nanofluidic sensing and transport. However, integrating active surface transport, localization and control over molecule motion remains challenging, limiting further progress in the realization of single-molecule devices. Here, we introduce an integrated surface acoustic wave (SAW)-hexagonal boron nitride (hBN) platform as a contactless actuation platform for directional transport of biomolecules over hBN surfaces. hBN provides an atomically flat surface on which DNA can be confined and still retain in-plane mobility, whereas SAW actuation enables active and guided transport without requiring specific electrical, dielectric or magnetic properties of the biomolecules. Trajectory- and jump-based analyses show that increasing the SAW power leads to higher displacement bias in the streaming direction, resulting in transport velocities along increasingly more linearized trajectories. We thus demonstrate that SAWs can induce tunable directional transport of DNA molecules confined on hBN surfaces, providing a new approach for the on-chip manipulation of biomolecules in nanofluidic systems. Our findings have important implications for the development of advanced single-molecule sequencing platforms and other technologies for biomolecular manipulation and analysis.

In this work, we extend this result to a scattering region coupled to three outgoing terminals (or channels), obtaining exact bounds for the statistics of the outgoing fermions. In particular, for two fermions and three terminals, four inequalities involving six measured probabilities determine whether a non-interacting scatterer can explain the observed statistics. Applying these inequalities to recent scattering experiments in cold atoms, we are able to identify a regime where interactions have a significant contribution on the outgoing particles. Therefore, our work opens up the possibility of identifying fermion interactions at the nanoscale in systems that range from mesoscopic conductors to optical traps in cold atoms.

Abstract: Large-scale quantum computing will require logical operations protected against high-energy impacts that disturb many superconducting qubits at once. We attribute this difference to report errors at temporal boundaries and ancilla faults propagating to data, which interact to increase the excess risk of a shallow band. We also find that the typed layout reaches the shallow-band reference risk with an estimated $49^{+2}_{-14}$ fewer protected ancillas. At distance 13, the advantage is not resolved under oracle decoding, and a five-round burst cap reverses the shallow-band ordering. These results indicate how the measurements of a logical circuit can guide protection placement within the simulated decoder and burst-duration regimes tested here.

Abstract: Lieb--Robinson bounds limit the spread of quantum information, but do not establish that a prescribed input can be prepared and transmitted with appreciable probability. Exact arithmetic certificates and a separate small-system numerical diagnostic support the quantitative analysis. The construction provides a model-specific benchmark for transport and readout in fluctuating quantum networks, linking a propagation mechanism to an observable lower bound and identifying the local error budgets required to preserve it. The resource estimates are conservative, graph-state supply is assumed, and the result does not establish background-free geometry or gravitational dynamics.

Abstract: Krylov complexity has become a central probe of operator growth, scrambling, and quantum chaos. Abstract: We investigate quantum gate teleportation as a primitive for satellite-enabled distributed quantum computing. These findings connect physical-layer modeling of satellite optical channels with gate-level performance in distributed quantum computing.

Abstract: How much memory does a quantum device need to prepare systems one at a time? Still, sequential preparation can require an unboundedly larger memory than collective one, and we investigate how this gap depends on the physical restrictions on the allowed interactions. Under noisy operations, we reveal transformations for which one auxiliary qubit suffices for preparing arbitrarily many independent outputs collectively, while no memory of fixed dimension can deliver them sequentially. Finally, for sequential protocols that repeat a fixed interaction, we show that a $d$-dimensional memory that succeeds for $d^2$ rounds will succeed forever, and that such a sequential preparation is equivalent to quantum catalysis.

Abstract: Cellular communication is done over an imperfectly predictable multimode environment, where the sender only knows about the distribution of possible intermode coupling and scattering a signal faces. At nonzero temperature, the Haar-averaged coherent information of the isotropic thermal input bounds the capacity from below, and its value per mode converges to an integral against Wachter's law. With noise, its fidelity is comparable to the original's; it needs 7 to 91 fragment variants per circuit in the simulations (7 to 61 in a run on ibm_marrakesh) and a classical reconstruction. Abstract: Quantum error-correcting codes trade decoding reliability against the resources needed to measure their checks. Against distance-multiplicity guidance, five configurations show significant gains and three do not, including one with a negative mean difference. All arms are scored in common truncated-reliability and normalized-check-weight coordinates. The framework connects reliability-check-weight trade-offs to bound-certified selection within evaluated CSS pools.

The simulator corresponds to a local hidden variable model, so we use Bell inequalities to prove the simulator can't also support Hadamard gates, CZ gates, or S gates. We note that CSS circuits appear ideal for benchmarking quantum error correction in the presence of atom loss errors.

We then prove that this obstruction can be removed locally: for fixed toric registers under sufficiently weak local-stochastic errors, enhancing only a vanishing fraction of checks near selected logical supports restores recovery while the bulk remains at finite measurement strength. With recoverability restored, we prove a further dimensional distinction in logical operations. Abstract: Where practical advantage emerges in quantum dynamics simulation remains a central debate in quantum computing. Claimed advantages can be rapidly narrowed or overturned by stronger classical methods, while progress on both sides can outpace the journal publication cycle. We present QSim-Arena, a research framework in which AI agents combine a closed loop of verification and benchmarking with open exploration. Hamiltonians, initial states and accuracy targets for evolved states or observable expectation values provide explicit specifications for evaluating simulation methods. Agents translate published techniques into executable components, reproduce reported results and benchmark their performance across diverse Hamiltonian families. Competing classical methods are implemented and independently crosschecked, providing credible baselines for end-to-end quantum resource estimates based on rigorous error bounds and empirical calibration. Alongside this closed loop, agents recombine classical simulation techniques and co-design fault-tolerant quantum circuits using knowledge extracted from the literature. Initial applications improve baseline resource estimates through the integration of established techniques. QSim-Arena thus treats quantum advantage assessment as an evolving, reproducible research process that can respond to advances in algorithms, error analysis and hardware.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

We then introduce the tangential capacity to quantify how effectively gate sets track these paths. Using this analysis, we find that conditional squeezing achieves the highest capacity, preparing GKP states in 824 ns with a fidelity of 0.99 and a minimum closeness to the path of 0.90, compared with 0.98 and 0.51 for conditional displacements and 0.83 and 0.19 for SNAP gates.

Abstract: We consider a fundamental quantum sensing task: detecting weak oscillating fields with unknown strength and frequency. Recent work by Allen et al. established a fundamental lower bound on the required sensing time, which they named the "Grover-Heisenberg limit", and gave a protocol attaining this limit. The protocol relies on auxiliary qubits, which are used to run Grover's algorithm as a subroutine, leaving open the question of whether a single sensing qubit can achieve the same scaling.

Abstract: Quantum phase estimation is a key component of many quantum algorithms. Here, we develop scalable tensor network methods (previous work was limited to ($l + m = 24$)) giving highly optimized state preparation circuits for optimally concentrated DPSS (Slepian) tapers. Our circuits are shallow enough for a demonstration on near-term devices for small values of $l$. The work floor is a two-way reduction to classical multiprocessor scheduling: every realisation \emph{induces} a $g$-machine schedule, and the construction realises any binning, so an optimal binning attains it. The standard $1/α$ subnormalisation and success probability are unchanged; the endpoints are sequential LCU ($g{=}1$) and the fully parallel realisation ($g{=}T$). Allowing the oracles to be split (McNaughton's rule) reaches the work floor $\sum_j d_j/g$. Two selector realisations trade space for depth: a dense (Möttönen) one is memory-tight at the standard LCU footprint but its multiplexed controls cost a $\log\log(m{+}1)$ factor on the makespan; a one-hot one is depth-tight at $T$ selector qubits. Abstract: This study evaluates quantum classical hybrid recurrent architectures for financial time-series forecasting: a Quantum Long Short-Term Memory (QLSTM) network and Quantum Reservoir Computing (QRC) with lag-based inputs. The QLSTM, with 2,105 parameters, is statistically indistinguishable from an LSTM 126 times larger, but equally indistinguishable from the matched classical control and from a plain LSTM of the same budget, so its parameter efficiency cannot be attributed to the quantum circuits.   <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用MILP与车辆级QUBO相结合的混合经典-量子架构：用Toshiba SQBM+以及Rigetti Ankaa-3、IQM Emerald上的QAOA求解子QUBO；在来自伦敦交通与位置数据的五个基准（10–70客户）上评估，并用强制可行性与相对经典基线单调改进的验证流水线校验。

Result: SQBM+实现经验证的车队总距离下降：25客户实例降低2.80%（7,927 m），55客户实例降低0.22%（1,186 m）；QAOA在最多4客户（16量子比特）的子问题上可稳定满足约束，构成当前硬件边界；诊断表明主要扩展瓶颈是分配层面的时间窗紧致度而非局部路径优化。

Conclusion: 混合经典-量子优化在近期具有实用价值，同时实验界定了当前量子硬件的经验极限；后续应聚焦分配阶段的时间窗处理以提升可扩展性。

Abstract: Solving large-scale Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problems with Time Windows (CVRPTW) remains challenging because strict operational constraints and scale limit both exact and heuristic methods. We present a hybrid classical-quantum architecture combining Mixed Integer Linear Programming (MILP) with vehicle-level Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO), solved using Toshiba SQBM+ and QAOA on Rigetti Ankaa-3 and IQM Emerald quantum processors. The decomposition avoids the variable growth and constraint-enforcement difficulties of monolithic CVRPTW QUBO formulations. We evaluate the framework on five realistic benchmarks derived from London traffic and location data, spanning 10--70 customers, using a validation pipeline enforcing feasibility and monotone improvement over classical baselines. SQBM+ achieves verified fleet-distance reductions of 2.80% (7,927 m) for the 25-customer instance and 0.22% (1,186 m) for the 55-customer instance. QAOA experiments establish a current hardware boundary for reliable constraint satisfaction at subproblems of up to 4 customers (16 qubits). Diagnostic results identify assignment-level time-window tightness, rather than local route optimization, as the dominant scalability bottleneck. The results demonstrate a practical near-term role for hybrid classical-quantum optimization while establishing empirical limits for current quantum hardware.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 以Caldeira-Leggett模型为出发点，构建热弛豫的量子-经典对应，比较单阱与对称双阱势中经典局域能量扩散与量子非局域能级跃迁的差别，并考察半经典极限ℏ→0。

Conclusion: 量子化并非仅修正弛豫速率，而是从根本上开启经典所不允许的反常弛豫通道。

Abstract: The Mpemba effect, in which an initially hotter system relaxes faster than a colder one, has been widely studied in both classical and quantum systems, yet identifying the specific effects of quantization requires a consistent comparison with the corresponding classical dynamics. Starting from the Caldeira-Leggett model, we establish a quantum-classical correspondence for thermal relaxation. We find that the locality of classical energy diffusion forbids the Mpemba effect in single-well and symmetric double-well potentials. Conversely, quantum dynamics allows for direct transitions between nonadjacent energy levels, overcoming the constraints of classical local energy diffusion and enabling the Mpemba effect in these potentials. In the semiclassical limit $\hbar\to0$, the nonlocal transitions become increasingly suppressed, restoring the classical constraints. Our findings reveal that quantization fundamentally unlocks anomalous relaxation pathways, rather than merely modifying relaxation rates.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子关联能否为非绝热退火带来标度优势仍是未决问题，需要一个保留QA哈密顿量结构与退火调度的经典基线来隔离量子关联的作用。

Result: 两者标度差异高度依赖模型：QA在干净低维系统中具明显优势，随维度升高而减弱，在三维及以上可忽略；无序与阻挫进一步缩小差异。

Conclusion: 明确了量子关联对退火标度产生定性影响的参数区间，并为评估其作用提供了经典基线。

Abstract: Whether quantum correlations give non-adiabatic annealing a scaling advantage remains an open question. We address it by comparing quantum annealing (QA) with quantum-inspired annealing (QIA), a classical protocol that retains the Hamiltonian structure and annealing schedule of QA while excluding nonlocal quantum correlations. Across models varying in dimensionality, disorder, and frustration, we find that the difference in scaling between the two protocols is strongly model dependent. QA shows a clear advantage in clean, low-dimensional systems, but the advantage diminishes with increasing dimensionality and becomes negligible in three or more dimensions. Disorder and frustration further narrow the difference. These results identify where quantum correlations have a qualitative effect on annealing scaling and provide a classical baseline for assessing their role.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子关联增强使经典电子结构计算代价高昂，NISQ上的混合量子-经典VQE是潜在替代方案。拟设选择对VQE性能与可扩展性至关重要，但化学启发拟设、自适应方案与硬件高效拟设（HEA）之间缺乏统一比较，导致各方法在不同分子尺度下的可扩展性与权衡不明确。

Method: 构建系统性基准研究，统一比较UCCSD、费米子ADAPT-VQE、BasicPCA HEA和激发保持（Excitation Preserving）HEA四类拟设，在H₂、LiH、N₂等不同尺寸分子上评估电路深度、参数数量、基态能量误差与NISQ可行性四项指标的权衡。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了各类VQE拟设在精度与资源效率间的关键权衡，指出亟需提升ADAPT-VQE的可扩展性与HEA的精度，并解决面向近期量子硬件的优化挑战。

Abstract: Electronic structure calculations become computationally expensive on classical computers as electron correlation increases. Quantum computing offers a promising alternative through hybrid quantum-classical Variational Quantum Eigensolver (VQE) on Noisy-Intermediate Scale Quantum (NISQ) devices. The choice of ansatz plays a pivotal role in the performance and scalability of the VQE, yet selecting an optimal ansatz remains challenging. Existing approaches including chemically inspired ansatz, adaptive schemes and Hardware Efficient Ansätze (HEA) lack a unified comparison. This gap makes the scalability and trade-offs of each method unclear for varying molecular sizes.
  This work presents a systematic benchmarking study comparing Unitary Couples Cluster Singles and Doubles Ansatz (UCCSD), fermionic ADAPT-VQE, BasicPCA HEA, and Excitation Preserving HEA across molecules of varying size like $H_2$, $LiH$, $N_2$. We evaluate trade-offs among circuit depth, parameter count, ground-state energy error, and NISQ feasibility. ADAPT-VQE achieves accuracy comparable to UCCSD with lower resource overhead, but becomes impractical for modest system sizes ($\sim$10 electrons) due to increasing circuit complexity. In contrast, HEA reduces hardware costs but requires more parameters and sacrifices accuracy, with performance strongly dependent on molecular size. The Excitation Preserving ansatz remains below chemical accuracy for $H_2$ and $LiH$, and fails for $N_2$, underscoring the need to balance expressibility and trainability. Overall, these results highlight key trade-offs between accuracy and resource efficiency across VQE ansätze, emphasizing the need to improve ADAPT-VQE scalability and HEA accuracy while addressing optimization challenges for near-term quantum hardware.

</details>

Abstract: Theoretical descriptions of the quantum electrodynamical interaction between fast electrons and nanostructures conventionally rely on standard macroscopic quantum electrodynamics (MQED), which, when applied to bounded objects in vacuum, formally requires an asymptotic limiting procedure to account for free-space radiation modes. This fundamental separation reveals an intrinsic Stokes--Anti-Stokes asymmetry, yielding distinct transverse diffraction patterns for energy loss and thermal energy gain, and establishing a generalized detailed-balance relation that governs the radiative reduction of the apparent temperature in nanoscale electron thermometry. In the illuminated regime, our framework rigorously incorporates quantum-optical states into macroscopic dissipative electrodynamics, demonstrating that...

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有理论对von Neumann熵的次可加性/强次可加性有完备刻画，但对Nichols–Wootters插值熵族（尤其是子熵subentropy）的次可加性猜想长期未决，且缺乏对其强次可加性缺陷的定量描述。本文旨在统一处理该熵族的次可加性与强次可加性缺陷，并给出可计算的上/下界与信息论解释。

Abstract: We prove subadditivity for the Nichols--Wootters family of entropies interpolating between the von Neumann entropy and the subentropy, with all spectra evaluated in one common ambient dimension. Beyond the von Neumann entropy, equality holds exactly when at least one marginal is pure. This settles the subadditivity conjecture concerning subentropy. Every member except the von Neumann entropy fails strong subadditivity, already on a maximally entangled two-qubit state tensored with a mixed qubit. Its strong-subadditivity defect takes both signs on quantum Markov states. For a pure bipartite state tensored with an ancillary state, we identify the defect with a separately concave product gap and determine its exact maximum under rank constraints. For arbitrary bipartite states, we obtain an explicit lower bound in the marginal purities. For two-qubit states, we determine the optimal coefficient multiplying the product of the smaller marginal eigenvalues in every common ambient dimension, including the subentropy coefficient $1/3$. A positive representation by Haar averages of compressed matrix entropies gives the pinching inequality underlying these results and also yields entropy-gain bounds for unital quantum channels.As an application, each entropy equals the classical communication capacity retained after a specified isotropic partial projective measurement, at a prescribed average input state, even when entangled block encodings are allowed.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate multiparameter quantum metrology and the energetic properties of an effective electron--proton spin system inspired by the hyperfine structure of the hydrogen atom. We analyze the estimation precision and the correlations between the two parameters, showing that the weak-commutativity condition is satisfied despite the noncommutativity of the associated symmetric logarithmic derivatives. We investigate the dependence of these quantities on the magnetic field, hyperfine coupling, temperature, and charging time. Our results provide a comprehensive characterization of the metrological properties and energetic performance of a hyperfine spin-based quantum system, highlighting its potential as a platform for quantum sensing and energy storage.

Abstract: Vibrational strong coupling within a few molecules has recently been demonstrated in infrared nanocavities at room temperature, opening new alternatives to explore chemical reactions assisted by the confined electromagnetic vacuum. However, the intrinsic mechanisms of how the quantized electromagnetic mode modifies reactivity are not fully understood. We study infrared photodissociation of a single molecule strongly coupled to a resonant infrared nanocavity mode. The coupled system is driven by a monochromatic infrared laser that vibrationally excites the molecule directly or injects photons into the nanocavity field. We compare the intracavity dynamics of CO2 and H2O molecules and demonstrate that the driving energy required for dissociation is up to three orders of magnitude lower inside the cavity by injecting photons into the nanocavity than by driving the molecule directly. We show that photodissociation enhancements are due to the interplay between nuclear anharmonicity, stronglight-mattercoupling, and vacuum field polarisation according to the dipolar nature of the molecular bond. Our results provide the conceptual and numerical tools for understanding how to control chemical processes and to design nanophotonics experiments at the few-molecule level within a cavity quantum electrodynamics perspective.

Abstract: Noise degrades quantum coherence and limits the precision of quantum sensors. Quantum control can counteract these effects, but existing strategies typically assume that the noise is known. In practice, noise is often only partially characterized, so its parameters must be estimated jointly with the Hamiltonian parameters of interest. Here we show that uncertainty in the noise parameters can destroy otherwise attainable Heisenberg scaling, even when coherence is preserved. For general Markovian dynamics under arbitrary quantum control, we derive a necessary and sufficient condition for each Hamiltonian parameter to retain this scaling and determine its optimal asymptotic precision when the condition holds. When the condition fails, we propose a protocol that uses a suitable noisy reference system to restore this scaling, and prove that a reference insensitive to the Hamiltonian parameters but subject to an independent copy of the sensor's noise recovers the optimal precision attainable with known noise. Our results reveal how incomplete knowledge of the noise affects the fundamental limits of quantum metrology, with broad implications for achieving quantum-enhanced precision in realistic environments.

</details>

Abstract: Quantum entanglement involving the centre-of-mass motion of macroscopic-scale objects plays an essential role in both fundamental science and emerging quantum technologies, yet generating and verifying such macroscopic nonclassical states remain formidable challenges. Here, we report a theoretical scheme to effectively generate, manipulate, and predict bipartite optomechanical entanglement based on a Fano-mirror cavity optomechanical system with a coherent feedback loop, where the cavity and Fano optical modes couple through coherent and dissipative interactions. We show that the Fano mirror forms a closed tripartite interaction loop with the cavity and mechanical modes, whereby the quantum interference between direct and indirect interaction pathways substantially enhances the cavity-mechanical entanglement and gives rise to a robust Fano-mechanical entanglement. More importantly, we find that coherent feedback combined with an appropriate optical frequency mismatch can further enhance the entanglement, and a trained supervised feedforward neural network can be used to predict the entanglement magnitude from the readily measurable optical signals, relaxing the requirement of full quantum-state tomography. Our work, establishing an all-optical and experimentally feasible approach to generate, manipulate, and characterize the macroscopic optomechanical entanglement, holds great potential for applications in quantum information processing.

</details>

Abstract: Quantum non-Gaussian (QNG) coherence is a critical resource for advanced quantum technologies with bosonic systems, operationally distinct from the coherence provided by Gaussian states. For many experimental platforms, its deterministic generation remains a formidable experimental challenge. Here we propose that pulsed Gaussian control of a linear oscillator alternating with dissipation by the smallest nonlinear element -- an unmeasured ground-state qubit coupled via resonant Jaynes-Cummings (JC) interactions -- deterministically generates high-order QNG coherence. Although such dissipative processes are phase-insensitive and do not generate local coherence alone, when added to Gaussian operations they deterministically produce high-quality binary superpositions of lower Fock states $(|0\rangle + |N\rangle)/\sqrt{2}$ up to $N=4$ with high fidelity ($\mathcal{F} > 0.98$), certified by the QNG coherence hierarchy, as well as binomial code states and logical magic states with high fidelity ($\mathcal{F} > 0.98$).

</details>

Abstract: Higher-order objectives possess many exact Hamiltonian encodings, and these encodings differ in the qubits, penalties, gates, and depth that they require. A separate construction extends this separation to gate and depth limits under a fixed synthesis. A separate routing experiment retains 52.9% of mixed-only budget points across five seeds within fixed candidate sets. In noiseless depth-one evaluation, the native encoding attains the best mean objective under the tested protocols, and the joint resource-and-quality test finds no mixed-only success at the primary budget. Resource feasibility is thus a sound reason to select a mixed encoding, whereas solution quality requires a separate assessment.

Abstract: Non-convex optimization plays an important role in modern machine learning since plenty of real-world problems have objective functions or constraints with non-convex properties. The non-convex nature of landscapes gives rise to numerous local minima and saddle points, dramatically hindering the ability of gradient descent-based methods to explore the landscape and obtain global optima. Here we present quantum tunneling search (QTS), an algorithm based on quantum tunneling walk and local annealing, which effectively explores the non-convex landscape by leveraging the quantum tunneling effect. In experiments, we map an objective function to the potential term of the waveguide Hamiltonian by controlling the refractive index with the femtosecond direct laser writing technique. With a proper input state, the average probability distributions on 10-micrometer-spaced 29 x 1 and 21 x 9 waveguide arrays highlight the local minima of various 1D and 2D non-convex landscapes, respectively. By further introducing the varying Hamiltonian approach, more efficient search and even the identification of the global minimum are achieved. Our work experimentally verifies that QTS is a successful and efficient non-convex optimization solver, demonstrating quantum speedups over classical stochastic gradient descent (SGD), which serves as a practical application of near-term analog quantum simulators in the field of machine learning.

Abstract: Engineered collective dissipation can correlate and even synchronize the motion of quantum systems. We illustrate this result for correlated thermal states and synchronized quantum van der Pol oscillators. In each case, motional correlations reduce the gate infidelity relative to independent thermal atoms at finite temperature. Our results establish atomic-motion correlations as a resource for high-fidelity neutral-atom gates and may also find applications in analog quantum simulators.

Abstract: $k$-partite XOR games and linear binary constraint system (BCS) games arise from systems of linear equations over $\mathbb{Z}_2$, providing distinct quantum relaxations of the same classical constraint satisfaction problem (CSP). We characterise perfect strategies in both the tensor-product and commuting-operator models as Kleisli morphisms of suitable state-dependent graded quantum monads on a category of $k$-partite relational structures.

We show that synchronous and $k$-partite quantum advantage are incomparable at the level of CSP instances. The GHZ instance admits three-player quantum advantage but no synchronous commuting-operator advantage, whereas the magic-square instance admits synchronous quantum advantage but no $k$-partite commuting-operator advantage for any number of players or proper colouring. Abstract: Bell inequality violations, or Bell nonlocality for short, is not only central to testing the foundations of quantum physics but have also become an essential ingredient of numerous quantum information protocols, from quantum key distribution to the device-independent certification of randomness and secure communication. Existing analyses of Bell nonlocality assume that a small set of predefined measurement directions can be reproduced exactly. But what if these directions cannot be reproduced at all? This may happen for fundamental or technical reasons, such as variations in the measurement apparatus. Here we show that the violation of a Bell inequality remains possible and that it also retains its operational significance, even when the measurement directions never repeat. We experimentally confirm these results using polarization-entangled photons, detecting only one particle pair per randomly chosen analyzer direction. Our results establish the robustness of Bell inequality violations against imperfect measurement repeatability and further connect Bell tests with the broader framework of randomized measurements. Moreover, they extend quantum information protocols to settings with limited measurement control, including quantum device certification, communication complexity, and quantum cryptography.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: In nanolasers, a few to a few hundred quantum emitters share a single mode of light. Coupled identically to the mode, the emitters do not radiate independently but form collective bright and dark states, producing superradiant bursts and strongly bunched photon statistics below the laser threshold. We investigate to what extent this behavior can be understood as random jumps between collective Dicke states. From the laser master equation, we derive a hierarchy of models defined by the polarization order, i.e., the number of photons involved in the coherence between the emitters and the light, and include the coherence to various orders. With all of the coherences eliminated, we obtain a Dicke Markov chain, a stochastic jump process over the photon number and the Dicke states, in which collective effects enter only through enhanced or suppressed transition rates. It predicts superthermal bunching and a strong even--odd dependence on the emitter number. It recovers about 80\% of the bunching in the most coherent regime, where conventional rate equations predict no superthermal bunching. Keeping the first polarization order, i.e., the optical polarization, and eliminating the second order, i.e., the two-photon coherences, yields a population-polarization model, which recovers the bunching of the full master equation almost perfectly. This hierarchy of polarization orders is an alternative to the cumulant expansion and, unlike it, recovers the master equation at the highest order. As a numerically exact reference, a symmetry-reduced master equation extends the simulation range all the way to 34 (24) emitters below (above) threshold. The Dicke Markov chain can be solved directly for hundreds of emitters and sampled for thousands of emitters, further extending the range of numerical methods. We compare the numerical performance of the methods in detail to guide the choice of technique.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高斯调制CV-QKD需在两个光场正交分量上编码，对态制备、调制与标定要求高并可能引入额外制备缺陷；一维高斯调制仅在一个正交分量编码、实现更简单，共轭分量约束安全性，但集成化的1D实验（独立激光相干接收+实用协调+有限码长参数估计+隐私放大）仍具挑战。

Method: 采用本地本振（LLO）架构与独立激光器，构建1D高斯调制CV-QKD系统，在12 km标准单模光纤上传输；系统包含相干探测、数字信号处理、信息协调、参数估计、基于物理性约束的安全性分析以及隐私放大，形成集成化的离线经典后处理链。

Result: 1D系统在渐近安全处理下获得1.421 Mbit/s的隐私放大速率，在有限码长情形下为0.364 Mbit/s。

Conclusion: 结果为一维高斯调制的性能—复杂度权衡提供了实验基线，并为开发更低复杂度的CV-QKD发射端奠定了实验基础。

Abstract: Continuous-variable quantum key distribution (CV-QKD) is a promising technology for quantum-secure communications, offering high secret-key rates and straightforward integration with coherent optical communication systems. Conventional Gaussian-modulated CV-QKD encodes information in both optical-field quadratures, increasing requirements for state preparation, modulation, and calibration, while potentially introducing additional preparation imperfections. Unidimensional (1D) Gaussian modulation offers a simpler alternative by encoding information in only one quadrature, while the conjugate quadrature constrains the protocol's security. However, an integrated experimental 1D implementation combining independent-laser coherent reception with practical reconciliation, finite-size parameter estimation, and privacy amplification remains challenging. Here, we experimentally demonstrate a 1D Gaussian-modulated CV-QKD system over 12~km of standard single-mode fiber using a local local oscillator (LLO) architecture with independent lasers and an integrated offline classical post-processing chain. The implementation includes coherent detection, digital signal processing, information reconciliation, parameter estimation, physicality-constrained security analysis, and privacy amplification. The 1D system achieves a privacy-amplified rate of $1.421$~Mbit/s under the asymptotic security treatment and $0.364$~Mbit/s under the finite-size scenario. These results establish an experimental baseline for the performance--complexity trade-off associated with unidimensional Gaussian modulation and provide an experimental basis for developing lower-complexity CV-QKD transmitters.

</details>

*Shantom K. Borah,Abhinav Vaishya,V. Lalitha,Asit Kumar Pradhan,Bane Vasic,Narayanan Rengaswamy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出循环群代数上的 qudit 提升乘积（LP）构造，得到对任意素数幂维度、任意堆叠阶数的准循环 qudit CSS 码，并证明完全堆叠情形下具有 Θ(log N) 逻辑 qudit 与近线性距离 Θ(N/log N)，同时给出加性译码器与线性合并伴随式译码器，模拟显示 qudit 码帧错误率低于比特码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QLDPC 码是实现低开销容错量子计算的有前景途径，qudit 在信息表示效率、魔术态蒸馏和代数结构丰富性上优于 qubit。渐近优的 QLDPC 码依赖非阿贝尔提升，但循环提升产生的准循环码具有循环结构，便于硬件译码器（尤其非二元译码器校验节点运算昂贵）高效实现，因此需要在保持循环提升实用性的同时推广到 qudit。

Method: 在循环群代数上给出 LP 构造的 qudit 推广，将超图乘积与 LP 码扩展到任意素数幂维度，并对每个堆叠阶数生成准循环 qudit CSS 码；通过与加性码的联系界定编码率；对一般 LP 因子类建立距离界；研究通用 qudit 码的加性译码器与完全堆叠码的线性合并伴随式译码器。

Result: 在完全堆叠情形下，由扩展图上提升 Tanner 码构造的 qudit LP 码族具有 Θ(log N) 个逻辑 qudit 和近线性最小距离 Θ(N/log N)。在等符号错误的去极化信道下蒙特卡洛仿真表明，qudit 码的帧错误率低于可比 qubit 码，且线性译码器在完全堆叠码上带来进一步增益。

Conclusion: cyclic 提升下的 qudit LP 码在保持准循环硬件友好结构的同时获得良好渐近参数与更优译码性能，为低开销容错量子计算提供了兼具理论保证与工程可行性的编码方案。

Abstract: Quantum low-density parity-check (QLDPC) codes are a promising route to fault-tolerant quantum computation with low overhead, and qudits offer advantages over qubits including more efficient information representation, improved magic state distillation, and richer algebraic structure for code design. Asymptotically good QLDPC codes arise from the lifted product (LP) construction with non-abelian lifts, but cyclic lifts remain attractive in practice because the resulting quasi-cyclic codes have circulant structure that hardware decoders exploit, which matters especially for non-binary decoders with costly check node operations. We introduce a qudit generalization of the LP construction over cyclic group algebras, which extends hypergraph product and LP codes to arbitrary prime-power dimensions and yields quasi-cyclic qudit CSS codes for every stacking order. We bound encoding rates via a connection to additive codes, and show that in the fully stacked case, qudit LP codes built from lifted Tanner codes on expander graphs form families with $Θ(\log N)$ logical qudits and almost linear minimum distance $Θ(N/\log N)$, via a distance bound that holds for a general class of LP factors. We also study an additive decoder for general qudit codes and a linear merged-syndrome decoder for fully stacked codes. Monte Carlo simulations under a depolarizing channel with equal symbol error probability show that qudit codes achieve lower frame error rates than comparable qubit codes, and that the linear decoder gives further gains for fully stacked codes.

Abstract: Quantum key distribution (QKD) generates shared secret keys whose security rests on the laws of physics rather than on computational assumptions. We also introduce a matrix formalism for passive wavelength routing that enables the algorithmic construction of uniquely and fully connected topologies. Unlike most previous demonstrations, which estimate key rates from raw coincidences, all rates reported here are obtained after the complete post-processing chain, with keys delivered in real time via ETSI GS QKD 014. Result: 得到若干混态不确定关系，分别约束协方差矩阵的对角积、迹与各对角元；导出由平均Uhlmann曲率给出的量子Fisher信息矩阵几何界，纯态时退化为Berry曲率对量子度规的界；揭示速度方差与价带Wannier函数展宽的光学求和规则受Berry曲率及速度算符对易子限定；并在自旋正则系综、拓扑材料、常见半导体与过渡金属硫族化合物上验证了这些几何界与不确定关系。

Conclusion: 量子不确定关系与量子几何界在同一框架下统一：Cramér-Rao界提供了协方差矩阵层面的约束，SLD选择使之对应Uhlmann/Berry曲率对Fisher信息与量子度规的限制，并可直接用于解释和检验真实材料的求和规则与几何性质。

Abstract: By utilizing the derivation of quantum Cramér-Rao bound and an argument put forward by Efimov and Dodonov, we derive several uncertainty relations for mixed states that constrain the diagonal product, trace, and each diagonal element of the covariance matrix of multiple observables. By choosing the observables to be the symmetric logarithmic derivative, the uncertainty relations imply various geometric bounds on quantum Fisher information matrix caused by mean Uhlmann curvature, which in pure states become geometric bounds on quantum metric caused by Berry curvature. Our formalism reveals optical sum rules for velocity variance and spread of valence band Wannier functions, and how they are bounded by the Berry curvature and commutator of velocity operators. Canonical ensembles of spins, topological materials, common semiconductors, and transition metal dichalcogenides are used to verify these geometric bounds and uncertainty relations.

</details>


### [93] [Communication inequalities: robust quantum advantage, measurement testing and dimension witnessing with minimal setups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15995)
*Albert Rico,Javier Fernández,Mani Zartab,John Calsamiglia,Some Sankar Bhattacharya*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文导出仅依赖输入-输出统计中四个相关与反相关事件参数的维度不等式，用于区分通信场景中的经典与量子统计，并借此认证SIC-POVM及构造维度见证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通信场景（CS）可视为观察量子行为的最简设置，但与Bell场景或含共享随机的制备-测量场景不同，此前尚缺乏区分经典与量子统计的通用方法，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 推导依赖相关与反相关事件的四参数维度不等式；在维度假设下分析不等式取等条件，并利用其构造量子统计的判定与认证方案。

Result: 不等式在维度假设下取等时可认证具有解析噪声鲁棒性的SIC-POVM；同时该不等式给出对相关与反相关统计均适用的维度见证，且无需测量不相容性。

Conclusion: 维度不等式为通信场景中经典与量子统计的区分提供了通用判据，并统一了SIC-POVM认证与维度见证，放宽了对测量不相容性的依赖。

Abstract: Communication scenarios (CS) are arguably the simplest setting in which quantum behaviors can be observed. Yet, in contrast to Bell scenarios or prepare-measure scenarios with shared randomness, no general method is known to separate classical and quantum statistics. Here we close this gap by deriving dimensional inequalities to separate classical and quantum statistics in CS. These depend only on four parameters of the input-output statistics, corresponding to correlated and anticorrelated events. Under dimensional assumptions, saturating one of the inequalities certifies SIC-POVMs with analytical noise robustness. The inequalities also yield a dimension witness for both correlated and anti-correlated statistics, without the need of measurement incompatibility.

</details>


### [94] [Quantum Material Generation via Coherent Hamiltonian Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16016)
*Jiang Yuhan,Matthew Otten*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出量子材料生成算法 QMage，在单一相干量子线路内以哈密顿量为引导，搜索同时满足原子组分、晶胞结构与原子位置约束且计算性质达标的晶体构型，面向容错量子计算时代给出量子比特数与 Toffoli 深度成本估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的生成模型仅学习已知材料的分布，外推能力差，且生成结构缺少稳定性保证；而材料性质本质上由哈密顿量决定，量子计算机可由哈密顿量直接计算性质，因此有必要建立以第一性原理为基础、而非依赖已知材料泛化的材料发现范式。

Method: 构建 QMage：将晶体构型（原子组分、晶胞结构、原子坐标）作为一个相干搜索空间；对每个构型的哈密顿量进行块编码（block-encoding），隔离目标本征态并评估性质，再用定点搜索（fixed-point search）放大满足目标的构型并测量。整体为单一相干量子线路，统一框架覆盖形成能、带隙、弹性与电导等多种目标。

Result: 从经典上常规可行的基准算例，到既有方法不可靠的目标区间，按逻辑量子比特数与 Toffoli 深度估算了算法成本，并表明同一框架可覆盖多类目标性质。

Conclusion: QMage 以第一性原理生成候选材料，而非从已知材料泛化，在目标性质经典难以预测的情形下有望推动材料发现，但依赖成熟的容错量子计算硬件。

Abstract: Discovering new materials with targeted properties is a central goal of materials science, yet the space of candidates is vast. Data-driven generative models learn the distribution of known materials, but they extrapolate poorly, and a generated structure is not known to be stable. A material's properties follow from its Hamiltonian, and a quantum computer can calculate them from it. We introduce the quantum material generative algorithm (QMage), a coherent search, guided by the Hamiltonian, over crystalline configurations of atomic composition, unit cell structure, and atomic positions, for one whose calculated properties meet the targets to within a given error tolerance. The computation is a single coherent quantum circuit in which each configuration's Hamiltonian is block-encoded, its target eigenstate isolated and properties evaluated, and the satisfying configurations amplified by fixed-point search and measured. One framework covers many targets, from formation energy and band gaps to elasticity and conductivity. We design the algorithm for the mature fault-tolerant era and estimate its cost in logical qubits and Toffoli depth from a classically routine benchmark to targets where established methods are unreliable. The approach generates candidate materials from first principles, rather than generalizing from those known, and could enable materials discovery when the target properties are difficult to predict classically.

</details>


### [95] [Operator uncertainty and cross-domain information in quantum signals: Second-moment bounds for quantum waveform design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16049)
*Mostafizur Rahaman Laskar,Sajad Daei,Gabor Fodor,Mikael Skoglund,Franco Nori*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 结合熵不确定关系与连续变量协方差描述，给出量子波形设计中到达时间与横向波数互信息的上界，并证明高斯波包可饱和该界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为时空耦合量子信号（量子波形）设计提供信息论层面的定量框架，明确在多大规模约束下可达多少信息，并给出可达性的充要条件。

Method: 将熵不确定关系与连续变量系统的高斯协方差描述相结合，对有限二阶矩与有限熵的态推导二阶矩（互信息）上界；以带双线性时空相位的高斯波包作为饱和态的构造；分析局域高斯测量、Rényi-2熵与量子Fisher信息；并计入加性探测噪声与时间抖动。

Result: 互信息不超过时频不确定度乘积与位形-波数不确定度乘积中较小者二倍的对数；带双线性相位的高斯波包对任意容许的标准差预算均可饱和该界，从而该界构成达成目标信息的充要条件；对该态，到达时间与横向波数的联合测量在局域高斯测量中最优，等于约化态的Rényi-2熵，并在固定输入宽度下达到耦合的量子Fisher信息；加性探测噪声下，时间抖动将记录信息限制在由抖动与脉冲宽度之比决定的上限。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了时空耦合量子信号设计的分析框架，并基于脉冲前沿倾斜与透镜提出一种光学实现方案。

Abstract: We derive second-moment bounds for quantum waveform design by combining entropic uncertainty relations with the covariance description of continuous-variable systems. For any state with finite second moments and entropies, the mutual information between the arrival time and the transverse wave number, which are compatible observables from different canonical pairs, is bounded above by the logarithm of twice the smaller of the time-frequency and position-wave-number uncertainty products. We demonstrate that a Gaussian wave packet with a bilinear space-time phase saturates this bound for every admissible set of standard-deviation budgets, and that the bound therefore provides a necessary and sufficient condition for reaching a target information within the canonical model. For this state, the joint measurement of the arrival time and the transverse wave number is optimal among local Gaussian measurements, yields the Rényi-2 entropy of the reduced state, and attains the quantum Fisher information for the coupling at fixed input widths. We also show that, with additive detector noise, timing jitter limits the recorded information to a level set by the ratio of jitter to pulse duration. These results provide a framework for designing spatiotemporally coupled quantum signals, and we propose an optical realization based on pulse-front tilt and a lens.

</details>


### [96] [TGen-SQD: Physics-Informed Transferable Configuration Generator for Sample-Based Quantum Diagonalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16080)
*Niloy Deb Roy Mishu,Lei Jiang,Mohsen Heidari,Ruizhe Zhang,Fan Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出TGen-SQD，一种跨多Hamiltonian联合训练的物理知情生成器，通过学习CI加权数据与轨道/系统级描述符生成紧凑行列式子空间，训练后权重冻结，在H10链上较PIGen-SQD基线能量误差降低约12%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SQD避免迭代式变分线路优化，但其精度与成本取决于能否选出紧凑且能刻画基态的行列式子空间；现有机器学习选择器依赖任务特定物理线索且通常需针对每个Hamiltonian重新训练，泛化性与成本均受限。

Method: TGen-SQD采用物理知情生成器，在多个Hamiltonian上联合训练；从configuration-interaction加权数据中学习，并利用轨道级与系统级描述符引导行列式生成；训练完成后权重冻结，可直接用于已见与零样本几何构型的推理。

Result: 在20量子比特H10链上，已见与未见几何构型的平均能量误差分别为40.18±1.52 mHa和43.97±1.20 mHa，在匹配采样数与提议预算下，较SOTA PIGen-SQD基线分别降低12.7%与11.7%。

Conclusion: 跨Hamiltonian联合训练且权重冻结的生成式选择器可在不重训的前提下兼顾已见与零样本构型，说明物理知情描述符与CI加权数据能有效提升SQD子空间选择效率与精度。

Abstract: Sample-based quantum diagonalization (SQD) avoids iterative variational circuit optimization, but its accuracy and cost depend on selecting a compact determinant subspace that captures the ground state. Existing machine-learning selectors use task-specific physical cues and typically retrain for each Hamiltonian. We introduce TGen-SQD, a physics-informed generator jointly trained across multiple Hamiltonians. It learns from configuration-interaction-weighted data and uses orbital- and system-level descriptors to guide generation. After training, its weights remain frozen for inference on seen and zero-shot geometries. On a 20-qubit H10 chain, TGen-SQD achieves mean energy errors of 40.18 +/- 1.52 and 43.97 +/- 1.20 mHa on seen and unseen geometries, respectively, 12.7% and 11.7% lower than the state-of-the-art PIGen-SQD baseline under matched shot and proposal budgets.

</details>


### [97] [Testing Quantum Advantage in High-Frequency Trading with Market-Derived Nonlocal Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16113)
*Daiwei Zhu,Brendan Pankovich,Alireza Marefat Khah,Haakam Aujla,Ramis Movassagh,Martin Roetteler,Johannes Borregaard*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"市场派生非局域博弈"：用历史订单簿数据与联合动作回测确定博弈的输入与收益，而非预设理想化模型；在双主体三股票跨场所套利场景中考察量子关联能否带来协调优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠缠可实现通信受限下的分布式协调（如碎片化市场中的高频交易），但既有研究多停留在理论设定博弈与收益结构的玩具模型，未检验模型对真实市场行为的拟合度。

Method: 以市场因素驱动的经典交易策略为起点，量子关联仅用于协调主体决策；计算精确经典最优解与量子收益上界；对存在量子优势的博弈，提出优化算法搜索显式纠缠策略，并在历史数据上做端到端回测。以双主体、三股票跨场所套利为实验场景。

Result: 由实测市场数据导出的博弈在实测交互尺度下未呈现可分辨的量子—经典差距；通过可控地调整流动性竞争与跨股票重定价，可识别出支持量子优势的机制区间，构造显式策略，并检验其优势能否在修改后的历史数据端到端回测中存活。

Conclusion: 将博弈构造锚定于真实订单簿数据与端到端回测，为评估量子协调优势提供了可检验框架；实证显示优势存在条件性，取决于流动性竞争与跨股票重定价等机制。

Abstract: Non-classical correlations enabled by entanglement could be exploited for distributed coordination tasks with communication constraints such as high-frequency trading in fragmented markets. However, previous studies have largely specified the game and its payoff structure theoretically, using toy market models without testing how well these models reflect observed market behavior. Here, we introduce market-derived nonlocal games, in which historical order-book data and backtests of joint actions determine the game's inputs and payoffs, rather than a prespecified idealized model. We start from classical trading strategies based on market considerations, using quantum correlations only to coordinate the agents' decisions. We compute exact classical optima and quantum upper bounds for the payoff. For games with a possible quantum advantage, we introduce an optimization algorithm to find explicit entangled strategies and end-to-end backtest them on historical data. We demonstrate the approach in a two-agent, three-stock cross-venue arbitrage setting. The game derived from the observed market data shows no resolved quantum-classical gap at the measured interaction scales. Controlled variations in liquidity competition and cross-stock repricing then show how the approach identifies regimes supporting quantum advantage, constructs explicit strategies, and tests whether their advantage survives end-to-end backtesting on modified historical market data.

</details>


### [98] [Phase Interference and Transverse Catalysts Govern the Noise Fragility of Long-Range Variational Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16115)
*Longhui Shen,Fangzhou Jin,Xiaoxia Yi,Bin Guo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 变分电路的单故障脆弱性 F 由故障相位在目标哈密顿量上的干涉方式决定，而非故障扩散距离；通过抑制横向"催化剂"可消除被点燃的脆弱性平台，实现最小脆弱性并减半退相位误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程纠缠门可缩短变分态制备，但同时加速算符在寄存器上的扩散，通常被认为使电路对噪声更脆弱。作者试图厘清非局域性、故障扩散与噪声脆弱性之间的真实因果关系，指出脆弱性来源被误认为与扩散范围相关。

Method: 以海森堡链为模型，比较具有相同广延支撑的两类双故障造成的能量损伤，证明其比值精确为 N/2；分析故障相位在目标哈密顿量上的相干干涉结构（相位匹配的"纵向光晕"）。进一步考察打破远端寄存器对称性的横向催化剂（空间非均匀单比特旋转、双比特 XX/YY 门的对翻转分量），并在含全宽长程 ZZ 门的噪声训练中分别/同时抑制两类催化剂。

Result: 相位匹配的纵向光晕在能量上"暗"（无损伤），直至被横向催化剂点燃；均匀旋转与守恒磁化交换门使其严格保持暗态。噪声训练中，单独抑制任一催化剂，电路仍被远程 XY 耦合维持在点燃平台上；同时抑制两者则消除平台，尽管故障算符更大仍达最小脆弱性，退相位误差减半。训练过程受"催化剂修剪与梯度力平衡"之间的竞争驱动：退火去除横向催化剂可挽救所有轨迹，而退火整个纵向扇区则不能。结合门计数暴露 λ=pK，单次脆弱性测量即可准确预测跨架构与噪声强度下的噪声诱导能量偏移。

Conclusion: 变分电路的噪声脆弱性由故障相位的干涉结构而非非局域扩散决定；ansatz 设计可归结为相干表达性与门计数之间的权衡，并可用 λ=pK 与单次脆弱性测量进行定量预测。

Abstract: Long-range entangling gates shorten variational state preparation, but they also accelerate operator spreading across the register, linking nonlocality to a presumed noise fragility. Here we show that the single-fault fragility $F$ of a variational circuit is governed by how fault phases interfere on the target Hamiltonian, not by how far faults spread. For Heisenberg chains we prove that two faults with identical extensive support can incur either bounded or extensive energy damage, in the exact ratio $N/2$. A phase-matched longitudinal halo remains energetically dark until ignited by transverse catalysts that break a symmetry of the remote register, namely spatially nonuniform single-qubit rotations or the pair-flip component of two-qubit $XX/YY$ gates, whereas uniform rotations and magnetization-conserving exchange gates leave it strictly dark. In noisy training with full-width long-range $ZZ$ gates, suppressing either catalyst alone leaves circuits trapped on an ignited plateau sustained by remote $XY$ couplings, whereas suppressing both eliminates the plateau, achieves minimal fragility despite larger fault operators, and halves the dephasing error. Training is driven by a race between catalyst pruning and gradient force balance, as annealing the transverse catalysts out of trapped circuits rescues every trajectory while annealing the entire longitudinal sector does not. Combined with the gate-count exposure $λ=pK$, a single fragility measurement accurately predicts the noise-induced energy shift across circuit architectures and noise strengths, reducing ansatz design to a trade-off between coherent expressivity and gate count.

</details>


### [99] [Quantum Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16117)
*Matthias M. Müller,Tommaso Calarco,Felix Motzoi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本章综述量子控制：界定控制问题、梳理误差来源与抑制手段，并介绍绝热方法、量子最优控制与机器学习等先进求解技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子系统动力学脆弱，需精确导向目标态或目标变换；量子控制既是量子技术应用的基石，又能揭示能量结构与时间动力学的基本关联。

Method: 综述性论述：提出控制问题的一般框架，分析基本误差来源及其抑制方法，并对比 adiabatic methods、quantum optimal control 与 machine learning 等求解策略。

Result: 归纳出量子控制问题的统一表述，明确了误差来源与相应的抑制途径，并对当前主流控制求解技术进行了分类介绍。

Conclusion: 量子控制是量子技术的核心组成部分；绝热方法、最优控制与机器学习构成了互补的技术谱系，共同支撑对脆弱量子动力学的精确操控。

Abstract: Quantum Control is the art of steering the fragile dynamics of a quantum system in a precise way toward a desired final state or transformation. It is a cornerstone of many quantum technology applications and at the same time allows us to shed light on the fundamental connection between the energy structure and the time dynamics of quantum systems at the heart of some of the most interesting phenomena of quantum mechanics. In this chapter on quantum control, we introduce the control problem, go through basic error sources and methods to suppress these errors and then discuss state-of-the-art techniques to solve the control problem, like adiabatic methods, quantum optimal control and methods of machine learning.

</details>


### [100] [Infinite or indefinite: the query cost of simulating the quantum switch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16229)
*Dantong Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明在电路模型中用量子开关的黑盒操作进行模拟需要无限次调用（最坏情形精确模拟），近似误差<1/2 需 Ω(d^{1/4}) 次调用，所给比特提取任务需指数次调用；但在区分去相位噪声与去极化噪声的任务中，量子开关仅需常数次器件使用，而电路模型需 Ω(d^{1/16}) 次，从而给出可证明的查询优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子开关利用因果序的相干叠加，不能被任何以确定次序调用各操作一次的量子电路所描述，但光子实验已实现其物理实现。作者想知道：在电路模型中，需要多少次对黑盒操作的调用才能复现量子开关的行为，以及这种不定因果序是否带来可证明的计算/查询优势（尤其是对量子纠错相关的噪声刻画）。

Method: 在电路模型（允许经典前馈与无限辅助量子存储）下与量子开关进行查询复杂度对比：(1) 若一个黑盒已知为酉操作，三次调用即足够；(2) 对 d 维系统上的通用信道，给出三个下界：最坏情形下无有限次调用可精确模拟；以半钻石范数度量、误差低于 1/2 的近似需 Ω(d^{1/4}) 次调用；存在普通电路需指数多次调用才能取得开关少数次即可提取的单个比特；(3) 构造噪声判别任务：实际器件为固定未知基下的去相位噪声，与理论去极化模型对比，两者纠缠保真度与平均门保真度相同，考察查询次数随维数 d 的标度。

Result: 三项主要结果：(i) 最坏情形精确模拟需要无限次调用；(ii) 近似到任意固定误差 <1/2（半钻石范数）需 Ω(d^{1/4}) 次调用，随量子比特数指数增长；(iii) 普通电路获取单个比特可能需指数多次调用，而开关只需少数几次。在噪声判别任务中，固定噪声强度下量子开关以与 d 无关的器件使用次数即可区分两类信道，而电路模型需 Ω(d^{1/16}) 次调用。

Conclusion: 量子开关（不定因果序）相对电路模型具有可证明的查询复杂度优势，且在噪声刻画这一对量子纠错至关重要的实际相关任务中体现出来；纠缠保真度与平均门保真度等常用指标无法区分该两种噪声，而开关可以。作者提出将此类优势推广到更实用的计算任务作为未来工作。

Abstract: The quantum switch applies two operations in a coherent superposition of their causal orders and is a simple example of indefinite causal order. No quantum circuit that uses each operation once in a definite order can even describe it, yet photonic experiments have demonstrated its physical realization. We ask how many calls to the black-box operations a quantum computer in the circuit model needs to reproduce the switch. If one of the black boxes is known to be unitary, three calls suffice. For general channels on a $d$-dimensional system, we prove three results. In all three, circuits may use classical feed-forward and unlimited ancillary quantum memory. First, no finite number of calls suffices for exact simulation in the worst case. Second, approximation to any fixed error below $1/2$, measured in half the diamond norm, requires $Ω(d^{1/4})$ calls, exponential in the number of qubits. Third, an ordinary circuit can require exponentially many calls to obtain a single bit that the switch extracts with a few uses. We consider a device whose actual noise is dephasing in a fixed unknown basis and test it against a theoretical depolarizing model. The two channels have the same entanglement and average gate fidelities, so these figures alone do not distinguish them. At fixed noise strength, the quantum switch tells them apart with a number of device uses independent of $d$, whereas the circuit model requires $Ω(d^{1/16})$ calls. Noise characterization is needed for quantum error correction, and this gives a provable query advantage of the switch in a task relevant to practical quantum computing. We invite future work that extends such advantages to more practical computing tasks.

</details>


### [101] [Hierarchical Decoding for Quantum Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16271)
*Xulong Tang,Narges Alavisamani,Joan Camps,Matthew Cha,Elise Jennings,Jack O'Connor,Humberto Munoz Bauza,Josh Izaac,Namit Anand,Ron Bewtra,Jeremiah Coleman,Johannes Heinsoo,Michael Ferguson,Ben Lau,P. Aaron Lott,Leonhard Neuhaus,Lee J. O'Riordan,Muhammad Osama,Masoud Mohseni,Blake R. Johnson,Yasuko Eckert*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文是一篇综述，系统梳理容错量子计算中经典解码器的算法、执行策略与码构造，以通用分类区分“解码器级层次”与“码级层次”，并评估性能权衡、硬件平台与报告缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算要求解码器跟上综合征数据流，并以受控的端到端时延向量子控制系统提供校正信息与逻辑测量结果；规模化下需兼顾高吞吐与低时延，而现有文献分类混乱、可比性不足，缺少统一框架。

Method: 采用统一分类法的文献综述：提出解码器级层次（多解码阶段/资源协同解一个码）与码级层次（码构造本身引入多级解码）的区分；先综述单码分层解码器及其级联预解码、窗口化、流式与分布式实现；再分析层次码与级联码在硬判决、软可靠度信息、综合征信息上的跨层协同；并考察量子比特模态对解码负载与时序要求的影响，以及CPU、GPU、FPGA、ASIC与低温平台等解码平台的适配性。

Result: 在已报告的评估条件下对比了各类算法、执行策略与码构造的性能与权衡；厘清文献间的关联，给出QEC设计探索的共享基础；指出性能报告规范与系统集成方面的空白。

Conclusion: 面向实用化量子纠错，需统一性能表征与系统集成标准，并在吞吐—时延—资源之间做明确的权衡设计；该综述为后续研究提供了分类框架与研究缺口指引。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computing requires classical decoders that can keep up with syndrome data streams and supply correction information and logical measurement outcomes to the quantum control system. Meeting these requirements at scale requires high throughput while controlling the end-to-end latency from readout to usable correction.
