In this paper, we organize decoding algorithms, execution strategies, and code constructions using common classifications and examine their performance and trade-offs under the reported evaluation conditions. We distinguish decoder-level hierarchy, where multiple decoding stages or resources cooperate to decode a single code, from code-level hierarchy, where the code construction itself introduces multiple decoding levels. We first review single-code hierarchical decoders and their cascaded predecoding and windowed, streaming, and distributed implementations. We then study hierarchical and concatenated codes, focusing on how decoding is coordinated across code levels through hard decisions, soft reliability information, or syndrome information. We also examine how qubit modalities shape decoding workloads and timing requirements, and review how different decoding platforms meet these requirements, including central processing units (CPUs), graphics processing units (GPUs), field-programmable gate arrays (FPGAs), application-specific integrated circuits (ASICs), and cryogenic platforms. By assessing design trade-offs, this paper clarifies connections across the literature and provides a shared foundation for QEC design exploration. It also identifies gaps in performance reporting and system integration to guide future research toward practical quantum error correction.

</details>


### [102] [Scaling Pêlagos: Multi-GPU Orchestration for Relay Belief-Propagation Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16285)
*Xulong Tang,Dionysios Diamantopoulos,Tristan Müller,Apo Kayi,Nelson Mimura Gonzalez,Muhammad Osama,Seetharami Seelam,Haralampos Pozidis,Blake R. Johnson,Yasuko Eckert*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作将Pêlagos Relay-BP量子纠错解码器扩展为多GPU架构（进程隔离编排、并行主机预处理、共享内存输出），在单gross与双gross码上实现约6–7倍近线性端到端加速，并部署于Oak Ridge的MI355X超算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算需要低逻辑错误率且满足实时约束的QEC解码器，启发式解码器的性能评估依赖大规模仿真；多GPU扩展是自然途径，但朴素地分发综合症批次(syndrome batches)会引入主机端预处理与输出收集开销，从而限制扩展性。

Method: 在Pêlagos Relay-BP解码器基础上引入三项优化：进程隔离的多GPU编排、并行主机端预处理、共享内存输出收集；在单gross [[144,12,12]]与双gross [[288,12,18]]电路上于多GPU环境评测（8×AMD MI300X与8×MI355X）。

Result: 单gross电路R=50下，8卡MI300X与MI355X的端到端加速分别为5.68×与6.23×；双gross电路分别为6.92×与7.01×；按GPU解码时间计吞吐，双gross加速达7.58×与7.45×，呈近线性GPU扩展。

Conclusion: 通过高效多GPU解码可显著加速大规模QEC仿真，并在Lux（OLCF的MI355X超算）上作为高通量QEC仿真的探路应用部署，为AI+HPC基础设施提供量子计算用例。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computing requires quantum error-correction (QEC) decoders that achieve low logical error rates under real-time operating constraints. These constraints motivate heuristic decoding algorithms whose error-correction performance must be evaluated through large-scale simulation. High-throughput decoding accelerates this evaluation and the exploration of decoder parameters. Using multiple graphics processing units (GPUs) is a natural way to scale these simulations, but naively distributing syndrome batches introduces host-side preprocessing and output-collection overheads that limit scaling. To address these limitations, we extend the Pêlagos relay belief-propagation (Relay-BP) decoder with process-isolated orchestration, parallel host preprocessing, and shared-memory output. We evaluate both one-gross ($[[144,12,12]]$) and two-gross ($[[288,12,18]]$) codes in a multi-GPU environment. Specifically, for the one-gross circuit at $R=50$, eight AMD Instinct MI300X GPUs and eight AMD Instinct MI355X GPUs achieve optimized end-to-end speedups of $5.68\times$ and $6.23\times$, respectively. For the two-gross circuit, the corresponding speedups are $6.92\times$ and $7.01\times$. When throughput is calculated using GPU decoding time, the corresponding two-gross speedups are $7.58\times$ and $7.45\times$, indicating near-linear GPU scaling. We deploy Pêlagos on Lux, Oak Ridge National Laboratory's AMD Instinct MI355X-based supercomputer, as a pathfinding application for high-throughput QEC simulation. This deployment provides a concrete quantum-computing use case for the AI + HPC infrastructure supporting the Genesis Mission's scientific-discovery goals. These end-to-end measurements demonstrate a practical path to accelerating large-scale QEC simulation through efficient multi-GPU decoding.

</details>


### [103] [Collective information backflow from a local population record: Identifiability and learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16334)
*Yifan Shi,Pengju Chen,Duan Wang,Quanzhen Ding,Ting Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究复合量子系统中信息回流（information backflow）的诊断与实验可获取的稀疏布居记录之间的不匹配问题，并给出在已知浴族内可用布居预测诊断、但在有限测量统计下无法统一准确推断的界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息回流通常由演化态对（如迹距离）定义，而实验往往只能获得有限的布居记录；作者希望厘清这种定义与可观测数据之间的差距，判定稀疏布居数据在何种条件下足以作为回流诊断的替代。

Method: 考虑两个相同量子比特集体耦合到具有指数关联函数的真空浴；用理想亮态布居轨迹确定与有限时间迹距离回流诊断相关的浴参数组合，诊断在固定时间网格与态对库上评估；采用支持向量学习或直接物理拟合由稀疏布居记录预测该诊断，并用双假设界分析有限测量统计的限制。

Result: 未分辨的失谐符号不改变该诊断；稀疏布居记录可借助支持向量学习或直接物理拟合预测诊断。有限统计下，双假设界可识别短布居记录相似但回流显著不同的浴；对一个含600总射击数的六时刻协议，在指定两个浴之一处，任何基于这些观测的估计量的期望绝对误差至少约为0.13。

Conclusion: 在已知浴族内，布居数据对回流诊断可以是充分的；但短而含噪的记录不允许对回流做统一精确的推断，二者需区分对待。

Abstract: Information backflow in a composite quantum system is defined through pairs of evolving states, whereas an experiment may provide only a restricted population record. We study this mismatch for two identical qubits coupled collectively to a vacuum reservoir with an exponential correlation function. An ideal bright-state population trajectory determines the bath-parameter combinations relevant to a finite-time trace-distance backflow diagnostic of the full two-qubit channel, evaluated on a fixed time grid and state-pair bank. The unresolved detuning sign leaves this diagnostic unchanged. This relation allows sparse population records to predict the diagnostic using support-vector learning or direct physical fitting. Finite measurement statistics impose a different restriction: a two-hypothesis bound identifies reservoirs with similar short population records but substantially different backflow. For one six-time protocol with 600 total shots, every estimator using those observations has expected absolute error at least approximately 0.13 at one of two specified reservoirs. Thus a population can be sufficient within a known bath family without permitting uniformly accurate inference from a short noisy record.

</details>


### [104] [Training quantum neural networks one job at a time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16365)
*Muhammad Faryad,Rabab Alomairy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将量子-经典混合训练视为"执行效率"问题：在相同job数与相同每job shots下，比较不同优化器如何"填充"每个job。对Pauli旋转电路，以参数移动模板（parameter-shift stencil）作为批处理，其梯度误差随每job shots呈反比下降，而SPSA存在无法通过增加shots消除的误差下限；仿真中批处理参数移动所需job数为SPSA的1/5至1/2，硬件上可在50个job内追平SPSA最终训练损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过云队列访问量子处理器时，每个往返（job）代价高昂，且单个job可携带多个电路。因此经典优化器决定的不仅是每shot学到多少，而是每job学到多少。作者希望在job数与shots数固定、仅"填充方式"不同的条件下比较优化器，从而把优化器设计与开销摊销、加速器资源高效利用联系起来。

Method: 以量子神经网络分类器为研究对象，在job数与每job shots相同的受控条件下比较优化器。发现直接移植面向QAOA的批处理模式搜索在参数达到数十个时失效；有效的可迁移原则是"在发送job前固定一批参数向量"。对由Pauli旋转构成的电路，采用parameter-shift模板作为自然批次，并分析其梯度误差；与之对照分析SPSA（同时扰动法）的误差结构。另提出结构感知的stencil search，将同类行读作正弦量。实验包括仿真（24–48参数、两个噪声水平）与IBM ibm_fez硬件（6与8量子比特、三次交错运行）。

Result: 理论上：参数移动模板的梯度误差随每job shots的倒数下降，而SPSA误差存在与shots无关的下限。仿真中：24至48参数下，批处理参数移动训练所需job数为SPSA的1/5至1/2，且该排序在两个噪声水平下保持；新的结构感知stencil search同样优于SPSA。硬件上：ibm_fez三次交错运行中，批处理优化器在SPSA的50个job中的第4至第20个job内即达到其最终训练损失；8量子比特时测试精度明显更高，6量子比特时差异不显著。所有硬件数据可由原始shots重新推导。

Conclusion: 混合量子-经典训练的关键瓶颈在于每次昂贵量子交互的有效利用，而非单shot信息量。在job内预先固定批次（对Pauli旋转电路即参数移动模板）可显著摊销通信与排队开销，并在精度上优于SPSA；但该批处理模式搜索不能直接平移至多参数QNN，可迁移的是"发送前固定批次"这一原则。

Abstract: When a quantum processor is reached through a cloud queue, a variational algorithm pays for every round trip, and a single round trip can carry many circuits. The classical optimizer therefore decides how much is learned per job, not only per shot. We study this for a quantum neural network classifier. We compare optimizers that are given the same number of jobs and the same shots per job, and that differ only in how they fill each job. This comparison treats hybrid quantum-classical training as an execution-efficiency problem: how to obtain useful optimization progress from each costly interaction with a quantum processor. The resulting perspective connects optimizer design to overhead amortization and efficient use of accelerator resources. A direct port of a batched pattern search that worked for the quantum approximate optimization algorithm stalls once the model has a few dozen parameters. What does transfer is the principle behind it: fill each job with a batch of parameter vectors that is fixed before the job is sent. For circuits built from Pauli rotations the natural batch is the parameter-shift stencil. We show that its gradient error falls as the inverse of the shots per job, while the error of simultaneous-perturbation methods (SPSA) has a floor that no number of shots removes. In simulation, batched parameter-shift training needs between a fifth and a half of the jobs SPSA needs, for 24 to 48 parameters, and the ordering holds at two noise levels. A new structure-aware stencil search reads the same kind of rows as sinusoids and also beats SPSA. On IBM's ibm_fez processor, in three interleaved runs at six and eight qubits, the batched optimizer reached SPSA's final training loss within 4 to 20 of its 50 jobs. Its test accuracy was clearly higher at eight qubits; at six qubits the difference was not significant. All hardware numbers can be re-derived from the raw shots.

</details>


### [105] [Generation and conversion of vortex light in coherently prepared hydrogen atoms and hydrogen-like ions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16367)
*Fan Meng,Guo-Feng Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出在相干制备的三能级Λ型原子介质中产生与转换光学涡旋的理论方案：下两能级间随方位角变化的相对相位给生成场附加相位缠绕，理想模型给出l₂=l₁+m关系，并在共振条件预言EIT与吸收抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有涡旋光产生与OAM模式转换多依赖空间光调制器、螺旋相位板等线性光学元件，缺乏基于原子相干的可调谐机制；作者希望利用空间原子相干作为控制涡旋光的新自由度。

Method: 三能级Λ型原子系综相干制备，下两能级间引入方位角依赖的相对相位（相干缠绕数m），通过解析推导求解生成场的相位缠绕，并用氢原子跃迁参数做数值示例，考察高斯输入与涡旋输入的两种情形；共振条件下分析吸收与亮态组分衰减、EIT的出现。

Result: 理想模型下获得l₂=l₁+m：高斯输入（l₁=0）可产生涡旋，入射涡旋可转换为不同OAM模式；解析结果与数值示例一致；共振时亮态成分被衰减、EIT出现，后续传播中吸收被强烈抑制。

Conclusion: 该方案为通过空间原子相干调控涡旋光提供了潜在框架；但空间相干的实验制备被明确列为初始假设，尚待解决，方案仍属理论层面。

Abstract: We propose a theoretical scheme for generating and converting optical vortices in a coherently prepared three-level Λ-type atomic medium. An azimuthally varying relative phase between the two lower states imparts additional phase winding to the generated field, yielding l2=l1+m in the ideal model, where l1 is the input topological charge and m the coherence winding number. Thus, a Gaussian input can generate a vortex, while an incident vortex can be converted to a different orbital angular momentum (OAM) mode. Analytical results and numerical examples using hydrogen transition parameters illustrate both processes. Under resonant conditions, the model predicts strongly suppressed absorption during subsequent propagation as the bright-state component is attenuated and electromagnetically induced transparency (EIT) emerges. Spatial coherence is assumed as an initial condition; its detailed experimental preparation remains open. This scheme offers a potential framework for controlling vortex light through spatial atomic coherence.

</details>


### [106] [No Convergence in Learned Eavesdropping-Detection Dynamics for BB84 Quantum Key Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16372)
*Ramon Jose C. Bagunu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将窃听者与检测器的博弈建模为交替最优响应博弈，发现防御方无法收敛，窃听窗口在低噪声下持续敞开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作显示强化学习窃听者可在BB84中低于门限的误码率下实施窃听并保留可用密钥；本文追问同样具备自适应学习能力的检测器能否关闭这一隐蔽窗口。

Method: 将交互刻画为表格型窃听者与表格型阈值自适应检测器之间的交替最优响应博弈；采用Q-Learning、SARSA、Double Q-Learning三种学习规则、三个信道噪声水平与统一虚警纪律；以可剥削性（新攻击者对最终检测器做最优响应所得的检测率与可蒸馏密钥比例）为指标；设置两组对照（冻结防御者重训攻击者、冻结攻击者重训防御者）以衡量最优响应过程的方差；并检验"后动者信息不对称"作为候选机制。

Result: 在二十轮内动力学未收敛，可剥削性不趋近不动点，轮间波动不衰减；两组对照表明双方波动幅度与最优响应程序本身的方差相当；行为依赖噪声：低噪声下隐蔽窗口仍敞开，中等噪声下防御者平均而言可遏制攻击者但博弈仍未稳定，高噪声下起抑制作用的实为信道而非学习策略；中等噪声下观察到显著的反持续性，低噪声下则与最优响应噪声一致。

Conclusion: 学习型检测器无法由任何单一轮次加以认证；关闭隐蔽窗口需打破信息结构，而非在自适应竞争中胜过攻击者。

Abstract: In prior work we showed that a reinforcement-learning eavesdropper can intercept BB84 quantum key distribution while holding the quantum bit error rate below the abort threshold and keeping a usable distillable key. In this paper we ask whether an equally adaptive learning detector can close this stealth window. We cast the interaction as an alternating best-response game between a tabular eavesdropper and a tabular threshold-adapting detector. We measure exploitability, which is the detection rate and distillable key fraction achieved by a fresh attacker best-responding to the final detector. Across three learning rules (Q-Learning, SARSA, Double Q-Learning), three channel-noise levels, and a uniform false-alarm discipline, the dynamics do not settle out to twenty rounds. The exploitability does not approach a fixed point, and its round-to-round swings do not decay. Two controls, one retraining the attacker against a frozen defender and one retraining the defender against a frozen attacker, show that the swings on each side match the variance of the best-response procedure itself. The dynamics fluctuate at the scale of best-response variance rather than converging, with neither player settling to a fixed response. The behavior is noise-dependent, and the stealth window stays open at the low noise where practical QKD operates. The defender contains the attacker on average at intermediate noise without the contest settling, and at high noise the channel rather than the learned policy suppresses the attack. As a candidate mechanism we examine a last-mover information asymmetry, supported by significant anti-persistence at intermediate noise and consistent with best-response noise at low noise. The results suggest that a learned detector cannot be certified from any single round, and that closing the window calls for breaking the information structure rather than out-adapting the attacker.

</details>


### [107] [Loss of the quadratic speedup in Grover search under local and coherent noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16398)
*Imama Tul Birrah Khan,Muhammad Faryad*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文解析了噪声下Grover算法的查询复杂度与成功概率演化：证明去极化噪声不减慢Grover旋转、仅使相干权重随深度几何衰减；局域噪声可归结为强度放大n倍的全局信道，据此给出各噪声模型下的封闭解与容忍阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grover算法的平方根级加速在噪声下会被侵蚀，但侵蚀速度缺乏解析刻画；既有工作多针对特定噪声模型给出数值或渐近结果，缺少统一、闭合形式的理论框架，也不清楚不同错误类型（相位翻转、比特翻转、去极化、振幅阻尼、相干角度误差）危害程度差异的机理。

Method: 在每层门后施加作用于整个寄存器的去极化信道（与门对易，故噪声态=无噪声态乘几何衰减的相干权重）；对独立量子比特错误，证明理想Grover算符将推出二维搜索平面的振幅冻结，从而精确给出单次错误的影响；大数据库极限下将搜索平面内部分约化为Bloch型方程（算法为旋转，噪声表现为泄漏与退相位），推导比特翻转、相位翻转、去极化、振幅阻尼的速率并求解；对旋转角相干误差用随深度线性展宽的带状界刻画成功概率。

Result: 去极化噪声下Grover旋转角不被减慢，仅相干权重几何衰减，最优停止点提前，期望查询代价仅在累积噪声较小时保持平方根标度；单次错误分析显示相位翻转在搜索早期危害最大、比特翻转在后期最大、组合错误在任何时刻均有害；局域噪声在主导阶等价于被量子比特数放大n倍的全局信道，故每比特可容忍错误率比数据库规模的逆平方根还快一个寄存器尺寸幂次下降；相干角度误差的成功概率被线性展宽带所界。

Conclusion: 论文以闭合形式统一复现了此前针对特定噪声模型的标度结果，并解释了不同错误类型危害差异的来源，为噪声下Grover算法的容错阈值与停止时间选择提供了可解析的设计准则。

Abstract: Grover's algorithm searches an unstructured database with a number of oracle queries that grows only as the square root of its size. Noise erodes this advantage, and we ask, analytically, how quickly. When a depolarizing channel acts on the whole register after every gate layer, it commutes with the gates, and the noisy state is the noiseless one with a coherent weight that decays geometrically with depth. The Grover rotation is not slowed. Its weight decays, the best stopping point moves earlier, and the expected query cost keeps its square-root scaling only while the accumulated noise over the circuit stays small. For independent errors on the qubits we show that the ideal Grover operator freezes any amplitude pushed out of the two-dimensional search plane. This gives the exact effect of a single error: phase flips do most damage early in the search, bit flips late, and combined errors at any time. For large databases it also reduces the noisy search to Bloch-type equations for the part of the state that stays in the plane, in which the algorithm is a rotation and the noise appears as leakage and dephasing. We derive the rates for bit-flip, phase-flip, depolarizing, and amplitude-damping noise and solve the equations. Local noise acts, to leading order, like a global channel strengthened by the number of qubits, so the tolerable error per qubit falls faster than the inverse square root of the database size by one power of the register size. For coherent errors in the rotation angle we bound the success probability by a band that widens linearly with depth. Our results recover, in closed form, scalings found earlier for particular noise models, and they explain why some errors are more harmful than others.

</details>


### [108] [Probing the extreme quantum violation of the Leggett-Garg inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16401)
*Akarshit Baranwal,Vishnu Purushothaman,A. K. Pan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文批判性检验了声称量子理论可超越"时间Tsirelson界"（TTB）的两项研究（PRL 113, 050401；PRL 135, 220202），指出其论证存在概念缺陷：一旦恰当处理相关问题，LG不等式的宏观实在论界即达到代数最大值，故不存在TTB的违背；这些"违背"依赖超出标准LG框架的形式，对检验宏观实在论与量子理论的不相容性无实质意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Leggett-Garg不等式用于检验宏观实在论与量子理论的不相容性，其最大量子违背常被称为时间Tsirelson界（TTB），类比于Bell不等式的Tsirelson界。然而近期若干研究声称TTB可在量子理论内被超越（如利用破简并测量或幺正叠加），这与TTB作为上界的地位相冲突，因此有必要审查这些主张的推理是否成立。

Method: 概念分析与理论论证：逐一审查所引文献中声称TTB违背的推导，识别其在测量定义、框架假设等方面的概念性缺陷；在此基础上重新界定宏观实在论界，并考察修正后不等式所能达到的界值。

Result: 所声称的TTB违背均源于概念上的不当处理；正确处理后，LG不等式的宏观实在论界升至代数最大值，因而无法获得对TTB的违背。此外，这些方案所依据的框架超出了标准LG框架。

Conclusion: 所谓TTB违背并不成立：既往主张依赖非标准LG框架，不能作为宏观实在论与量子理论不相容性的有效判据。TTB作为量子违背上界的地位在标准框架内得以保持。

Abstract: The Leggett-Garg (LG) inequalities were formulated for testing the incompatibility between the classical worldview of macrorealism and quantum theory. By analogy with the Tsirelson bound for Bell inequalities, the maximal quantum violation of LG inequalities is commonly referred to as the temporal Tsirelson bound (TTB). Nevertheless, several studies have claimed that, unlike the Tsirelson bound, the TTB can be exceeded within quantum theory. In particular, by employing degeneracy-breaking measurement in [\href{ https://doi.org/10.1103/PhysRevLett.113.050401}{Phys. Rev. Lett. \textbf{113}, 050401 (2014)}] and by using the superposition of unitaries in [\href{https://doi.org/10.1103/vydp-9qqq}{Phys. Rev. Lett. \textbf{135}, 220202 (2025)}], it is argued that the TTB can be violated. In this work, we critically examine the reasoning underlying these purported TTB violations and show that the corresponding arguments suffer from conceptual shortcomings. We further show that once these conceptual issues are addressed appropriately, the macrorealist bound of LG inequality reaches the algebraic maximum, and hence no violation of the TTB is obtained. In particular, we demonstrate that the proposed TTB violations rely on frameworks that go beyond the standard LG framework, and therefore are of no relevance for testing the incompatibility between macrorealism and quantum theory.

</details>


### [109] [Engineering Bosonic Steady States through Fock-Space Currents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16431)
*Yan Liu,Jiahua Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出统一的Fock空间电流工程框架，用稳态电流平衡律实现可选择的Fock态自主稳定化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fock态是量子技术的重要资源，但固有损耗使其稳定化困难；已有方案多针对特定微观过程，缺乏统一物理图像与可迁移设计原则。

Method: 构建Fock空间电流工程框架，推导稳态电流平衡律，预测布居背景随Fock数反比标度，并量化局域电流对目标Fock态布居的选择性增强；结合全量子模拟验证。

Result: 电流平衡律揭示不同微观实现遵循同一稳态设计规则，仅通过移动局域跃迁即可选择不同目标态而无需改变整体稳定化架构；全量子模拟证实可从真空自主建立目标态布居。

Conclusion: 该工作给出Fock态稳定化的共同物理图像，并建立了工程化可选择玻色稳态的可迁移设计原理。

Abstract: Fock states are valuable resources for quantum technologies, but intrinsic loss makes their stabilization challenging. Whereas existing stabilization schemes are typically formulated for specific microscopic processes, here we develop a unified Fock-space current-engineering framework for the autonomous stabilization of selectable Fock states. We derive a stationary current-balance law that predicts a population background scaling inversely with Fock number and quantifies how a local current selectively enhances the target Fock-state population. This law reveals how distinct microscopic implementations obey the same steady-state design rule, allowing different target states to be selected by moving a local transition without changing the overall stabilization architecture. Full quantum simulations further support these predictions and demonstrate the autonomous buildup of target-state populations from the vacuum. Our results provide a common physical picture of Fock-state stabilization and establish a transferable design principle for engineering selectable bosonic steady states.

</details>


### [110] [Circuit-Level Analysis of Quantum Error Correction in Second-Generation Quantum Repeaters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16435)
*Suraj Vishwanath,Jonas Hawellek,Roberto Ferrara,Christian Deppe*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 对含稳定子量子纠错的第二代量子中继器进行电路级仿真，比较Steane码与五量子比特码在不同噪声下的端到端性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第二代量子中继器需依赖量子纠错提升保真度，但码的选择与主导物理噪声机制的关系尚不明确，缺乏电路级定量比较。

Method: 构建含编码、综合征提取、解码与纠缠交换的两段式中继链路模型，在电路级直接注入非相干噪声（退极化、退相位）与相干噪声（系统性过旋转），比较Steane码与五量子比特稳定子码。

Result: 相干噪声下Steane码优于五量子比特码；低噪声非相干场景且受影响物理比特较多时，五量子比特码端到端保真度更高；高噪声区不编码反而最优；混合噪声下两类噪声存在中等程度的交叉影响。

Conclusion: 码的选择须适配主导噪声机制，且电路复杂度与硬件感知实现方式在相干噪声主导情形下显著影响性能，为中继器纠错设计提供依据。

Abstract: We present a circuit-level simulation of second-generation quantum repeaters with stabilizer-based quantum error correction. We model a two-segment repeater link including encoding, syndrome extraction, decoding, and entanglement swapping, and compare the performance of the single-error-correcting Steane and five-qubit stabilizer codes under different noise scenarios. To that extent, we consider noise sources with incoherent (depolarizing and dephasing) and coherent (systematic over-rotation) errors and incorporate them directly at the circuit level. While the Steane code outperforms the five-qubit code at coherent errors, the five-qubit code achieves higher end-to-end fidelities under incoherent noise in the low-noise regime when a large number of physical qubits is affected by it. At higher noise regimes, employing no code turns out to be the best option. In mixed noise scenarios, we observe moderate cross-effects between the noise classes regarding the error correction performance. We conclude that code selection in second-generation quantum repeaters must be adapted to the dominant physical noise mechanism and that circuit complexity and hardware-aware implementation options influence performance in coherent-error-dominated regimes, providing design insights for error correction in quantum repeaters.

</details>


### [111] [Learning Sparse Graph States with Local Pauli Measurements: Tight Nonadaptive Bounds and an Adaptive Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16456)
*Matteo De Francesco*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在单量子比特X/Y/Z基测量下识别图态所需副本数：证明非自适应学习器的$d^2$依赖不可避免，而副本间自适应（广度优先）可突破该壁垒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ouyang与Tomamichel给出$d$-正则图态识别需$O(d^2\log n)$副本、任意测量下界$Ω(d\log n)$，并提问对$d$的二次依赖是否必要。本文旨在判定该二次依赖的本质来源，并考察自适应策略能否降低副本复杂度。

Method: 核心观察：单副本至多揭示邻接矩阵某主子矩阵的二元零度（binary nullity）。对随机完美匹配并的零度控制使用傅里叶估计；二分图情形采用types方法。下界针对非自适应学习器，并允许无限制经典解码器及常数比例错误边。

Result: 当$d^3\ln n\le n$时，任何非自适应策略需$Ω(d^2\log n)$副本，即二次依赖必要。对二分隐藏的连通二分$d$-正则对数直径图，非自适应需$Θ(d^2\log n)$；广度优先自适应仅需$O(d\log^2 n/\log\log n)$；公开二分结构后非自适下降至$Θ(d\log n)$。

Conclusion: $d$的二次依赖对非自适应学习器不可避免（即使经典解码无约束），但自适应可显著超越该壁垒；下界由单副本揭示的主子矩阵二元零度刻画。

Abstract: We study how many copies of an unknown $n$-qubit graph state are needed to identify its graph when every qubit is measured in the $X$, $Y$ or $Z$ basis. Ouyang and Tomamichel showed that $O(d^2\log n)$ copies suffice for $d$-regular graphs, while any measurement needs $Ω(d\log n)$, and asked whether the quadratic dependence on $d$ is necessary. We show that it is for nonadaptive learners: if $d^3\ln n\le n$, every nonadaptive strategy needs $Ω(d^2\log n)$ copies, even with an unrestricted classical decoder and even if a small constant fraction of the edges may be reported wrongly. We then show that adaptivity between copies can beat this barrier. On connected bipartite $d$-regular graphs of logarithmic diameter whose bipartition is hidden, nonadaptive learners still need $Θ(d^2\log n)$ copies, but a breadth-first adaptive learner needs only $O(d\log^2 n/\log\log n)$; revealing the bipartition brings the nonadaptive cost down to $Θ(d\log n)$. The lower bounds rest on a simple fact: one copy reveals at most the binary nullity of a principal submatrix of the adjacency matrix. We control this nullity for random unions of perfect matchings through Fourier estimates and, in the bipartite case, a method-of-types argument.

</details>


### [112] [Symmetry and quasi-gauge invariance of Krylov complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16474)
*Edoardo Ferretti,Matteo G. A. Paris,Claudia Benedetti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究了Krylov扩展复杂度的普适不变性：两类保持能量分布与Hamiltonian矩不变的幺正变换不改变复杂度；手征连续时间量子行走中手征相位一般会解除谱简并，导致长时平均复杂度的不连续跃变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Krylov扩展复杂度刻画量子系统在时间演化中对Hilbert空间的探索程度，但其在何种变换/参数下保持不变、以及手征性与输运不对称是否直接对应复杂度响应，尚缺乏系统刻画。作者希望厘清复杂度的不变性来源及其与谱结构的关系。

Method: 通过识别两类保持复杂度的幺正变换（Hamiltonian的对称算符，以及初始态为其本征态的局域U(1)变换），并将该性质与初始态能量分布的 invariance 及Hamiltonian矩的保持不变性相联系；把局域U(1)变换应用于手征连续时间量子行走中的准规范变换，分析循环（circulant）Hamiltonian中手征相位对谱简并的解除效应，并用非循环量子路由器考察相位依赖的谱权重的作用。

Result: 手征相位在循环Hamiltonian中一般解除谱简并，并使长时平均复杂度发生不连续变化；对局域初始态与非简并谱，复杂度为 C̄=(D−1)/2（D为Hilbert空间维数），与连通度无关；非循环量子路由器表明相位依赖谱权重具有额外作用。结论是Krylov复杂度可在保持概率时间反演对称时发生变化，其对手征性的响应源自谱变化而非直接源自输运不对称。

Conclusion: Krylov复杂度的不变性由能量分布与Hamiltonian矩决定；手征性的作用通过谱结构（简并解除与谱权重）体现，而非直接对应输运不对称，因此复杂度可作为探测谱变化的量而非输运不对称的直接指标。

Abstract: Krylov spread complexity quantifies how extensively a quantum system explores its Hilbert space during time evolution. We identify two classes of unitary transformations that preserve the complexity of the associated dynamics, and relate this property to the invariance of the energy distribution of the initial state and of its associated Hamiltonian moments. These transformations are either symmetry operator of the Hamiltonian, or local $U(1)$ transformations of which the initial state is an eigenstate. The latter naturally arise as quasi-gauge transformations in chiral continuous-time quantum walks, to which the invariance properties are applied. We show that chiral phases in circulant Hamiltonians generically lift spectral degeneracies, leading to a discontinuous change in the long-time-averaged complexity. For a localized initial states and a nondegenerate spectrum, complexity is $\bar{C}=(D-1)/2$, where $D$ is the Hilbert-space dimension, independently of the degree of connectivity. A non-circulant quantum router illustrates the additional role of phase-dependent spectral weights. These examples show that Krylov complexity can change even when probability time-reversal symmetry is preserved, linking its response to chirality to spectral changes rather than directly to transport asymmetry.

</details>


### [113] [Quantum Mpemba effect from logical-information exposure in monitored circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16479)
*Bochen Huang,D. L. Zhou,Chenfeng Cao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文在受监测Clifford链中构造并量化了一种纠缠熵的Mpemba逆转效应：未编码的Bell对态经过一个测量周期后，其平均纠缠熵低于由更少Bell对制备的加扰参考态，且差距随链长线性增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典与量子Mpemba效应关注初始偏离稳态更远的状态反而更快趋近稳态的现象。作者希望澄清该效应在含测量的量子多体动力学中如何出现，并区分"真正的量子Mpemba效应"与平凡的表观逆转，给出可证明的边界与判据。

Method: 以受监测Clifford链为平台，比较一个循环后未编码Bell对态（初始熵更高）与加扰参考态（由更少Bell对制备）的平均纠缠熵；提出"逻辑信息暴露"机制，即稳定子态每被记录解析出一个独立逻辑宇称即损失一个ebit；利用半链内加扰可把逻辑信息分散到多格点而保持所有Schmidt系数不变；并用独立精确unitary two-design编码器证明参考系综的熵超越上界，结合稳态熵界在128与256量子比特规模上认证该效应。

Result: 未编码Bell对态的平均纠缠熵低于加扰参考态，差距随链长线性增长；在独立精确two-design编码器下，任何初始平均熵不更大的纯态参考系综在任何时刻领先的平均熵至多为两个ebits；当稳态熵为次广延时，未编码逆转为大尺寸下的量子Mpemba效应；并在128与256量子比特上通过稳态熵界完成认证。

Conclusion: 纠缠熵中的Mpemba逆转可由逻辑信息暴露机制解释，并受two-design参考系综的严格上界约束；在次广延稳态熵条件下，该效应确为大规模量子Mpemba效应，并已在高量子比特数下得到认证。

Abstract: In the Mpemba effect, a state initially farther from stationarity overtakes one that starts closer. After one cycle in a monitored Clifford chain, an unencoded Bell-pair state has lower mean entanglement entropy than a scrambled reference prepared from fewer pairs. The gap grows linearly with chain length. This reversal is driven by logical-information exposure: a stabilizer state loses one ebit per independent logical parity resolved by the record. Scrambling within each half can spread this information over many sites while preserving every Schmidt coefficient. We prove that with independent exact unitary two-design encoders, any pure reference ensemble with no greater mean initial entropy can lead in mean entropy by at most two ebits at any time. If the stationary entropy is subextensive, the unencoded reversal is a quantum Mpemba effect at large sizes. We further certify this effect at 128 and 256 qubits using stationary-entropy bounds.

</details>


### [114] [Effect of atomic interactions on quantum communication protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16519)
*Zixian Hong,John C. Schotland*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立原子介质中光子量子通信的第一性原理分析框架：从场与二能级原子系综耦合的实空间量子化出发，导出偏振编码单光子态的有效模型，并将其应用于超密编码与BB84协议，揭示通信容量与密钥率随原子-场耦合强度的单调衰减及临界行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光子量子通信分析多采用理想信道模型，缺乏从微观光-物质相互作用出发的、对偏振编码单光子在原子介质中传输的定量描述；作者希望统一刻画通信效率（超密编码容量）与通信安全性（BB84密钥率）在真实原子介质中的退化规律。

Method: 对与二能级原子系综耦合的电磁场做实空间量子化，构建偏振编码单光子态的有效模型；将该框架用于超密编码，在擦除、退相位与去极化信道下评估固定Bell态字母表的协议容量；用于BB84时，提出丢弃擦除结果、并用微观模型估计内禀损耗率的修正协议，推导渐近密钥率。

Result: 超密编码容量随原子-场耦合增强单调下降；BB84修正协议的渐近密钥率同样随耦合强度单调降低，并在超过有限临界耦合后归零。

Conclusion: 原子-场耦合是限制原子介质中光子量子通信性能的关键参数，微观第一性原理模型可定量预测通信容量与安全密钥率的退化及临界阈值，为介质中量子通信协议的评估与优化提供统一工具。

Abstract: We develop a first-principles framework for analyzing photonic quantum communication in an atomic medium. Starting from a real-space quantization of the electromagnetic field coupled to an ensemble of two-level atoms, we derive an effective model for polarization-encoded single-photon states. We apply this framework to superdense coding to study communication efficiency and to the BB84 quantum key distribution protocol for communication security. For superdense coding, we evaluate the protocol capacity of the fixed Bell-state alphabet under erasure, dephasing, and depolarizing channels and show that it decreases monotonically with increasing atom-field coupling. For BB84, we formulate a modified protocol that discards erasure outcomes and uses the microscopic model to estimate the intrinsic loss rate. We derive the corresponding asymptotic key rate and show that it decreases monotonically with coupling strength and vanishes beyond a finite critical coupling.

</details>


### [115] [Asymptotic Attainability of the Channel RLD Fisher-Information Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16578)
*Masahito Hayashi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出信道RLD Fisher信息等于最优渐近SLD Fisher信息的精确判定条件（仅涉及Choi矩阵及其一阶导数的半定可行性条件），并给出该比值无界的显式例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信道RLD Fisher信息是可计算的上界，但不总可达（即使Choi矩阵正定）；需要确定等式成立的确切条件并量化该上界可能有多松。

Method: 基于Zhou与Jiang的渐近优化框架，推导以Choi矩阵及其一阶导数为输入的半定可行性等式判据；对广义退相干信道化为非负证书权重（和为1）的线性方程组；在完全退相干点计算任意有限次使用下的最优SLD信息，并考察无辅助、重复探针是否达到它。

Result: 判据允许RLD矩阵最大本征值退化、Choi矩阵秩亏且导数支撑在其值域上，且原信道族在附近可变秩；完全退相干点处最优SLD信息由重复无辅助探针达到，且对自适应策略仍最优；构造出信道RLD信息与最优渐近SLD信息每次使用之比的例子：随维数增长无界，或在固定量子比特维数下随最小Choi本征值趋于零而无界。

Conclusion: RLD上界的可达性由Choi层面的半定可行性精确刻画；该上界一般不可紧，其与真实渐近SLD极限的差距可任意大，说明基于RLD的量子信道参数估计评估需谨慎。

Abstract: The channel right-logarithmic-derivative (RLD) Fisher information is a computable upper bound on the optimal asymptotic symmetric-logarithmic-derivative (SLD) Fisher information per channel use over causally ordered, parameter-independent strategies. The bound is not always attained, even if the Choi matrix is positive definite. We determine exactly when equality holds using a semidefinite feasibility condition involving only the Choi matrix and its first derivative. The condition allows a degenerate largest eigenvalue of the RLD matrix and rank-deficient Choi matrices with derivatives supported on their range. The original channel family may change rank nearby. For generalized dephasing channels with a positive-definite correlation matrix at the evaluation point, the condition becomes a system of linear equations for nonnegative certificate weights that sum to one. At a completely dephasing point we also calculate the optimal SLD information for every finite number of uses and show that a repeated, ancilla-free probe attains it, even against adaptive strategies. Explicit examples give an unbounded ratio of the channel RLD information to the optimal asymptotic SLD information per use, either as the dimension grows or at fixed qubit dimension as the smallest Choi eigenvalue tends to zero. We derive the equality criterion and its consequences from the asymptotic optimization of Zhou and Jiang.

</details>


### [116] [Beauty and charm jet identification with end-to-end training on a superconducting quantum processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16607)
*Lucio Anderlini,Pietro Asinari,Gianluca Bertaina,Davide Calonico,Marco Coïsson,Alessio Gianelle,Donatella Lucchesi,Giovanni Passaleva,Matteo Maria Piredda,Lorenzo Sestini,Michele Veltri,Davide Zuliani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在IQM 5比特超导量子计算机上首次实现LHCb实验b/c喷注分类的完整变分量子线路机器学习流程（含训练与测试），并证明硬件噪声未显著降低性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重味喷注（b、c夸克碎裂形成的粒子束）鉴别是LHC实验中最困难的分类任务之一；经典机器学习已取得成功，量子机器学习被视为潜在替代方案，但问题维度高（通常涉及数百个观测量），而当前量子计算机比特数有限，能否胜任此类任务尚属开放问题。

Method: 面向LHCb实验b喷注与c喷注分类构建变分量子线路，部署于都灵理工大学安装的IQM 5比特超导量子计算机；使用LHC实验的模拟数据，将机器学习工作流的全部阶段（训练与测试）均在真实量子硬件上执行；并提出量子硬件训练时间评估与线路优化的框架。

Result: 据作者所知，这是首个在真实量子硬件上完成全部训练与测试流程的LHC实验喷注鉴别量子算法演示；结果表明硬件引起的量子噪声未显著劣化分类性能。

Conclusion: 该工作为基于超导量子比特的更大规模量子器件应用铺路：更多比特可编码超出经典模拟能力的信息，并为探究高能物理分类任务中是否最终可实现量子优势迈出一步。

Abstract: The identification of heavy-flavor jets, i.e. collimated streams of particles originating from the fragmentation of beauty and charm quarks produced in proton-proton collisions, represents one of the most challenging classification tasks in measurements at Large Hadron Collider experiments. In recent years, advanced machine learning techniques have been successfully employed to address this problem, while quantum machine learning has emerged as a promising alternative approach. However, the high dimensionality of the problem, typically involving hundreds of observables, raises an open question: whether the current generation of quantum computers, characterized by limited qubit count, can effectively tackle such complex tasks. In this work, a variational quantum circuit for the classification of b- and c-jets at the LHCb experiment is implemented on a 5-qubit superconducting quantum computer manufactured by IQM and installed at the Politecnico di Torino. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of a quantum algorithm for jet identification using simulated data from an LHC experiment in which all stages of the machine-learning workflow, including training and testing, are performed on real quantum hardware. The results demonstrate that the impact of hardware-induced quantum noise does not significantly degrade the performance. A framework for the evaluation of the training time on the quantum hardware and for the circuit optimization is also proposed. These findings pave the way for future applications on larger quantum devices based on superconducting qubits, featuring a larger number of qubits enabling the encoding of information beyond the reach of classical simulation and providing a step toward investigating whether quantum advantage may eventually be achievable in high-energy physics classification tasks.

</details>


### [117] [A Quantum Algorithm for Computing All Eigenvalues and Eigenstates of Sparse Hermitian Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16610)
*Krishnakanta Barik,Goutam Paul*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种量子算法，用于计算稀疏厄米矩阵的全部互异特征值（以指定精度二进制串表示）并为每个特征值在其特征空间内制备对应特征向量（保持为量子态），运行时间为 Õ(n s² log²n / Δ_min)，在逆间隙达 O(n)、稀疏度 s = o(√n/log n) 时优于经典 O(n³) 全谱全特征向量计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏厄米矩阵的特征值与特征向量计算是代数基础问题，也是众多科学与计算应用的核心子程序。经典稠密方法（Householder 三对角化 + QR 迭代）需 O(n³) 时间；Lanczos 利用稀疏性可将全部特征值计算降至 O(n²s)，但恢复全部特征向量仍需 O(n³)。因此希望借助量子算法在全谱及全部特征向量的获取上获得更优的渐进复杂度。

Method: 针对 n×n、s-稀疏的厄米矩阵 H，设计量子算法：将特征值编码为给定精度的二进制串，特征向量以量子态形式保存在各自特征空间内，从而无需重建经典向量；算法输出支持以制各特征态 |x⟩ 对可观测量 M 估计期望值 ⟨x|M|x⟩。复杂度分析以归一化矩阵的最小谱间隙 Δ_min 度量，并在标准稀疏访问模型假设下进行。

Result: 算法运行时间为 Õ(n s² log²n / Δ_min)。当 Δ_min^{-1} = O(n)（即 n 个互异特征值情形下的最佳标度，如近乎均匀间隔的谱，例如 perfect-state-transfer 自旋链与 Sylvester–Kac 矩阵）且稀疏度 s = o(√n/log n) 时，本算法在计算全部特征值与特征向量上相较经典 O(n³) 方法取得更优的渐进运行时间。

Conclusion: 在所设稀疏访问假设与谱间隙条件下，量子算法可在全谱及全部特征向量（以量子态形式）的获取任务上渐进超越经典 O(n³) 方法，为利用稀疏性与量子态表示的谱计算提供了新路径。

Abstract: Computation of eigenvalues and eigenvectors of a sparse Hermitian matrix is a fundamental problem in algebra, arising both as a problem of independent interest and as a key subroutine in a wide range of computational and scientific applications. In this work, we develop a quantum algorithm that determines the spectrum of a sparse Hermitian matrix, defined as the set of its distinct eigenvalues, and for each eigenvalue, prepares a corresponding eigenvector as a quantum state in its eigenspace. The eigenvalues are represented as binary strings with a prescribed precision, while the corresponding eigenvectors are kept as quantum states, eliminating the need to reconstruct their classical counterparts. The algorithm instead enables estimation of expectation values $\braket{x|M|x}$, where $\ket{x}$ is a prepared eigenstate and $M$ is an observable. For an $n \times n$ $s$-sparse Hermitian matrix $H$, dense methods based on Householder tridiagonalization and QR iteration compute all eigenvalues and eigenvectors in $\mathcal{O}(n^3)$ time. Exploiting sparsity, the Lanczos method computes all eigenvalues in $\mathcal{O}(n^2 s)$ time in exact arithmetic, but recovering all eigenvectors still requires $\mathcal{O}(n^3)$ time. In contrast, our algorithm runs in time $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(n s^2 \log^2 n / Δ_{\min})$, where $Δ_{\min}$ denotes the minimum spectral gap of the normalized matrix. For inverse gaps up to $Δ_{\min}^{-1} = \mathcal{O}(n)$, the most favorable scaling for $n$ distinct eigenvalues, attained by nearly uniformly spaced spectra such as those of perfect-state-transfer spin chains and the Sylvester-Kac matrix, and for sparsity up to $s = o(\sqrt{n}/\log n)$, the proposed algorithm achieves an improved asymptotic runtime over the classical $\mathcal{O}(n^3)$ approach for computing all eigenvalues and eigenvectors, under standard sparse-access assumptions.

</details>


### [118] [Quantum tunneling of a measurement-driven active particle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16626)
*Tobias Nadolny,Matteo Brunelli,Christoph Bruder,Michel Fruchart*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并演示了一种量子活性粒子：其运动由自旋取向锁定（自旋-轨道耦合+阻尼），取向通过量子Zeno效应下的连续测量而保持，单条量子轨迹呈现run-and-tumble式主动运动，并研究了其与势垒相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典活性粒子（active matter）具有由取向决定的自驱动持续运动，但量子层面是否存在对应物及其行为尚不清楚，本文旨在将活性粒子概念引入量子体系并考察其隧穿性质。

Method: 利用自旋-轨道耦合与阻尼使粒子运动方向锁定于自旋取向；借助量子Zeno效应（连续测量）使自旋取向保持持久，从而在单条量子轨迹层面实现主动的run-and-tumble运动；随后分析粒子撞击势垒时的行为与透射率。

Result: 单条量子轨迹表现出主动run-and-tumble运动；粒子撞击势垒时，其持续速度导致在势垒附近积累，而非自由量子粒子的反射行为；量子隧穿使该活性粒子的透射率高于类似经典粒子。

Conclusion: 自旋-轨道耦合、阻尼与量子Zeno效应可共同构建量子活性粒子，其轨迹与势垒相互作用显著区别于自由量子粒子，且隧穿效应提升其越障能力，为量子活性物质研究提供了原型。

Abstract: Active particles can exhibit persistent motion set by their orientation. We demonstrate this key property for a quantum active particle: The particle's motion is locked to the orientation of its spin due to spin-orbit coupling and damping, and the orientation is made persistent through continuous measurement via the quantum Zeno effect. As a result, individual quantum trajectories feature an active run-and-tumble motion. When the particle impinges on a potential barrier, its persistent velocity leads to accumulation near the barrier, in contrast with the reflection of a free quantum particle. We show that quantum tunneling enhances the active particle's transmission rate compared to that of an analogous classical particle.

</details>


### [119] [Geometric Theory of Quasiperiodically Driven Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16683)
*Chengzhi Ye,Marin Bukov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文借助反向绝热驱动构造了描述任意准周期驱动的一般框架，实现用有效平均能量算符Æ与规范势分解幺正演化，并给出准周期驱动基于动力学不变空间几何与拓扑的四重分类（(非)阿贝尔、(非)扭曲），进而推广Floquet定理并定义由不变本征空间合并标记的遍历性概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期驱动（Floquet）系统已有有效含时无关哈密顿量与本征态理论，而准周期驱动虽在非平衡动力学、哈密顿量工程与控制中应用广泛，却缺乏类似的通用描述框架，导致其演化难以用有效静态哈密顿量及其本征态刻画。

Method: 以counterdiabatic driving为基础，识别并确定任意准周期驱动的动力学不变空间（Floquet态的推广）；以精确有效平均能量算符Æ与相应规范势分解幺正演化，并在频率格点算符空间中用变分原理计算Æ与规范势；随后依据不变空间的几何与拓扑构造四重分类，并据此推导Floquet定理在不可通约准周期驱动下的直接推广及非阿贝尔推广。

Result: 得到准周期驱动的四类划分：(非)阿贝尔×(非)扭曲；其中阿贝尔非扭曲类可直接将Floquet定理推广至不可通约准周期驱动，非阿贝尔类给出Floquet理论的非阿贝尔推广；并提出遍历性概念，其出现以动力学不变本征空间的合并为标志，类比混合经典相空间中遍历性的涌现。

Conclusion: 所提出的有效数学框架、变分原理与非阿贝尔遍历性概念不仅适用于准周期驱动，还可推广到更广泛的驱动系统。

Abstract: Quasiperiodically driven systems give rise to a rich set of physical phenomena in contemporary quantum physics, with applications ranging from nonequilibrium dynamics to Hamiltonian engineering and control. However, unlike periodically driven (Floquet) systems, their study is hindered by the absence of a generic theory to describe their evolution using an effective time-independent Hamiltonian and its eigenstates. Based on counterdiabatic driving, we construct a general framework to identify and determine the dynamically invariant spaces of arbitrary quasiperiodic drives (i.e., the generalization of Floquet states). We decompose their unitary evolution using the exact effective average-energy operator Æ and the corresponding gauge potential, which we compute via a variational principle in frequency-lattice operator space. We then formulate a four-fold taxonomy of quasiperiodic drives based on the geometry and topology of their dynamically invariant spaces into (non)abelian and (un)twisted classes. The abelian untwisted class admits a direct extension of Floquet's theorem to incommensurate quasiperiodic drives; going beyond, we derive a nonabelian generalization of Floquet theory. Finally, we propose a concept of ergodicity whose onset is marked by coalescing dynamically invariant eigenspaces, in direct analogy to the emergence of ergodicity in a mixed classical phase space. The effective mathematical framework, variational principle, and nonabelian ergodicity we put forward apply beyond quasiperiodic drives.

</details>


### [120] [Persistent-mediator architecture for universal fault-tolerant quantum computation with color codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16691)
*Zhi-Cheng He,Jingbo Wang,Dong E. Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出跨维度"持久中介"架构：2D色码数据寄存器与3D中介模块在每周期边界保持各自编码，经瞬态受保护子系统耦合，实现可重复、确定性的逻辑T门循环而无需消耗或破坏性读出3D模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2D色码只能提供横向Clifford门，3D码可提供横向T门。既有跨维度方案要么切换计算编码，要么消耗一个3D编码态；若复用同一3D保护块，可省去反复的辅助态制备与末端读出。核心问题：3D模块能否在保持编码与保护的同时，为独立2D寄存器连续中介T门。

Method: 构造膜状联合逻辑测量，配合匹配的跨维度CZ、互补的弦状surgery与feed-forward，闭合确定性逻辑T门循环；周期内仅通过瞬态受保护子系统耦合，不做后选择、不做破坏性中介读出，并使3D块恢复初态。以稳定子分析确定逻辑作用与保持的空间距离；用Clifford替身做现象学与电路级模拟。

Result: 稳定子分析确认逻辑作用正确且空间距离保持；有限距离标度接近匹配的3D存储基准；顺序测试显示低错误区逻辑错误近似线性累积。

Conclusion: 持久中介使高维非Clifford能力可被重复调用，同时计算寄存器保持2D编码，免去辅助态重复制备与终端读出，为跨维度容错T门提供可复用方案。

Abstract: Two-dimensional color codes provide transversal Clifford gates, whereas suitable three-dimensional codes provide a transversal $T$ gate. Representative cross-dimensional schemes either switch the computational encoding or consume a 3D encoded state. Reusing the same protected 3D block would remove repeated auxiliary-state preparation and terminal readout, raising a central question: can the 3D module remain encoded and protected while mediating successive $T$ gates on a separate 2D register? Here we introduce a cross-dimensional persistent-mediator architecture that retains the 2D data register and 3D mediator as distinct encoded modules at each cycle boundary, coupled only through a transient protected subsystem. We construct a membrane-like joint-logical measurement and combine it with a matched cross-dimensional $CZ$, complementary string-like surgery, and feed-forward to close a deterministic logical $T$-gate cycle. The cycle avoids post-selection and destructive mediator readout and restores the 3D block to its initial logical state. Stabilizer analysis establishes the logical action and preserved spatial distance. Phenomenological and circuit-level simulations of a Clifford surrogate show finite-distance scaling close to the matched 3D memory benchmark, while sequential tests show approximately linear logical-error accumulation in the low-error regime. Persistent mediation enables repeated access to higher-dimensional non-Clifford capability while keeping the computational register in its 2D encoding.

</details>


### [121] [Thermodynamically Consistent Quantum-Classical Hybrid Dynamics: A Metriplectic Approach to Generalized Lindblad and Bloch-Torrey Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16708)
*Azeddine Zaidni,Ayoub Arraji,Philip J. Morrison*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出量子-经典混合动力学的度规辛（metriplectic）框架，通过能量守恒与非负熵产生保证热力学一致性，并给出Lindblad方程与Bloch–Torrey方程的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子-经典混合动力学普遍缺乏内禀的热力学一致性：混合演化中能量守恒与熵产生非负性难以同时保证，且量子与经典子系统间的耦合缺乏清晰的热力学解释。

Method: 构造度规辛框架：将混合Poisson括号与metriplectic 4-括号相结合，令量子与经典子系统之间的耦合完全由耗散过程产生；在此基础上导出热力学一致的混合型Lindblad主方程与Bloch–Torrey方程的推广形式。

Result: 获得热力学一致的量子弛豫与经典扩散的统一描述，并给出熵产生的清晰解释；混合Lindblad与Bloch–Torrey方程被推广至该框架内。

Conclusion: metriplectic 4-括号可为量子-经典混合动力学提供系统性的热力学一致表述，将耗散耦合、能量守恒与非负熵产生统一于单一几何框架中。

Abstract: We develop a metriplectic framework for quantum--classical hybrid dynamics that ensures thermodynamic consistency through conservation of energy and non-negative entropy production. The approach combines a hybrid Poisson bracket with a metriplectic $4$-bracket, with coupling between quantum and classical subsystems arising solely through dissipative processes. Within this hybrid formulation, we produce thermodynamically consistent generalizations of the Lindblad master equations and the Bloch--Torrey equations for open quantum systems. This provides a unified description of quantum relaxation and classical diffusion, together with a clear interpretation of entropy production.

</details>


### [122] [Probabilistic distillation of superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16719)
*Gökhan Torun*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究非正交态叠加（如光猫态）在"无叠加操作"下的态转换，给出最优转换概率的确定原则，并完整求解两个参考态情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 叠加态作为量子资源的价值取决于其各分量的干涉方式，但缺少定量刻画"叠加性"作为资源在态转换中的可转换性与最优概率的理论框架。

Method: 以"无叠加操作"（不能确定性地使各分量更相消干涉且保留全部分量）为自由操作集合；对分量置换对称的源态推出最优转换概率的固定原则；对相干态参考态证明光子损耗、光子减除等逐分量映射操作均属无叠加操作；对两个参考态完全求解，并分析集体操作与三参考态情形。

Result: 两参考态情形完全可解：等模系数叠加态（相干理论中最大相干态的对应物）彼此不等价——奇猫态经归一化光子减除可确定性变为偶猫态，而反向转换概率随振幅减小而趋于零；偶猫态与Yurke-Stoler猫态两个方向都不能确定性互转。集体操作优于逐个转换，但在相干蒸馏中无增益。三参考态及以上等正重叠时，无态可确定性到达所有其他态；干涉最相消的等模叠加态可确定性得到最大相干态，且对全体叠加的到达能力至少不劣于最大相干态，最坏情形下则无优势。

Conclusion: 叠加性构成一种有结构、非等价的资源：等模叠加态之间存在严格的转换序与概率不对称；该框架为猫态等非正交叠加态的操纵与资源量化提供了判据。

Abstract: How well a superposition of nonorthogonal states, such as an optical cat state, serves as a resource depends on how its components interfere. Here we determine the optimal probability of converting one such superposition into another by superposition-free operations. These operations cannot make the components of a superposition interfere more destructively with certainty while keeping all of them, and this principle fixes the optimal probability for every source that is symmetric under permutations of its components. When the reference states are coherent states, as in a cat state, every operation that maps each of them to a coherent state, such as photon loss or photon subtraction, is superposition-free. For two reference states we solve the problem completely. Superpositions with coefficients of equal modulus, the counterparts of the maximally coherent states of coherence theory, are therefore inequivalent resources: an odd cat state becomes even with certainty by a normalized photon subtraction, whereas an even one becomes odd only with a probability that vanishes for small amplitudes, and the even and the Yurke$-$Stoler cat states cannot be converted into each other with certainty in either direction. Collective operations on several copies can do better than converting them one at a time, except in the distillation of coherence, which gains nothing from them. For three or more reference states with equal positive overlaps, no state reaches every other state with certainty; the equal-modulus superpositions that interfere most destructively still yield a maximally coherent state with certainty and reach every superposition of all reference states at least as well as such a state does, but in the worst case no better.

</details>


### [123] [Ground-State Spin Squeezing and Collective Excitations in $\mathbb{Z}_2$-Symmetric Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16738)
*Arkadiusz Kosior,George Mihailescu,Karol Gietka*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示基态自旋压缩与集体激发之间的物理机制，指出在二次型集体模式下压缩由两个横向涨落代价之比决定、集体能隙由其几何平均决定，并给出可同时增强压缩与增大能隙的开隙分支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基态自旋压缩通常与量子临界点附近激发能隙闭合相关联，但能隙闭合会增加态制备难度并降低热鲁棒性，因此需要厘清压缩与集体激发的关系，寻找在能隙增大时仍可增强压缩的机制。

Method: 以各向异性 Lipkin–Meshkov–Glick 模型为出发点分析二次型集体模式；推导压缩、极化与按集体自旋跃迁强度加权的激发能之间的精确关系；结合 XXZ 链的弱耦合与自旋波分析；并对幂律相互作用（含最近邻极限）与 Ising 链做精确有限尺寸计算。

Result: 二次型集体模式下，压缩由两横向涨落代价之比决定，集体能隙由二者几何平均设定；造成代价不相等的配对项由 Z2 宇称允许但非必然。XXZ 链中压缩可在集体能隙与多体能隙同时增大时发展；有限尺寸计算确认存在使两能隙重合且随压缩改善而增大的负耦合分支，最近邻情形下该分支比能隙闭合分支具有更好的热鲁棒性；Ising 链中交错激发可降低多体能隙而不直接贡献集体涨落。

Conclusion: 研究解释了基态压缩既可伴随集体能隙闭合也可伴随能隙打开，并识别出兼具增强热鲁棒性与更快绝热制备潜力的开隙分支。

Abstract: Ground-state spin squeezing is a resource for quantum-enhanced measurements, but is commonly associated with a closing excitation gap near a quantum critical point. A closing gap can complicate state preparation and reduce thermal robustness. Here we identify the physical mechanism linking squeezing to collective excitations and establish when stronger squeezing accompanies a larger many-body gap. Using the anisotropic Lipkin--Meshkov--Glick model, we show that, for a quadratic collective mode, the ratio of two transverse fluctuation costs determines squeezing, whereas their geometric mean sets the collective gap. The pair terms responsible for unequal costs are allowed, but not guaranteed, by $\mathbb Z_2$ parity. To extend this picture beyond a single mode, we derive an exact relation between squeezing, polarization, and excitation energies weighted by their collective-spin transition strengths. For XXZ chains, weak-coupling and spin-wave analyses explain how squeezing can develop while both the collective and many-body gaps increase. Exact finite-size calculations for power-law interactions, including the nearest-neighbor limit, confirm a negative-coupling branch on which the two gaps coincide and increase as squeezing improves. For nearest-neighbor interactions, this branch also retains squeezing at higher temperatures than a gap-closing branch with comparable ground-state squeezing. In the Ising chain, by contrast, a staggered excitation can lower the many-body gap without directly contributing to collective fluctuations. Our results explain why ground-state squeezing can accompany either a closing or an opening collective gap, and identify a gap-opening branch with enhanced thermal robustness and the potential for faster adiabatic preparation.

</details>


### [124] [Reduced filtering using classical control hierarchy with linear optimality for continuous quantum error correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16751)
*Manuel Guatto,F. A. Cárdenas-López,Felix Motzoi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将连续量子纠错重构为经典控制论问题，提出由双线性约化模型、精确条件滤波器与标准估计/反馈设计构成的层级框架，证明精确滤波器维数仅由子空间决定（3·2^{n-k-1}），并揭示与修正反馈环的两类失效机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续量子纠错在阈值与硬件开销上优于数字范式，但复杂的模型估计严重削弱其实用性；作者希望用经典控制理论的精确约化模型替代高维量子估计，从而恢复其可实现性。

Method: 对任何所有稳定子生成元均被连续监测、且由单一Pauli恢复通道驱动的[[n,k,d]]稳定子码，构造精确双线性约化模型与精确条件滤波器，证明滤波器在3·2^{n-k-1}维闭合，且在不变量子空间滤波中为最小；据此分析并修正反馈环失效：强监测会阻尼控制器用于操纵稳定子的旋转伙伴并沿恢复路径注入反作用扩散，故在纠错阶段降低测量强度；在线性化于码空间时综合征不可控导致控制器停滞，改用偏移工作点设计的增益消除该简并；并证明零测量极限下仅综合征滤波器在每个恢复边界精确。

Result: 三量子比特码（三个被监测宇称）中，降低测量强度使伙伴操纵LQR控制的纠缠保真度提升0.096；零测量极限下的committed-recovery控制器仅需2^{n-k}类概率，在三比特比特翻转码中纠缠保真度0.947（对比基于测量的估计协议0.920），在五比特完美码中0.726（对比连续控制器至多0.407），为所测方法中最高。

Conclusion: 连续量子纠错可完全纳入经典控制论框架：精确滤波维数由综合征空间而非希尔伯特空间决定，失效机制可被诊断并修复，且committed-recovery控制器以极低存储代价取得当前最优纠缠保真度。

Abstract: Continuous quantum error correction promises reduced thresholds and hardware overhead compared to the digital paradigm, but its complex model estimation has greatly reduced its practicality. We recast this problem as a classical control-theoretic state hierarchy made of an exact bilinear reduced model, an exact conditional filter, and standard estimation and feedback design. For any $[[n,k,d]]$ stabilizer code whose generators are all continuously monitored and which is driven by a single Pauli recovery channel, we prove that the exact filter closes at dimension $3\cdot2^{\,n-k-1}$. This dimension is set by the syndrome space instead of the Hilbert space and is minimal within invariant-subspace filtering. The reduced model exposes and corrects two failure mechanisms of the feedback loop. Strong monitoring damps the rotational partner through which the controller steers the stabilizer and injects back-action diffusion along the recovery path; lowering the measurement strength during correction raises the entanglement fidelity of partner-steering LQR control by $0.096$ in the three-qubit code with three monitored parities. Linearizing at the codespace leaves the syndrome uncontrollable, so the controller can stall; a gain designed at a shifted operating point removes this degeneracy. In the limit of zero measurement during correction, we prove that a syndrome-only filter is exact at every recovery boundary. The resulting committed-recovery controller needs only $2^{\,n-k}$ class probabilities and gives the highest entanglement fidelity compared among the methods tested in the literature, $0.947$ against $0.920$ for a measurement-based estimator protocol in the three-qubit bit-flip code, and $0.726$ vs at most $0.407$ for continuous controllers in the five-qubit perfect code.

</details>


### [125] [Inter-city 18.9 km free-space QKD over a dense urban corridor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16774)
*Ignacio H. López Grande,Nicolas Linale,Giacomo Paganini,Achraf El Bouazzaoui,Adrián S. Blanca,Ilektra Karakosta-Amarantidou,Manu Canals i Codina,Adam Vallés,Andrea Gorni,Jorge Fuenzalida,Evelyn A. Ortega,Valerio Pruneri*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在巴塞罗那市区18.9 km自由空间链路上，利用符合航空规范的785 nm诱骗态BB84系统（室温单光子探测器、接收端无自适应光学）完成40分钟外场实验，平均信道衰减约19 dB、平均QBER 1.31%，估计有限码长成码率超过11 kbit/s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地面自由空间QKD可补充光纤网络，但在密集城区的长距离水平链路部署困难：大气湍流、严苛的瞄准跟踪要求与运行约束显著提升系统复杂度，需要验证简化架构在城市环境下长距离运行的可行性。

Method: 采用面向部署的模块化诱骗态BB84发射端，工作波长785 nm，接口通用自由空间光终端与独立GPS授时同步架构；接收端使用室温单光子探测器且不使用自适应光学；在城市大都会区18.9 km水平链路上开展外场测试，并基于实测信道参数估算有限码长密钥率。

Result: 40分钟外场测试中平均信道衰减约19 dB，平均QBER为1.31%，据此估计平均有限码长安全密钥率超过11 kbit/s；同时给出光源/发射端性能与光学链路特性表征。

Conclusion: 结果表明，采用相对简单、符合航空规范的架构可在密集城区实现城际自由空间QKD，验证了该方案在城市大都会环境长距离部署的可行性。

Abstract: Terrestrial free-space quantum key distribution (QKD) provides a flexible complement to fiber-based networks, but its deployment over long horizontal paths in dense metropolitan environments remains challenging, as atmospheric turbulence, demanding pointing and tracking requirements, and operational constraints can substantially increase system complexity. We experimentally demonstrate the operation of a decoy-state BB84 system over an 18.9~km free-space link across a densely populated urban area in the metropolitan region of Barcelona using an aviation-compliant optical architecture. The system operates at 785~nm using room-temperature single-photon detectors and requires no adaptive optics at the receiver. The modular QKD transmitter, based on a deployment-oriented design, is interfaced with a versatile free-space optical terminal and an independent GPS-disciplined synchronization architecture, facilitating its integration into heterogeneous optical infrastructures. We present the transmitter performance and optical-link characteristics, together with a 40-minute field test characterized by an average channel attenuation of approximately 19~dB and a mean QBER of 1.31\%. From the measured channel parameters, we estimate an average finite-size secret key rate exceeding 11~kbit/s. These results demonstrate the feasibility of inter-city free-space QKD over dense urban areas using a comparatively simple architecture.

</details>


### [126] [Quantum Keynesian Beauty Contest with Continuous Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16829)
*Hiu Ming Lau,Kareem Raslan,Kieran Hymas,Qiang Sun,Azhar Iqbal,Derek Abbott,James Q. Quach,Andrew D. Greentree*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将p-Beauty Contest博弈映射到量子连续变量线路，用增加的多模压缩实现全局变换以抑制理性boom/bust行为，并指出该效应可由经典全局中介复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典p-BC中理性玩法在p>1或p<1时分别导致boom或bust，收益结构极端化；作者希望借助量子博弈论框架考察量子过程能否缓解这种极端理性行为。

Method: 构建p-BC的量子连续变量实现：对玩家报价施加由多模压缩（multimode squeezing）生成的全局变换；对2人、3人及mean-field多玩家情形建模；推导单值测量下收益概率分布的解析形式，并与期望值测量设置对比；同时构造经典信道的全局中介版本作为对照。

Result: 增加多模压缩的全局变换可削弱boom-and-bust理性行为；相同机制可通过经典全局中介实现，说明全局中介是抑制boom/bust的博弈论核心机制；给出单值测量收益分布的解析表达式，并显示其与期望值测量下的理性行为存在差异。

Conclusion: 量子p-BC中的行为抑制并非量子独有，而源于全局中介这一博弈论机制；量子实现提供了具体手段，且测量方式（单值vs期望值）会改变所观察到的理性行为。

Abstract: The development of quantum computing has opened up opportunities to re-explore conventional game models by the integration of quantum processes. Through the established framework of quantum game theory, we examine the problem of speculative pricing in the Keynesian Beauty Contest model -- the $p$-Beauty Contest ($p$-BC). In the $p$-BC, players are rewarded with the optimal payoff for successfully bidding at some fraction $p$ of the average of all of the players' bids. Consequently, \textit{rational} plays in the $p$-BC inexorably lead to boom or bust strategies for $p>1$ or $p<1$ respectively. We demonstrate a quantum continuous-variable implementation of $p$-BC where the boom-and-bust rational behavior is mitigated through a global transformation of the players' bids generated by increasing multimode squeezing. While we focus on a quantum mechanical implementation of the $p$-BC, we show that an entirely analogous game can be formulated via classical channels by a global mediator. Thus we identify global mediation as the underlying game-theoretic mechanism for quenching boom and bust phenomena in our model. We analyze the quantum $p$-BC for two, three, and many players in a mean-field type implementation. We derive an analytical form for the probability distribution of the payoff under single-value measurements of the quantum processing circuit, and explore how the rational behavior differs from the expected-value measurement setting.

</details>


### [127] [Uncertainty-aware quantum machine learning based on Dempster-Shafer theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16903)
*Marie Kempkes,Jakob Spiegelberg,Evert van Nieuwenburg,Vedran Dunjko*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于Dempster-Shafer理论的量子分类框架，使参数化量子电路能从数据中学习标签子集上的质量函数，从而表达预测不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子机器学习工作多聚焦于模型的表达能力与平均性能（如准确率），而忽视了预测的可靠性，即模型无法表达预测不确定性。

Method: 引入Dempster-Shafer证据理论，将不确定性表示为分配给标签集合（而非单一标签）的权重；设计训练策略让参数化量子电路直接从数据学习这些分配，高效制备对应量子态，并支持多种后处理策略，包括在指数级多子集质量函数下仍具多项式资源需求的量子Dempster-Shafer融合流程。

Result: 实现了参数化量子电路对质量函数的直接学习与对应量子态的高效制备，并为量子Dempster-Shafer融合提供了资源需求为多项式的实用方案，即使质量函数定义在指数级数量的子集上。

Conclusion: 该工作使量子模型能够表达对自身预测的不确定性，将量子模型的评估从单纯的预测性能扩展到可靠性维度，是迈向不确定性感知量子分类的一步。

Abstract: A large body of work in quantum machine learning has centered on characterizing the expressivity and performance of quantum models. In contrast, little attention has been paid to the reliability of predictions beyond average performance measures such as accuracy. In this work, we introduce an uncertainty-aware quantum classification framework based on Dempster-Shafer theory. This theory allows to express uncertainty through weights assigned not only to individual classification labels but to sets of labels. We introduce a training strategy that enables parameterized quantum circuits to learn such assignments directly from data. This provides an efficient way to prepare the corresponding quantum states and enables the application of different post-processing strategies. In particular, it offers a practical setting for quantum Dempster-Shafer fusion procedures with polynomial resource requirements, even when the corresponding mass functions are defined over exponentially many subsets. Overall, our work represents a step toward enabling quantum models to express uncertainty about their predictions, thereby extending the evaluation of quantum models beyond predictive performance alone.

</details>


### [128] [Symmetry-Controlled Criticality and Zeno Localization in a Monitored Quasiperiodic Superconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16912)
*Pinaki Singha,Marcin Szyniszewski,Nilanjan Roy,Auditya Sharma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究连续监测下带配对项的自由费米子模型，揭示准周期性同时调控局域化与监测场论对称类，发现从$\sim(\ln L)^2$纠缠相到面积律相的转变及多重分形临界线，并在Zeno区给出非厄米BdG有效描述与闭式局域化长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量诱导转变强烈依赖对称性，但其与准周期局域化、超导配对的相互作用尚未被探索；作者希望厘清对称性、准周期局域化与测量反作用如何共同决定监测超导体中的临界纠缠与微观轨迹局域化。

Method: 构建带配对项的连续监测自由费米子模型，准周期性同时控制局域化并改变监测场论的对称类；以拓扑纠缠熵作为量子态扩散模拟中非局域关联的探针；与DIII、D类非线性sigma模型对照；在深度Zeno区导出单条轨迹的有效非厄米Bogoliubov–de Gennes描述并解析求得局域化长度。

Result: 识别出临界相（von Neumann纠缠熵按$(\ln L)^2$标度）与面积律相之间的转变，而干净极限下无转变，与DIII和D类sigma模型一致；转变线表现出强多重分形性，且非单调依赖准周期强度，作者将其归因于对称类交叉；Zeno区闭式局域化长度与数值结果定量吻合。

Conclusion: 对称性、准周期局域化与测量反作用的结合决定了监测超导体中的临界纠缠行为与微观轨迹局域化，为测量诱导相变与准周期物理的交叉提供了统一图像。

Abstract: Measurement-induced transitions of free fermions depend strongly on symmetry, but their interplay with quasiperiodic localization and superconducting pairing remains largely unexplored. We study a continuously monitored model of free fermions with pairing, where quasiperiodicity both controls localization and changes the symmetry class of the monitored field theory. Using topological entanglement entropy as a probe of nonlocal correlations in quantum state diffusion simulations, we identify a transition between a critical phase whose von Neumann entanglement entropy scales as $(\ln L)^2$ and an area-law phase, whereas no transition occurs in the clean limit, consistent with the corresponding DIII and D nonlinear sigma models. The transition line exhibits strong multifractality and depends nonmonotonically on quasiperiodicity, which we associate with the symmetry crossover. Deep in the Zeno regime, we derive an effective non-Hermitian Bogoliubov-de Gennes description of individual trajectories and obtain a closed-form localization length that quantitatively agrees with numerics. Our results reveal how symmetry, quasiperiodic localization, and measurement backaction combine to determine both critical entanglement and microscopic trajectory localization in monitored superconductors.

</details>


### [129] [When can local unitaries generate a complete orthogonal basis?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16921)
*Julian Eisfeld,Hermann Kampermann,Nikolai Wyderka*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文解决了两量子三态系统（3×3）中存在通用局域可编码性（ULE）的公开猜想，并对一般维数系统给出构造与排除条件，还赋予ULE操作意义（等价于一般测量相对投影可模拟测量的判别优势）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局域可编码性问：给定多体纯态能否仅用局域幺正变换补全为正交基。若某系统所有态都具备此性质则称ULE，其在量子信息、工程与数学中有应用。该问题答案依赖局域维数：已证2×2、4×4、8×8成立，3×3仅为猜想，四比特及以上由Pauwels与Gühne证明非ULE。作者旨在解决3×3猜想、扩展维数情形的结论，并给出操作解释。

Method: 证明两qutrit（3×3）的通用性以解决猜想；构造多类系统中通用局域编码（d×2, d≥2；d×3, d=3,4及d≥6；含一或两个无限维子系统的任意二分系统）；将启发式参数计数严格化为有限维多体系统通用性的必要条件，用以排除无穷多情形；并建立非ULE与"一般测量相对投影可模拟测量在检测未知随机局域幺正旋转任务中的优势"之间的等价关系。

Result: 证实3×3系统为ULE并解决该猜想；在d×2（d≥2）、d×3（d=3,4及d≥6）以及含一或两个无限维子系统的二分系统中给出通用局域编码构造；导出通用性的必要条件，从而排除无穷多有限维多体情形的ULE；证明非ULE等价于一般测量在检测随机局域幺正旋转任务中对投影可模拟测量的优势。

Conclusion: 局域可编码性的维数依赖结构被大幅厘清：3×3猜想成立，通用性在多类系统（含无限维）可实现，且存在无穷多被严格排除的有限维多体情形；ULE由此获得操作解释，与测量判别优势紧密关联。

Abstract: The question of local encodability asks: can a given, multipartite pure quantum state be completed to an orthonormal basis using only local unitary rotations? If any such state in a given system has this property, it is called universally locally encodable (ULE), a feature, which has applications in quantum information theory, engineering and math. Despite its easy formulation, the answer to this question depends nontrivially on the local dimensions of the system: While universal local encodability is known for $2\times 2$, $4\times 4$ and $8\times 8$-dimensional systems, it was so-far only conjectured to hold in $3\times 3$, and non-ULE was recently proved for systems of four and more qubits by Pauwels and Gühne.
  We resolve the open conjecture by proving universality for two qutrits. Furthermore, we construct (among others) universal local encodings in dimensions $d\times2$ for $d\ge2$, in dimensions $d\times3$ for $d=3,4$ and $d\ge6$, and in every bipartite system with one or two infinite-dimensional subsystems. For finite-dimensional multipartite systems, we turn a heuristic parameter count into a necessary condition for universality, which excludes universal local encodability in infinitely many cases. Finally, we give universal local encodability an operational interpretation by showing that non-ULE is equivalent to whether general measurements yield an advantage over projectively simulable ones in discrimination tasks where unknown, random local unitary rotations have to be detected.

</details>


### [130] [Communication Supremacy of a Qubit in Holevo Frenkel Weiner Scenario](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16940)
*Partha Patra,Sumit Mukherjee,A. K. Pan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在不注入纠缠或测量不相容等额外量子资源、且共享随机性自由可用的前提下，本文证明 Holevo–Frenkel–Weiner（HFW）框架中量子比特通信相对经典比特通信具有优势：发送方准备满足特定密码学约束的六个态，接收方执行三结果测量，该约束对两类通信的能力影响不同，从而产生量子优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流观点认为，在 HFW 通信任务框架下，除非提供额外量子资源或限制经典共享随机性（SR），量子比特相对经典比特没有优势。作者希望挑战这一"共识"，在额外量子资源不注入、SR 免费的情形下寻找量子比特通信的优越性。

Method: 考虑一个 prepare-measure 场景：Alice 准备受特定密码学约束的六个量子态，Bob 执行三结果测量；构造一个 witness（见证量）用于区分超出经典策略集合的量子策略，并推导其最优量子优势；进一步分析该 witness 在噪声情形下的鲁棒性；并研究 Bob 的三结果 qubit POVM 的相干性探测能力。

Result: 所构造的 witness 能够区分量子策略与经典策略集合，并给出最优量子优势；该优势在含噪条件下依然存在，具备实验可行性；同时证明 Bob 的三结果 qubit POVM 具有独特的相干性探测能力。

Conclusion: 在 HFW 框架中，即便 SR 免费且无额外量子资源，受约束的态准备与三结果 qubit 测量仍可带来量子比特通信优势；该优势可无种子地生成认证随机性（Bob 无输入），且具噪声鲁棒性，其非经典来源可归因于三结果 POVM 对相干性的探测能力。

Abstract: The prevailing wisdom asserts that in the Holevo Frenkel Weiner (HFW) framework of communication tasks, a qubit offers no advantage over a c bit unless additional quantum resources are supplied or the classical shared randomness (SR) is restricted. In contrast, we establish the supremacy of qubit communication in the HFW framework when additional quantum resources, such as entanglement or measurement incompatibility are not injected and SR is a free resource. We consider a prepare-measure scenario where the sender, Alice, prepares six states subject to a specific cryptographic constraint, and the receiver, Bob, performs a three-outcome measurement. Such a constraint on preparation impacts the power of qubit communication quite differently than that of c-bit communication, ultimately giving rise to the quantum advantage. We introduce a witness capable of distinguishing quantum strategies that lie beyond the set of possible classical strategies and demonstrate the optimal quantum advantage. {As an application, we demonstrate the generation of certified randomness without requiring any seed randomness, since Bob has no input.} We further analyse the robustness of the witness and show that the advantage persists in noisy situations, making it suitable for experimental realisation. Finally, we demonstrate that Bob's three outcome qubit positive operator valued-measure (POVMs) has a distinct capability of detecting coherence, which can be regarded as the non-classical feature responsible for showcasing the quantum advantage.

</details>


### [131] [Unified master equation for driven multilevel quantum systems: Derivation, dynamics, and spectral signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16952)
*Sagarika Basak,D. Blume*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 常规主方程无法准确刻画多能级量子系统中的驱动修正耗散；作者基于Lewis-Riesenfeld动力学不变量，在Born-Markov理论框架内推导出可处理任意驱动的统一主方程，并以三能级Λ系统的荧光谱揭示常规描述所遗漏的实验可及特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统主方程对驱动下的耗散描述依赖对浴频率的采样方式，且忽略非世俗耗散耦合，导致多能级系统在连续波、周期调制及非周期（ramped、chirped）驱动下的耗散动力学建模不可靠，需要统一处理框架并明确其适用范围。

Method: 利用Lewis-Riesenfeld动力学不变量，在Born-Markov理论内推导适用于任意驱动的统一主方程；并将其应用于恒Rabi耦合（连续波）、周期调制Rabi耦合以及非周期（ramped与chirped）驱动协议；以周期驱动三能级Λ系统的荧光谱作为诊断量，对比常规主方程的预测，并将偏差归因于浴频率采样不准确与非世俗耗散耦合的忽略，进而标定需要统一处理的参数区域。

Result: 统一主方程可覆盖上述多种驱动协议；周期驱动Λ系统的荧光谱出现常规描述所缺失的特征，这些差异可追溯至浴频率采样误差与非世俗耗散耦合的遗漏，并给出需采用统一处理的参数范围；荧光谱为实验可及的谱学判据。

Conclusion: 驱动修正耗散须以统一主方程描述；荧光谱可作为检验耗散建模可靠性的谱学指南，为量子控制、量子模拟、量子传感与量子信息处理提供可靠建模依据。

Abstract: Drive-modified dissipation in multilevel quantum systems is not, in general, accurately captured by conventional master equations. We derive a unified master equation using Lewis-Riesenfeld dynamical invariants, accommodating arbitrary driving within Born-Markov theory. We demonstrate the applicability of the unified master equation to constant-Rabi-coupling (continuous-wave), periodically-modulated-Rabi-coupling, and nonperiodic (ramped and chirped) driving protocols. Fluorescence spectra of a periodically driven three-level $Λ$ system reveal signatures missed by conventional descriptions. We trace the limitations of the conventional descriptions to inaccurate bath-frequency sampling and the neglect of nonsecular dissipative couplings, and map out the parameter regimes that require a unified treatment. The fluorescence spectra provide experimentally accessible signatures, which serve as a spectroscopic guide to reliable modeling of dissipative processes that can inform quantum control, simulation, sensing, and information processing.

</details>


### [132] [Higher-Group Statistics on Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16962)
*Chen Yang,Yu-An Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个格点框架，将高阶群统计定义为闭合跃迁算符序列的Berry相位，并给出两种构造统计过程的方法，证明球面上统计过程由H_{d+2}(B𝔾,ℤ)分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同维度缺陷之间的耦合（畴壁置换任意子、弦相交产生粒子）由高阶群描述，但缺乏一个统一、可计算且能刻画更精细相位的统计框架，尤其是需要超越通常±1不变量的更细判别量。

Method: 以闭合跃迁算符序列的Berry相位（对算符局域修改不变）定义高阶群统计；核心例子为嵌入3维的闭合Majorana链，构造相位为+i的loop-flip过程；在2维用charge-wall统计区分两种ℤ4 toric code；方法一将Smith标准形算法推广到高阶群，由格点几何与给定局域移动生成过程；方法二直接由同调类构造过程，避免枚举指数多缺陷构型。

Result: 构造出相位+i的loop-flip，通过链与物理费米子间费米宇称的中间转移细化了±1不变量；证明有限高阶群𝔾在d-球面上的统计过程由H_{d+2}(B𝔾,ℤ)分类；区分了任意子统计相同但规范理论不同（D4与Q8量子双重）的两种ℤ2电荷共轭富化ℤ4 toric code；在1、2、3维区分2-group反常。

Conclusion: 高阶群统计为刻画量子多体系统中耦合缺陷提供了新不变量，可作为高阶群对称保护拓扑相边界反常的探测工具。

Abstract: Crossing a domain wall can permute anyons, and intersecting strings can create a particle. These couplings between defects of various dimensions are specified by higher groups. We develop a lattice framework defining higher-group statistics as Berry phases of closed sequences of hopping operators, invariant under local modifications of the operators. Our central example is a closed Majorana chain, a 1-dimensional topological superconductor, embedded in 3 dimensions. We construct a loop-flip process with phase $+i$, refining the usual $\pm 1$ loop-flip invariant through intermediate transfer of fermion parity between the chain and physical fermions. In two dimensions, charge-wall statistics distinguish two $\mathbb Z_4$ toric codes enriched by $\mathbb Z_2$ charge-conjugation symmetry, with identical anyon statistics but distinct gauged theories: the quantum doubles of the dihedral group $D_4$ and the quaternion group $Q_8$. We provide two complementary constructions of statistical processes. The first extends the Smith normal form algorithm in [Phys. Rev. X $\textbf{16}$, 011010 (2026), arXiv:2412.01886] to higher groups, generating processes from lattice geometry and prescribed local moves. For a finite higher group $\mathbb G$, we prove that statistical processes on a $d$-sphere are classified by $H_{d+2}(B\mathbb G,\mathbb Z)$. The second constructs processes directly from homology classes without examining exponentially many defect configurations, enabling us to study statistics for complex higher-group structures where the previous method is computationally prohibitive. These processes detect boundary anomalies of higher-group symmetry-protected topological phases. For example, we distinguish 2-group anomalies in 1, 2, and 3 dimensions. Higher-group statistics provide new invariants for characterizing coupled defects in quantum many-body systems.

</details>


### [133] [Improved Low-Cost Quantum Error Mitigation with Benchmarked-Noise Zero-Noise Extrapolation (bnZNE)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16971)
*Joseph Harris,Kevin Lively,Peter Ken Schuhmacher*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出并实验验证了基准噪声零噪声外推（bnZNE）：用由应用电路生成的可经典模拟、结构镜像的基准电路，在每个噪声缩放级别上灵敏测量有效错误率（可为非整数），改进ZNE的经典外推步骤，在同等总采样成本下比标准ZNE更精确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准ZNE在同等总采样成本下精度受限，其外推步骤依赖噪声缩放级别的有效错误率估计，而该估计通常较粗糙。作者希望以低成本改进经典外推环节，从而提升误差缓解精度。

Method: 理论推广并实验演示bnZNE；提出randomized bnZNE以处理每个噪声级别运行多个随机电路的情形，从而可结合Pauli twirling与读出误差缓解等噪声定制技术；使用从标准应用电路衍生的可经典模拟、结构镜像的基准电路，灵敏测量各缩放级别的有效错误率（超越整数取值）；并给出针对IQM超导器件定制基准电路的新生成方法，在IBM与IQM硬件上对多类大规模应用进行测试。

Result: 在门级去极化噪声下bnZNE可达到与标准ZNE同阶的缓解效果；在IBM与IQM硬件上的大量大规模应用中，bnZNE一致地比标准ZNE产生更准确的结果。

Conclusion: bnZNE是ZNE的一种通用替代方案，能在等效成本下于utility规模实现更准确的结果，可作为有前景的误差缓解方法推广使用。

Abstract: We theoretically extend and experimentally demonstrate benchmarked-noise zero-noise extrapolation (bnZNE), a simple and low-cost method for quantum error mitigation which aims to outperform the highly popular ZNE method for an equal total shot cost by improving the classical extrapolation step. We recently proposed bnZNE as a new error mitigation method (Harris et al., arXiv:2603.10224 (2026)) and showed that it can mitigate noise to the same order as standard ZNE under gate-wise depolarizing noise. Here, we introduce randomized bnZNE which addresses the case of running multiple random circuits per noise level, allowing for the incorporation of noise-tailoring techniques like Pauli twirling and readout error mitigation, and test the method on the IBM and IQM hardware for a plethora of large-scale applications, observing bnZNE to consistently produce more accurate results than standard ZNE. Our approach enhances the extrapolation process to the zero-noise limit by using classically-simulable and structure-mirroring benchmark circuits, generated from the standard application circuit, to sensitively measure the effective error rate at each scaled noise level beyond simple integer values. We give a new method for generating tailored benchmark circuits for the IQM superconducting devices and test them experimentally. Our results demonstrate that bnZNE presents a versatile alternative to ZNE which can enable more accurate results at utility scales with an equivalent cost.

</details>


### [134] [Entanglement detection from moments of the realigned state: application to continuous-variable quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17021)
*Serge Deside,Vojtěch Kala,Nicolas J. Cerf*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文基于将密度算符经实对齐映射（realignment map）后所得算符的奇异矩与弱矩，提出一族连续变量纠缠判据，并指出二阶实对齐矩已足以判据多类量子光学态的纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续变量量子系统的纠缠验证通常依赖全态层析，实验代价高；已有部分转置矩方法虽可多拷贝测量，但需要可替代的、易获取的判据族。

Method: 对密度算符施加实对齐映射，构造基于映射后算符奇异矩与弱矩的可分离性判据；用耦合若干相同态拷贝的线性干涉仪测量这些实对齐矩，类比多拷贝测部分转置矩的方案；重点采用二阶实对齐矩。

Result: 二阶实对齐矩判据即可见证广泛量子光学态的纠缠；对高斯与非高斯态（含损耗NOON态）均展示出检测能力，且矩可由线性干涉仪多拷贝光学方案直接获取。

Conclusion: 实对齐映射矩提供了一族实验可行、以二阶矩为主的连续变量纠缠判据，拓展了多拷贝测量技术在纠缠检测中的适用范围。

Abstract: Certifying entanglement is a central problem in quantum information sciences, especially for continuous-variable quantum systems where full state tomography is experimentally demanding. We introduce a family of separability criteria based on the singular moments and weak moments of the operator obtained by applying the realignment map to the density operator. In practice, we find that the criterion based on the second-order moments of the realigned operator is already sufficient to witness the entanglement of a wide range of quantum optical states. The realigned moments can be readily accessed using an optical scheme consisting of a linear interferometer that couples several identical copies of the state, similarly to the multicopy technique used to measure the partial-transpose moments of a state [Phys. Rev. Research 8, 033064 (2026)]. We illustrate the detection capability of the second-order realigned-moment method for both Gaussian and non-Gaussian states, including lossy NOON states.

</details>


### [135] [Ergodicity breaking for data classification in a dissipative Ising model with competing two- and four-body interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17036)
*Simon Kochsiek,Mario Boneberg,Igor Lesanovsky*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文揭示了数据分类与耗散多体量子动力学之间的内在联系：可将量子神经网络映射为Lindblad型耗散量子Ising模型，利用非遍历动力学中不同吸引盆收敛到可区分稳态的机制实现量子态分类，并证明含二体与四体竞争的Ising模型可通过稳态流形设计实现多类分类及非线性决策边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子数据处理与多体系统动力学之间存在深刻关联，量子神经网络可映射为耗散量子Ising模型；作者希望借助这一对应关系，从多体动力学的角度理解并设计量子数据的分类机制，回答"物理动力学如何充当量子分类器"这一问题。

Method: 建立量子神经网络与Lindblad主方程描述的耗散量子Ising模型之间的映射；考虑同时含二体与四体竞争相互作用的Ising模型；采用平均场计算与精确小尺度数值模拟相结合的方法，分析其稳态流形结构与吸引盆结构。

Result: 非平衡非线性引发的非遍历动力学使初始位于不同吸引盆的量子态演化至可区分的稳态；通过设计稳态流形（稳态集合的数目与分布），可支持多类分类；对吸引盆结构的分析表明该系统能够以非线性决策边界对量子态进行分类。

Conclusion: 耗散多体动力学的吸引子结构可作为量子数据分类的物理资源；含竞争二体/四体相互作用的Ising模型为多类量子分类及非线性决策边界提供了可设计的平台，将量子机器学习与多体非遍历物理统一起来。

Abstract: We explore the close relationship between the processing of data and the dynamics of interacting many-body systems. For example, certain classes of quantum neural networks can be mapped onto dissipative quantum Ising models whose evolution is described by a Lindblad master equation. In these architectures, emergent nonlinearities lead to non-ergodic dynamics under which initial states located in different basins of attraction evolve towards distinguishable stationary states. This mechanism underlies the classification of quantum data. Motivated by this connection, we consider an Ising model with competing two- and four-body interactions. Using a combination of mean-field calculations and exact small-scale numerics, we show that the stationary state manifold can be designed to enable multi-class classification. We also investigate the structure of the ensuing basins of attraction, thereby showing that these systems allow for the classification of quantum states with nonlinear decision boundaries.

</details>


### [136] [Physical Observables and Superselection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17051)
*Martin Paul Vaughan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从"何为物理"的角度综述超选择规则（superselection rule）的概念：它限制哪些自伴算子可代表可观测量，并进一步约束状态与物理过程；同时讨论由电荷或能量守恒导致的超选择规则，以及退相干或缺乏共享参考系所诱导的有效超选择规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子力学中状态为Hilbert空间单位向量、可观测量的自伴算子，但超选择规则的存在打破了这一朴素对应关系；作者希望从"超选择规则如何影响我们对物理的界定"这一视角系统梳理该概念及其成因。

Method: 综述性分析：从概念层面梳理超选择规则对可观测量集合的限制及其对状态与物理变换过程的含义；论证基于电荷守恒（可能还有能量守恒）的超选择规则的合理性；并讨论由退相干或缺少共享参考系所导致的有效超选择规则。

Result: 综述表明：超选择规则使Hilbert空间分解为超选择扇区，并非所有自伴算子都是物理可观测量，状态与变换亦受相应约束；除电荷等守恒量给出的严格规则外，退相干与无共享参考系可产生有效超选择规则。

Conclusion: 超选择规则是界定"物理上可实现"的可观测量、状态与操作的重要结构；对其概念的清晰梳理有助于统一理解守恒律起源与退相干/参考系起源两类超选择现象。

Abstract: It is well-known that a physical state may be represented by a unit vector in a Hilbert space, whilst a physical observable may be represented by a self-adjoint operator on that same space.
