# physics.optics [[Back]](#toc)

### [151] [AI-assisted search for self-compressing gigawatt subcycle field waveforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14672)
*Aleksei M. Zheltikov,Youchan Park,Petr Zhokhov,Julia M. Mikhailova*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 论文研究充气空芯光纤中多模超短脉冲的非线性动力学，实现脉冲自压缩至高功率亚周期光场波形，并借助生成式预训练Transformer加速最优压缩区间的参数搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索脉冲自压缩至高功率亚周期场波形背后的物理机制，并在多模非线性动力学参数空间中寻找最优压缩区制；同时解决传统参数扫描中前期搜索极其繁琐、耗时的问题。

Method: 将脉冲对比度和"无脉冲分裂"等物理判据形式化为可操作的停止规则（stopping rules）；把搜索的繁琐前期阶段委托给生成式预训练Transformer模型以大幅提速；对压缩动力学的精细调节则依赖对场相位敏感的非线性过程的调控。

Result: 形式化停止规则结合GPT模型使参数搜索效率显著提升，实现搜索速度的大幅加快；但针对理想压缩脉冲输出的精确自压缩动力学调整仍无法完全交由AI代理完成，因为相关判据仍高度主观，涉及亚周期场结构的细微特征。

Conclusion: 通过场相位敏感非线性过程的调控，可实现亚周期光场工程的最终精度，把脉冲自压缩推进到高功率亚周期场波形，并为光纤式阿秒光波工程开辟路径。

Abstract: We explore the physics behind pulse self-compression to high-power subcycle field waveforms and search for the optimal regimes of such compression over the parameter space as laid out by multimode nonlinear dynamics of ultrashort laser pulses in gas-filled hollow fibers. With the pertinent physical criteria for pulse contrast and no pulse breakup formalized as operational stopping rules, a radical speedup of such search is achieved by delegating its tedious preliminary stages to generative pretrained transformer models. The precise adjustment of self-compression dynamics vis-a-vis the desired compressed pulse output is, however, not yet delegable to AI agents, as the criteria for such search are still largely subjective, operating with fine aspects of subcycle field structure, not yet formalizable as clear search-stopping instructions. An ultimate precision in such subcycle field engineering is attainable via a management of field-phase-sensitive nonlinear processes, enabling pulse self-compression to high-power subcycle field waveforms and opening paths toward fiber-optic attosecond lightwave engineering.

</details>


### [152] [High-directivity pulsed beams for effective power and information transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14860)
*Richard W. Ziolkowski*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文用矢量球面波展开构造超指向性、极单向（SDEU）针状波束的基元多极源，并证明沿轴方向达到Harrington正常指向性极限、后瓣在远场恒为零；该性能可被均匀圆阵继承，并用零面积脉冲（ZAP）无缝移植到时间域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为波束（能量聚焦）的宽窄主要由辐射口径尺寸决定；作者希望以严格解析方式构造一个既达到指向性极限又完全单向的基元源，并探究在脉冲驱动下决定能量束宽窄的真正设计变量，从而提升局域电磁能量与信息投递能力。

Method: 基于矢量球面波展开（VSWE）设计基元多极源，使电、磁多极在反方向逐阶精确抵消，得到闭合形式的前后比；将单元组成均匀圆阵（UCA），以零面积脉冲（ZAP，如单周期ZAP）驱动，把频域性能完整映射到时间域；提出判据说明能量束与强度束孰窄由辐射波形的单一属性决定；并设计强度增强型（IE）ZAP，用脉冲激励的Huygens偶极子阵列实现近场聚焦。

Result: 解析证明并经数值验证：单元沿波束轴达到Harrington正常指向性值，辐射场线极化、幅度方位对称、远场后瓣恒为零；获得任意有限远场半径下的闭式前后比。UCA继承SDEU特性；单周期ZAP的能量束更窄。定制IE-ZAP使焦区强度较窄带参考提升近200倍，焦斑在轴向与横向均缩小十倍以上，相对自身有效波长远小于波长。

Conclusion: 脉冲波形而非辐射口径，才是控制局域电磁能量与信息投递的设计变量；仅用脉冲激励的Huygens偶极子阵列即可实现深度亚波长的强度增强聚焦。

Abstract: A vector spherical wave expansion (VSWE) is used to engineer an elemental multipole source whose radiated field is a superdirective, extremely unidirectional (SDEU) needle beam. It is proved analytically and confirmed numerically that the element attains Harrington's normal-directivity value along its beam axis. Its radiated field is linearly polarized with an azimuthally symmetric magnitude; its back lobe vanishes identically in the far field. The unidirectional behavior is quantified at any finite far-field radius by a closed-form front-to-back ratio, obtained from an exact per-degree cancellation of the electric and magnetic multipole contributions in the backward direction. Uniform circular arrays (UCAs) of these elements inherit the SDEU behavior. Driving the arrays with zero-area pulses (ZAPs) transfers the frequency-domain performance intact into the time domain. It is shown that whether the radiated energy beam is narrower or wider than the corresponding intensity beam is decided by a single property of the radiated waveform, not by the size of the radiating aperture. A simple single-cycle ZAP is used to quantify the performance of the UCAs; its energy beam is the narrower one. The same criterion is shown to govern the focal spot of near-field-focused pulse-driven arrays. A custom-designed intensity-enhancement (IE) ZAP is introduced that raises the focal intensity by almost 200-fold over a narrow-band reference while producing a focal spot more than ten times smaller in both the axial and transverse directions, deeply subwavelength with respect to its own effective wavelength. It is demonstrated that these stunning IE results are produced simply with an array of pulse-excited Huygens dipole sources. These results identify the pulse shape, rather than the aperture, as the design variable that governs localized electromagnetic energy and information delivery.

</details>


### [153] [A Field-Deployable 2U Ultra-Stable Laser with Sub-Hertz Linewidth Based on Optoelectronic Oscillator Locking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15005)
*Dongdong Jiao,Linbo Zhang,Xue Deng,Jing Gao,Yue Gao,Mengfan Wu,Qi Zang,Guanjun Xu,Tao Liu*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 报道了封装于标准2U机箱的最紧凑现场可部署超稳激光系统，采用光电振荡器（OEO）锁定50 mm长、19 mL低振动敏感度参考腔，积分线宽约0.42 Hz、1 s稳定度约5E-15，体积仅为同类可搬运超稳激光的四分之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验室内超稳激光性能优异但体积庞大，而能在实验室外运行的小型化、高性能超稳激光长期是未实现的目标；现有可搬运方案体积仍偏大，难以满足现场时间频率计量、量子技术与环境传感需求。

Method: 采用紧凑化设计：50 mm长、19 mL的微型光学参考腔，具备低振动敏感度；以光电振荡器（OEO）锁定方式实现激光稳频，该方法结构简单、适合小型可搬运系统；整体封装于标准2U机架。

Result: 积分线宽约0.42 Hz；分数频率稳定度1 s约5E-15（0.4 s约2.2E-15）；100 Hz以下相位噪声接近腔热噪声极限，100 Hz–100 kHz接近OEO锁定理论极限；系统体积约为已报道同性能集成可搬运超稳激光的四分之一。

Conclusion: 该工作将可现场部署超稳激光的体积显著压缩而不牺牲性能，为时间频率计量、量子技术及环境传感等现场应用提供了实用化平台。

Abstract: Compact, high-performance ultra-stable lasers that can operate outside the laboratory remain a long-standing goal. Here we report, to our knowledge, the most compact field-deployable ultra-stable laser, packaged in a standard 2U rack chassis. It is stabilized to a 50 mm-long, 19 mL optical reference cavity with low vibration sensitivity by optoelectronic oscillator (OEO) locking, an approach particularly suited to compact, transportable systems. The laser delivers an integrated linewidth of about 0.42 Hz and a fractional frequency stability of about 5E-15 at 1 s averaging time (about 2.2E-15 at 0.4 s). Measured phase noise approaches the cavity thermal-noise limit below 100 Hz and the theoretical OEO-locking limit from 100 Hz to 100 kHz. The system volume is roughly one quarter of the volume of previously reported integrated, transportable ultra-stable lasers of comparable performance, making it well suited for field applications in time-frequency metrology, quantum technologies, and environmental sensing.

</details>


### [154] [Analytic T-Matrix Formalism for Helmholtz Resonators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15208)
*Egor V. Domoratskii,Polina A. Medvedeva,Dmitrii N. Maksimov,Lana A. Glazko,Alexey A. Proskurin,Nikolay S. Solodovchenko,Mingzhao Song,Yong Li,Mihail I. Petrov,Andrey A. Bogdanov*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出二维圆形声学Helmholtz共振腔（薄刚性壁）的解析T矩阵形式，基于孔径投影算子与孔径-傅里叶映射，可处理任意角度宽度与位置的多孔径，并用于分析Willis耦合、方向依赖吸收与多极紧凑表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对带孔径共振腔的散射分析缺乏紧凑、可解析且能显式刻画外部场与腔模耦合/解耦的T矩阵描述，尤其在多孔径、非对称结构中难以识别Willis耦合等通道并实现快速收敛计算。

Method: 引入孔径投影算子，导出含显式耦合系数的紧凑T矩阵；构造孔径-傅里叶映射与边缘自适应基以加速收敛；方法推广至任意角宽与位置的多孔径；将T矩阵按反演对称分解为偶/奇部分，并结合内部损耗分析互易性（相反入射消光相等）与原点平移对多极通道的再分配。

Result: 散射谱与全波数值模拟一致；单/双孔径情形下通过偶奇分解识别Willis耦合相关通道；证明破缺反演对称且有内部损耗时可实现方向依赖的散射与吸收，而互易性保证正反入射消光相等；平移展开原点可重分配辐射至不同多极通道，从而获得更紧凑的T矩阵表示。

Conclusion: 该解析T矩阵框架为多孔径Helmholtz共振腔提供高效、物理透明的散射描述，是分析Willis耦合与方向性吸收/散射的有力工具。

Abstract: We develop an analytic T-matrix formalism for two-dimensional circular acoustic Helmholtz resonators with thin rigid walls. The central idea is to use an aperture projection operator to obtain a compact expression for the T-matrix with explicit coefficients describing coupling and decoupling between external fields and cavity resonances. An equivalent formulation providing much faster convergence uses an aperture-to-Fourier map and an edge-adapted basis for the aperture flux. The method extends directly to multiple apertures of arbitrary angular widths and positions. The calculated scattering spectra agree with full-wave numerical simulations. For single- and double-aperture resonators, we decompose the T-matrix into even and odd parts under inversion to identify the channels associated with Willis coupling. We show how internal losses enable direction-dependent scattering and absorption when inversion symmetry is broken, while reciprocity ensures equal extinction for opposite incidence. Finally, we show how shifting the expansion origin redistributes radiation among multipole channels, enabling more compact T-matrix representations.

</details>


### [155] [Ultrahigh-speed digital holography reveals blood flow beneath the skin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15535)
*Yann Fischer,Zacharie Auray,Maxime Boy-Arnould,Michael Atlan*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 激光多普勒全息(LDH)以约16,000帧/秒、10 mW漫射近红外照明记录，经菲涅尔数值重建与主成分滤波，可在闭合眼睑皮肤下及眼周显示分支血管血流信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有眼内成像系统难以在不进行复杂固定(制动)程序的情况下观察闭合眼睑下及眼周的血流，作者希望将LDH扩展至皮肤下血流可视化。

Method: 超高速数字全息：单名志愿者，约16,000帧/秒记录，10 mW漫射近红外照明；采用菲涅尔数值重建与主成分分析(PCA)滤波提取血流信号。

Result: 重建结果清晰显示出分支状血管网及血流信号，验证了LDH在皮肤下血流成像中的可行性。

Conclusion: LDH可将眼成像系统拓展至无需复杂固定程序的皮下血流可视化，但样本仅一人，属原理性验证。

Abstract: Laser Doppler holography (LDH) leverages ultrahigh-speed digital holography to reveal blood-flow signals in vessels beneath the skin of the closed eyelid and around the eye. We demonstrate this in one volunteer using recordings acquired at approximately 16,000 frames/s under 10~mW of diffuse near-infrared illumination. Numerical Fresnel reconstruction and principal-component filtering reveal branching vascular patterns. These results extend an ocular imaging system to blood-flow visualization beneath the skin without requiring elaborate immobilization procedures.

</details>


### [156] [Laser-induced damage threshold of periodic plasmonic metasurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15590)
*Sebastian Beer,Amit Shaha Surja,Jeetendra Gour,Alessandro Alberucci,Uwe D. Zeitner,Stefan Nolte*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该论文实验与数值研究了周期性金纳米棒超表面中表面晶格共振（SLR）、二次谐波产生（SHG）与激光损伤阈值（LIDT）之间的相互作用，揭示了非线性效率与光学鲁棒性之间的固有取舍，并给出高功率非线性等离激元超表面的设计准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等离激元超表面凭借亚波长场局域可产生强非线性响应，但其性能受制于较低的激光损伤阈值（LIDT）。作者希望厘清SLR激发位置（基频或二次谐波波长）对SHG效率与损伤阈值的不同影响，从而指导高性能器件的设计。

Method: 以周期性金纳米棒超表面为对象，开展角度分辨实验测量并结合数值模拟，分别研究基频波长处的SLR与二次谐波波长处的SLR对SHG效率及LIDT的作用。

Result: 激发位于基频波长的SLR会显著增强SHG效率，但同时降低LIDT；而激发位于二次谐波波长的SLR则不影响损伤阈值，从而使SHG信号得以最大化。

Conclusion: 研究确立了非线性效率与光学鲁棒性之间的基本权衡关系，并为优化周期性等离激元超表面以用于高功率非线性应用提供了设计准则。

Abstract: Plasmonic metasurfaces enable strong nonlinear optical responses due to sub-wavelength field confinement, yet their performance is fundamentally limited by low laser-induced damage thresholds (LIDT). Here, we present a comprehensive experimental and numerical study of the interplay between surface lattice resonances (SLRs), second-harmonic generation (SHG), and LIDT in periodic gold nanobar metasurfaces. Angle-resolved measurements reveal that excitation of SLRs at the fundamental wavelength strongly enhances SHG efficiency while simultaneously reducing the LIDT. In contrast, excitation of an SLR at the second-harmonic wavelength does not affect the damage threshold, allowing the SHG signal to be maximized. Our findings establish a fundamental trade-off between nonlinear efficiency and optical robustness and provide design guidelines for optimizing periodic plasmonic metasurfaces for high-power nonlinear applications.

</details>


### [157] [Gradient-Free Inverse Design of 3D Nanophotonic Devices Using the Level-Set-Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15656)
*Oliver Kuster,Carsten Rockstuhl,Thomas Jebb Sturges*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 将无梯度优化与水平集方法(LSM)结合，用高斯径向基函数构造紧凑水平集表示，实现在严格施加二值化制造约束（如结构连通性）下对全三维纳米光子器件进行逆向设计，并以Y分支器为例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D纳米光子增材制造使器件设计空间扩展到三维，引入额外自由度；利用这些自由度需逆向设计方法能高效搜索大设计空间并满足制造约束。基于伴随梯度的优化难以处理不可微或导数不可得的二值制造约束（如连通性），因此需要一种无需梯度、可灵活嵌入任意约束的逆向设计框架。

Method: 以高斯径向基函数构建的水平集表示三维几何，仅用数百个设计变量即保留充分几何自由度；在此紧凑表示上采用无梯度优化（避免伴随计算），从而可调用任意Maxwell求解器及导数不可得/难以求取的辅助约束算法，并支持高度并行（embarrassingly parallel）流程；结合连通分量分析算法严格强制材料与空气孔的结构完整性。

Result: 在严格保证设计材料与空隙结构连通性的条件下成功优化Y分支器；验证了无梯度LSM可精确施加二值制造约束，避免梯度法中常用的可微近似；紧凑变量表示使无梯度优化在全三维器件上具备可行性。

Conclusion: 无梯度优化与水平集方法的结合为3D增材制造纳米光子器件提供了一种实用逆向设计途径，可严格处理不可微制造约束并兼容任意求解器与并行工作流，拓展了三维纳米光子设计的约束处理能力。

Abstract: Advances in 3D nanophotonic additive manufacturing enable increasingly complex, almost free-form devices for manipulating light at sub-wavelength scales. Extending nanophotonic designs into three dimensions introduces additional degrees of freedom. Exploiting this freedom requires inverse-design methods that can efficiently navigate this large design space while accommodating fabrication constraints. Here, we combine gradient-free optimization with the level-set method (LSM) to provide such an approach for 3D additive manufacturing. Representing the 3D geometry as a level set constructed from Gaussian radial basis functions retains substantial geometric freedom with only a few hundred design variables. This compact representation makes gradient-free optimization practical for fully 3D nanophotonic devices, eliminating the need for adjoint calculations. This unlocks the use of any Maxwell solver or auxiliary algorithms that should express fabrication constraints for which the required derivatives are unavailable or impractical to obtain, and enables embarrassingly parallel workflows. Consequently, binary fabrication requirements, such as structural connectivity, can be rigorously enforced, avoiding the differentiable approximations commonly used in gradient-based optimization. Here, we demonstrate the combination of the gradient-free LSM with a connected-component analysis algorithm to enforce the structural integrity of the design. We demonstrate the applicability of the gradient-free LSM by optimizing a Y-splitter while strictly enforcing the structural integrity of the material and the void in the design.

</details>


### [158] [Towards dense optical communications with partially coherent light: I. Bimodal Stokes communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15701)
*Ayman F. Abouraddy,Bahaa E. A. Saleh*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出"Stokes通信"(S-Comm)方案，利用部分相干光的多模斯托克斯参数，将N_D=N_f²−1个独立数据流复用进N_f个光模式，突破传统复用容量限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统光复用中N_f个正交光谱/空间模式仅能承载N_f个独立数据流，信道容量受模式数线性限制；部分相干光的高信息承载潜力长期被忽视。

Method: 以N_f×N_f厄米相干矩阵的广义斯托克斯参数作为调制对象，而非调制各模式幅度。给出N_f=2（双模场）设计：3个数据流经开关键控(off-keying)编码，经由相干调制器（将数据流编码进双模相干度）、斯托克斯编码器（将数据流分配至指定斯托克斯参数）、斯托克斯复用器（合成物理模式）；接收端执行逆操作恢复数据流。

Result: 在N_f=2时成功将N_D=3个数据流复用至两个模式，验证了收发机设计方案；模式数增大时信道容量预计显著提升，且方案利于片上集成。

Conclusion: 部分相干光的未被利用的高信息承载能力可支撑密集光通信；S-Comm为提升光信道容量提供了新途径，并具备集成化扩展前景。

Abstract: In conventional optical multiplexing, $N_{\mathrm{f}}$~orthogonal spectral or spatial modes multiplex $N_{\mathrm{f}}$ independent data streams transmitted over an optical channel (e.g., a multimode optical fiber supporting $N_{\mathrm{f}}$~guided modes). We introduce here a new optical communications scheme that we call `Stokes communications' (S-Comm), whereby $N_{\mathrm{D}}=N_{\mathrm{f}}^{2}-1$ independent data streams are multiplexed into $N_{\mathrm{f}}$~optical modes, potentially yielding a dramatic increase in the channel capacity. Rather than modulating the amplitude of each mode to encode a data stream, we make use of multimode partially coherent light and modulate each generalized Stokes parameter in the $N_{\mathrm{f}}\times N_{\mathrm{f}}$ Hermitian coherence matrix associated with the field. We present here designs for the S-Comm transmitter and receiver making use of bimodal fields ($N_{\mathrm{f}}=2$) in which $N_{\mathrm{D}}=3$ data streams are encoded via on-off keying and multiplexed in two modes. We describe the main blocks in the S-Comm transmitter: coherence modulators that encode a data stream in the degree of coherence of a bimode (a two-mode partially coherent field), Stokes encoders that assign each data stream to a prescribed Stokes parameter, and Stokes multiplexers that combine bimodes into physical modes. We also describe the reverse operations implemented in the S-Comm receiver to recover the data streams. Significantly higher channel capacity is expected for a larger number of physical modes, which is facilitated by the amenability of the presented designs to on-chip integration. These results suggest that the long-appreciated but unexploited higher information-carrying capacity of partially coherent light can help realize dense optical communications.

</details>


### [159] [Towards dense optical communications with partially coherent light: II. Multimode Stokes communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15715)
*Ayman F. Abouraddy,Bahaa E. A. Saleh*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文将“Stokes通信”（S-Comm）方案从双模推广到多模（N_f>2）部分相干光场，给出对角与非对角Stokes参数需分别处理的发射机/接收机详细设计，并指出可用集成光子回路实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在相同模式张成的条件下，部分相干光比相干光携带更多信息量，有望提升光通信的信道密度：一个由N_f个模式张成的部分相干场，其相干矩阵可由广义Stokes参数唯一确定，可复用N_D=N_f²−1路数据流。作者此前仅针对双模（N_f=2，N_D=3）提出S-Comm实现，本文旨在将其扩展到多模情形。

Method: 将S-Comm策略推广至N_f>2的多模场；分析并指出对角与非对角Stokes参数需要不同的处理方式；针对对角复用与非对角复用的数据流，分别给出收发两端的详细设计方案，并使各功能模块适配集成光子电路平台。

Result: 获得多模S-Comm的收发机完整设计：对角与非对角Stokes参数分别采用不同的复用/解复用结构，可支持N_D=N_f²−1路数据流的复用传输；所提系统的构建模块可由集成光子电路平台直接实现。

Conclusion: 多模部分相干光（“结构化相干”）的片上处理对光通信及更广泛的光信号处理具有深远意义。

Abstract: Partially coherent light has a higher informational-carrying capacity than coherent light when both fields are spanned by the same modes, which implies that partially coherent light may increase the channel density in optical communications. A partially coherent field spanned by $N_{\mathrm{f}}$ modes can multiplex $N_{\mathrm{D}}=N_{\mathrm{f}}^{2}-1$ data streams in the generalized Stokes parameters that uniquely identify its coherence matrix -- a scheme we call `Stokes communications' (S-Comm). We have proposed in an accompanying paper a realization of S-Comm for bimodal fields $N_{\mathrm{f}}=2$ that multiplex $N_{\mathrm{D}}=3$ data streams. Here we extend this proposed strategy for S-Comm to multimode fields $N_{\mathrm{f}}>2$. We find that the diagonal and off-diagonal Stokes parameters require different treatments and we provide detailed designs for the transmitter and receiver for both diagonally and off-diagonally multiplexed data streams. The building blocks of the proposed system can be readily implemented in integrated photonic circuit platforms. Our results indicate that on-chip processing of multimode partially coherent light (or `structured coherence') may have profound implications for optical communications and for optical signal processing in general.

</details>


### [160] [Metasurfaces with Anisotropic Electric and Magnetic Conductivities: Parameter Retrieval for Arbitrary Incidence and Polarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15752)
*Dimitrios Chatzidimitriou,Alexandros Pitilakis,Odysseas Tsilipakos*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出一种在任意偏振斜入射下由单向散射数据解析反演各向异性超表面等效表面电导率（含磁可极化）的公式，并给出由电导率张量正演反射/透射系数的逆表达式，经微波与THz电磁/磁元胞验证并解释偏振转换机理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统超表面等效参数反演方法局限于各向同性元胞、均匀衬底，或需同时获取两侧反射与透射系数，实验实施复杂；需要一种不需双向散射信息、适用于各向异性元胞与斜入射任意偏振的简化反演方案。

Method: 推导电、磁可极化各向异性超表面在斜入射任意偏振下的等效表面电导率解析表达式，仅依赖单向散射（反射或透射）信息；以微波频段电磁元胞与THz频段磁元胞为代表性结构进行验证，并基于反演得到的各向异性电导率分析其偏振转换能力；同时给出由已知表面电导率张量计算反射与透射系数的逆（正演）表达式。

Result: 解析公式经微波电磁元胞与THz磁元胞验证有效；反演所得各向异性电导率为偏振转换行为提供了物理解释；所给出的正演表达式可用于超表面综合与辐射问题。

Conclusion: 所提方法简化了各向异性超表面的等效参数提取（无需双向散射数据），兼具反演与正演能力，适用于微波与THz频段，对超表面综合设计与辐射分析具有实用价值。

Abstract: Metasurfaces come in a great variety of unit cell geometries and material composition. A common approach for describing them relies on retrieving effective surface parameters (e.g., electric and magnetic conductivities) from plane-wave scattering data. Conventional retrieval methods are often limited to isotropic unit cells, a uniform host material, or lead to increased complexity by requiring reflection and transmission coefficients from both sides of the metasurface. Here, we derive analytical expressions for the equivalent surface conductivities of an electrically- and magnetically-polarizable anisotropic metasurface under oblique plane-wave incidence with arbitrary polarization. The proposed formulation does not require bidirectional scattering information, thus simplifying the process especially in experimental studies. The method is validated through representative electric and magnetic meta-atoms at microwave and THz frequencies, respectively. Their ability for polarization conversion is demonstrated and discussed relying on the physical insight provided by the retrieved anisotropic conductivities. In addition, we present the inverse expressions for calculating the reflection and transmission coefficients from known surface conductivity tensors, which can prove useful for metasurface synthesis and radiation problems.

</details>


### [161] [Subpicosecond Optical Time Transfer under Simulated Motion with Electro-Optic Frequency Combs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15763)
*Nolan Matthews,Joshua Olson,Robert Rockmore,Sean Krzyzewski*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 利用电光频率梳构建运动条件下双向光学时间传递的地面模拟器，在km级光纤链路上验证了速度线性增至400 m/s时亚皮秒精度的时钟偏移连续跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能光学频率梳时间传递走向实际系统时受平台运动（尤其大相对速度）挑战，缺乏可控的运动环境验证手段。

Method: 以敏捷电光频率梳（electro-optic frequency comb）搭建实验室内的双向光学时间传递运动模拟器，通过直接调控频率梳参数人为引入相对运动效应；在km量级光纤链路上测试速度曲线从静止线性增至400 m/s的时间传递性能。

Result: 经后处理数据分析，在整个速度扫描过程中实现两站点间时钟偏移的连续跟踪，精度达亚皮秒（subpicosecond）量级。

Conclusion: 结果表明电光频率梳在已部署系统中的光学时间传递中具有良好通用性与应用潜力。

Abstract: Extension of high performance optical frequency comb-based time transfer to real-world systems is challenged by platform motion, particularly for large relative velocities. Here, we utilize agile electro-optic frequency combs to construct a laboratory-based simulator of a two-way optical time transfer system under motion. Through direct control of the electro-optic frequency comb parameters, we can artificially induce the effects of relative motion. We performed tests of the time transfer performance across a km-scale fiber link for velocity profiles that linearly increase from rest up to 400 m/s. Through a post-processed data analysis, we demonstrate continuous tracking of the clock offset between sites with subpicosecond precision throughout the velocity sweep. These results illustrate the versatility of using electro-optic frequency combs for optical time transfer in deployed systems.

</details>


### [162] [Intense and stable second-harmonic generation in robust MePEA-based quasi-2D halide perovskite crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15772)
*Grigorii Verkhogliadov,Dmitry Semyonov,Elizaveta Sapozhnikova,Dmitry Tatarinov,Marina Tepliakova,Nina Ratmanova,Ivan Andreev,Ivan Melnichenko,Natalia Kryzhanovskaya,Alexey Zhukov,Quynh Le-Van,Daria Khmelevskaia,Anatoly Pushkarev*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 研究探讨苯乙铵与4-甲基-2-苯乙铵间隔阳离子对准二维卤化物钙钛矿晶体对称性及二次谐波响应的影响，并证实间隔阳离子诱导的对称破缺与非线性光学活性之间的直接关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准二维卤化物钙钛矿可通过有机间隔阳离子的变化调控结构与光学性质，是潜在的非线性光学平台，但间隔阳离子如何影响晶体对称性并决定二次谐波产生（SHG）响应尚缺乏系统认识。

Method: 对比苯乙铵（PEA）与4-甲基-2-苯乙铵间隔阳离子、不同单八面体层数n的准二维钙钛矿相；采用腔体限域结晶法生长横向尺寸达数百微米的高质量单晶；进行SHG光学表征，并结合功率依赖与偏振分辨测量。

Result: 所制备晶体呈现强且时间稳定的SHG信号；功率依赖与偏振分辨测量确认信号的二阶非线性起源，并揭示各向异性发射图样。

Conclusion: 间隔阳离子诱导的中心对称破缺与非线性光学活性存在直接关联，准二维卤化物钙钛矿可作为对称性工程化的高效频率转换与集成非线性光子学材料。

Abstract: Quasi-2D halide perovskites offer a unique platform for nonlinear optics applications, where structural and optical properties are tuned by the variation of organic spacer cations. Notably, quasi-2D perovskite single crystals can demonstrate intense and stable second-harmonic generation signals when a proper composition is selected. Here, the influence of phenethylammonium and 4-methyl-2-phenylethylammonium spacers on the crystal symmetry and second-harmonic signal response of quasi-2D perovskite phases with a varying number of single-octahedron [PbX$_6$]$^{4-}$ layers ($n$) is investigated. High-quality perovskite crystals with lateral dimensions of several hundred micrometers are fabricated using a cavity-confined crystallization synthesis method. Optical characterization of the crystals shows strong and temporally stable second-harmonic generation. Power-dependent and polarization-resolved measurements confirm the second-order nonlinear origin of the observed response and reveal anisotropic emission patterns. Our results demonstrate a direct correlation between spacer-induced center-symmetry breaking and nonlinear optical activity. These results establish quasi-2D halide perovskites as a promising class of symmetry-engineered materials for efficient frequency conversion and integrated nonlinear photonics.

</details>


### [163] [Efficient refractive-index sweeps in the discrete dipole approximation using shifted Krylov methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15909)
*Clément Argentin,Konstantin G. Inzhevatkin,Maxim A. Yurkin*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 将移位Krylov子空间方法引入DDA，复用同一几何在多复折射率下的求解，大幅降低矩阵-向量积次数（130倍）与实际墙钟时间（最多40倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散偶极近似（DDA）在光学数据库生成等应用中，需对同一粒子几何在大量复折射率取值下反复仿真，现有做法对每个折射率独立求解，计算时间成比例增长，效率低下。

Method: 对均匀粒子，折射率变化仅影响相互作用矩阵的对角极化率项，对应线性系统仅相差单位矩阵的标量平移。据此采用适配复对称矩阵形式的移位Krylov求解器，并在ADDA代码中实现；在二维复折射率域上以均匀球形粒子进行评测。

Result: 移位求解器的消光截面与独立仿真结果的相对差异低于迭代方法容差；在测试配置下矩阵-向量积次数减少约130倍；大范围折射率扫描的最终墙钟加速最高达40倍。分析了加速比与折射率取值数量、计算后端及收敛率空间变化的关系。

Conclusion: 移位Krylov方法可高效复用DDA中同一几何的多折射率求解，显著降低计算成本；散射场计算已成为新瓶颈，优化后有望进一步提升加速比。

Abstract: The discrete dipole approximation (DDA) is a flexible numerical method for simulating electromagnetic scattering by particles of arbitrary shape and internal structure. In many applications, such as optical-database generation, the same particle geometry must be simulated for many values of the complex refractive index, which are currently handled independently with proportional increase of computational time. For homogeneous particles, however, variations of the refractive index affect only the diagonal polarizability term of the interaction matrix. The corresponding linear systems therefore differ only by scalar shifts of the identity matrix, making them suitable for shifted Krylov-subspace methods. In this work, we use a shifted Krylov solver adapted to the complex-symmetric formulation of the matrix and implement it in the ADDA code. The method is evaluated for a homogeneous spherical particle over a two-dimensional domain of complex refractive indices. The shifted solver reproduces the extinction cross sections obtained from independent simulations with relative differences below the iterative-method tolerance, while reducing the number of matrix-vector products by a factor of 130 for the tested configuration. We further analyze how the achievable speedup depends on the number of refractive-index values, the computational backend, and the variation of the convergence rate across the parameter space. The final wall-time acceleration for large refractive-index sweeps was demonstrated to be up to 40 times, which can be further improved by optimizing the scattered-fields calculations that became a new bottleneck.

</details>


### [164] [What Can Next Generation Ultrafast Light Sources Tell Us About Quantum Materials?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15997)
*Allan S. Johnson*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文是一篇展望性综述（perspective），系统梳理超快光谱研究量子材料时仍缺失的光源能力（如更宽的波段覆盖、更高场强、更短的阿秒脉宽、更好的相干/重复频率与时空分辨率等），并指出这些下一代超快光源可望取得决定性突破的关键开放问题，如解耦强关联自由度、探测非平衡态与隐藏序等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超快光谱已成为现代超快光源的主要应用方向：飞秒脉冲可在时域上解耦量子材料中通常强耦合的内禀自由度，从而揭示驱动各类有序与涌现现象的关键相互作用。在硬X射线至THz、强场非平衡、阿秒脉宽等实验已大量开展的背景下，作者认为有必要（1）反思当前光源工具箱在量子材料研究上仍缺失的能力，（2）明确需要依赖正在上线或设计中的下一代光源才能解决的科学问题。

Method: 以perspective/综述形式进行定性分析：梳理已有超快光源与实验技术的覆盖范围（光子能量从硬X射线到THz、场强足以制备非平衡态、脉宽低至阿秒），对照量子与强关联材料研究的需求，归纳缺失的能力并列举典型开放问题。

Result: 摘要中未给出具体实验或定量结果；主要产出是缺失能力的分类清单（波段、场强、脉宽、相干性/重复频率、时空与能量分辨率等）以及若干可由新光源带来决定性影响的研究方向。

Conclusion: 下一代超快光源在填补上述缺失能力后，将显著推动对量子与强关联材料中相互作用、非平衡态及涌现序的理解；作者呼吁以光源能力与科学问题的匹配为出发点规划未来的超快光谱研究。

Abstract: Ultrafast spectroscopy of quantum materials has emerged as a principle application of modern ultrafast optical sources. Measurements made with ultrashort pulses allow the normally strongly coupled internal degrees of freedom inside these systems to be de-coupled in the time-domain, revealing the key interactions for the different orders that lead to dramatic emergent phenomena. This has naturally motivated a wide range of experiments, and quantum materials have now been studied with ultrafast sources spanning from the hard X-ray to the THz, with fields intense enough to create non-equilibrium states, and using pulses down to the attosecond duration. As the optical spectroscopist's toolbox becomes increasingly complete, it is worth pausing and reflecting on what are the capabilities that we currently lack for examining quantum materials, and what are the problems that require the next generation of ultrafast sources now coming on-line or being designed. Here I will offer an overview of the remaining missing capabilities in ultrafast optical sources as required for studying quantum materials, and identify some outstanding problems where I believe these new sources can provide decisive impact in our understanding of quantum and strongly-correlated materials.

</details>


### [165] [Mapping the High-Field Response of Superconducting Nanowire Single-Photon Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16071)
*Harshit Agarwal,Yueh-Chun Wu,Katyayani Seal,Niels Noordzij,Andreas Fognini,Ronan Gourgues,Gábor B. Halász,Benjamin J. Lawrie,Nirjhar Sarkar*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作通过原位旋转控制，将NbTiN超导纳米线单光子探测器（SNSPD）的暗计数响应测量拓展至14 T，揭示其性能强烈各向异性，并给出高场部署路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNSPD正越来越多地集成到需在大磁场下工作的低温量子传感平台，但其高场工作极限与失效机制尚不明确。

Method: 采用原位探测器取向控制，在最高14 T下表征NbTiN SNSPD的暗计数响应，并以较小磁场下的常规工作性能作为基准进行对比分析。

Result: 性能呈强各向异性：垂直磁场迅速压缩可用偏置电流范围，近平行取向则可在更大总场下工作，12 T仍保持有限工作裕度、14 T仍可测得暗计数响应；角依赖主要由磁场垂直分量主导，符合涡旋介导的超导电流尺度抑制机制；同一场依赖电流尺度亦决定电脉冲幅度，将高场工作与读出性能直接关联。

Conclusion: 研究建立了将SNSPD部署于多特斯拉量子传感与高场低温实验的实用途径。

Abstract: Superconducting nanowire single-photon detectors (SNSPDs) are increasingly being integrated into cryogenic quantum-sensing platforms that operate in large magnetic fields. Here, the high-field limits of these measurements were probed by characterizing the dark count response of a NbTiN SNSPD at up to 14~T using in situ control of the detector orientation and benchmarking that performance against normal detector operation in smaller magnetic fields. The detector performance is strongly anisotropic: perpendicular magnetic fields rapidly reduce the usable bias-current range, whereas near-parallel alignment enables operation at substantially larger total fields, with a finite operating margin retained to 12~T and measurable dark-count response at the 14~T field limit. The angular dependence is governed predominantly by the perpendicular component of the magnetic field, consistent with vortex-mediated suppression of the superconducting current scale. The same field-dependent current scale also determines the electrical pulse amplitude, linking high-field detector operation directly to readout performance. These results establish a practical route for deploying SNSPDs in multi-tesla quantum-sensing and high-field cryogenic experiments.

</details>


### [166] [Optical symmetry breaking for single-image field reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16094)
*Egor Manuylovich*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 工程化相位掩模打破模态乘积简并，实现单幅强度图直接重建42模复数光场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机只记录强度，重建光场通常依赖多次测量或迭代相位恢复；在已知空间模态场中，强度仅由复系数的成对乘积决定，而光学对称性使这些乘积不可区分，导致不同场产生近乎相同的图像。

Method: 优化相位掩模并配合传播，分离成对模态签名，同时保持探测器上的光通量；再用标定线性逆与秩一投影直接恢复模态振幅与相对相位，无需迭代。

Result: 仿真中优化掩模满足随机掩模不满足的条件数与光捕获要求；实验中以空间光调制器或被动扩散器重建42模光纤场，中位复数场误差分别为9.1%和4.6%（对比全息参考）；单帧计算2.1 ms，批量吞吐超过4000帧/秒。

Conclusion: 测量空间的对称性破缺可作为快速单帧光场重建的途径，并将此前5模实验验证扩展至42模。

Abstract: Light carries information in both amplitude and phase, although cameras record only intensity. Reconstructing the optical field is at the heart of wavefront sensing, microscopy and coherent communications. Existing methods often rely on multiple measurements or iterative phase retrieval. In fields described by known spatial modes, intensity is determined by pairwise products of their complex coefficients. Yet optical symmetries can make these products indistinguishable, leaving different fields with nearly identical images. Here, we show that engineering the optical measurement to break these symmetries enables direct reconstruction of the complex field from a single intensity image. We optimise a phase mask followed by propagation to separate pairwise modal signatures while retaining light on the detector. A calibrated linear inverse and rank-one projection then recover modal amplitudes and relative phases without iterative phase retrieval. In simulations, optimised masks satisfy conditioning and light-capture requirements unmet by random masks. Experiments reconstruct 42-mode fibre fields using either a spatial light modulator or a passive diffuser, with median complex-field errors of 9.1% and 4.6%, respectively, against holographic references. This extends an earlier five-mode experimental demonstration of fast intensity-only modal decomposition. Per-frame computation takes 2.1 ms, and batched throughput exceeds 4,000 frames per second. These results establish measurement-space symmetry breaking as a route to rapid single-image optical field reconstruction.

</details>


### [167] [Integrated optical isolators enabled by 2D semiconductors via spin-momentum locking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16125)
*Fernando Martín-Romero,Víctor J. Gómez,Alberto G. Curto*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文通过计算研究基于非互易二维半导体的集成光隔离器，并基于自旋-谷极化与光可调磁性提出全光隔离器新概念，确立二维半导体作为集成非互易器件的先进平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有集成光隔离器多为需外加磁场的磁光隔离器，限制器件集成与应用；且大量非互易效应在替代材料平台上尚未被开发，因此需探索无需外磁场的二维半导体平台。

Method: 采用计算方法系统研究多种基于非互易二维半导体的集成隔离器设计，分析几何结构与光学性质对器件性能的影响；并提出基于自旋-谷极化和过渡金属硫化物单层中光可调磁性的全光隔离器概念。

Result: 揭示了器件几何与光学参数对隔离性能的影响规律；验证了基于过渡金属硫化物单层的全光隔离方案在原理上的可行性，实现常规磁光材料难以获得的全光操作功能。

Conclusion: 二维半导体可作为集成非互易器件的先进材料平台，借助自旋-动量锁定将偏振相关效应与传播方向关联，支持无需外磁场的全光隔离等新颖功能。

Abstract: Integrated optical isolators based on spin-momentum locking hold great potential for protecting photonic integrated circuits from unwanted reflections. Currently, the most common implementations are magneto-optical isolators that require an externally applied magnetic field. Nevertheless, a wide range of nonreciprocal effects remains to be exploited in alternative material platforms. Although the majority of these effects induce changes to the propagation of light as a function of its polarization, they can be directly connected to the propagation direction through spin-momentum locking. We employ a computational approach to investigate various designs of integrated isolators based on nonreciprocal 2D semiconductors, studying the influence of their geometry and optical properties on the device performance. Additionally, we propose a novel concept for an all-optical isolator based on spin-valley polarization and optically tunable magnetism in transition metal dichalcogenide monolayers. Our results establish 2D semiconductors as an advanced platform for integrated nonreciprocal devices, enabling functionalities that are difficult to achieve with conventional magneto-optical materials like all-optical operation.

</details>


### [168] [Axial von Mises Framework for Tensor-Dependent Second-Harmonic Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16232)
*Sui Ip Ng,Yi-Ping Ho,Ken-Tye Yong*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文基于单一轴向von Mises纤维取向分布，推导投影纤维轴统计与张量依赖的SHG谐波之间的闭式关系，给出各阶角谐波分解为局部张量系数与轴向圆矩的乘积，并据此分析谐波零点、灵敏度损失与反演歧义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SHG谐波测量被用于推断胶原纤维取向与张量特性，但谐波幅值与纤维角度分布、检测几何之间的定量关系缺乏显式解析框架，导致对谐波零点、反演歧义等物理可辨识性的判断不清。

Method: 假设域内相干发射、域间强度叠加，采用单一轴向von Mises群体分布，推导投影纤维轴统计量与各阶SHG角谐波（平行检偏下含2、4、6阶；理想等权无检偏下含2、4阶）的闭式解析表达式，并用制备角膜的正向/反向记录做经验性提取验证，附加热动力学扩展讨论。

Result: 平行检偏检测含二、四、六次谐波，理想等权无检偏检测含二、四次谐波；公式揭示几何特定的谐波零点、灵敏度损失及次级固定角比值的反演歧义，特别指出无检偏四次谐波在ρ=1/3处的零点并不意味着所有取向灵敏度丧失。制备角膜数据中，纤维束ROI的正反向二次谐波均可分辨但归一化幅值不同，四次谐波未能分辨；中央ROI呈现不同的正反向剖面，低信号覆盖与表观纤维排列在记录间略有差异。

Conclusion: 贡献在于对SHG谐波给出解析解释并明确物理推断的适用条件；测量未能确立绝对磁化率比ρ、轴向浓度κ，也未能实验区分热无序与降解。角度测量的可行性得到支持，但定量归一化形状一致性尚未确立，热学扩展仅说明假定动力学后果而非标定组织轨迹。

Abstract: We derive closed-form relations between projected fibril-axis statistics and tensor-dependent second-harmonic generation (SHG) harmonics using a single axial von Mises population. Under coherent emission within domains and detected intensity addition across domains, each angular harmonic factors into a local tensor coefficient and an axial circular moment. Parallel-analyzer detection contains second, fourth and sixth harmonics; ideal equal-weight analyzer-free detection contains second and fourth harmonics. The resulting formulas identify geometry-specific harmonic zeros, sensitivity loss and ambiguous inversion of a secondary fixed-angle ratio. In particular, the analyzer-free fourth-harmonic zero at $ρ=1/3$ does not imply loss of all orientation sensitivity. Prepared-cornea forward/reverse recordings illustrate extraction of empirical analyzer-free harmonics. In the bundle ROI, second harmonics are resolved in both sweeps, while their normalized amplitudes differ and fourth harmonics remain unresolved. An exploratory central ROI from the same recordings yields a different forward/reverse profile. Within the same operator-identified tissue, low-signal space coverage and apparent fibre alignment vary slightly across recordings. These observations support angular-measurement feasibility while leaving quantitative normalized-shape agreement unestablished. The thermal extension illustrates consequences of assumed kinetics rather than calibrated tissue trajectories. The contribution is an analytical interpretation of SHG harmonics with explicit conditions for physical inference; the measurements do not establish absolute susceptibility ratio $ρ$, axial concentration $κ$, or experimental separation of thermal disorder from degradation.

</details>


### [169] [Bright and Uniform Silicon Carbide Nanophotonic Arrays for Scalable Nanoscale Quantum Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16252)
*Chenyang Liao,Chihang Luo,Xin Chen,Cunliang Xin,Hongfang Zuo,Haoyu Sun,Fazhan Shi,Mengqi Wang,Ya Wang*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 通过自对准工艺结合局部氧离子注入，将PL5/PL6色心确定性定位于4H-SiC纳米柱中心，实现可规模化、超高亮度（~1 Mcps与~2 Mcps）且高度均匀的量子纳米光子阵列，并用于纳米尺度核磁共振探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SiC自旋缺陷具备近表面稳定性、近红外发射与CMOS兼容性，是纳米尺度量子传感的潜力平台；但光子收集效率低、缺陷随机分布，阻碍了实用化、大规模器件的实现。

Method: 将自对准纳米加工工艺与局部氧离子注入相结合，把PL5和PL6色心确定性地定位在4H-SiC纳米柱的精确中心，构建可规模化、高均匀性的量子纳米光子阵列；随后将该器件应用于核磁共振（NMR）实验。

Result: 获得创纪录的光子计数率：PL5约1 Mcps、PL6约2 Mcps，创下SiC色心的新基准，且阵列内亮度近乎一致；同时成功演示对外部纳米尺度核自旋的探测。

Conclusion: 该SiC纳米柱阵列可作为扫描量子探针与可扩展传感器网络的稳健构建模块。

Abstract: Silicon carbide (SiC) spin defects are emerging as powerful platforms for nanoscale quantum sensing due to their near-surface stability, near-infrared emission, and CMOS compatibility. However, poor photon collection efficiency and the reliance on randomly distributed defects hinder the realization of practical, large-scale devices. Here, we demonstrate scalable, ultra-bright, and highly uniform SiC quantum nanophotonic arrays. By combining a self-aligned fabrication process with localized oxygen ion implantation, we deterministically position PL5 and PL6 centers at the exact center of 4H-SiC nanopillars. This architecture yields record-high photon count rates of $\sim$1 Mcps (PL5) and $\sim$2 Mcps (PL6), establishing a new benchmark for SiC color centers while maintaining near-identical brightness across the array. Furthermore, we apply these devices to nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR), successfully demonstrating external nanoscale nuclear-spin detection. These results establish our SiC nanopillars as robust building blocks for scanning quantum probes and scalable sensor networks.

</details>


### [170] [Theory of interferometric background suppression for highly sensitive electro-optic sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16296)
*Hiroaki Ito,Daiki Okazaki,Shigeki Tokita,Masato Ota*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出通过信号光与本振（参考）光相消干涉抑制电光采样中的探测背景光，并用Jones矩阵模型比较五种探测构型，确定平衡探测在强背景抑制下保持线性弱场响应，从而允许更高输入功率而不使光电二极管饱和，实现电光信号增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电光采样中增大探测光功率可增强EO信号，但探测器饱和限制可用功率，而饱和主要由与外加电场无关、幅度远大于EO调制分量的背景光引起。因此需要抑制该背景以提升信号。

Method: 利用电光调制探测光与未调制参考光之间的相消干涉抑制背景；建立Jones矩阵模型对五种探测构型进行对比分析，识别出平衡探测适用于在强背景抑制下维持线性弱场响应。

Result: 背景抑制后可采用更高输入光功率而不使光电二极管饱和，EO信号随之增强；在单管饱和约束下，优化后的信号增强近似与总输入功率的平方根成正比。总输入功率5 W、单管饱和5 mW时，优化构型相比常规平衡探测获得约12倍信号增强。

Conclusion: 通过相消干涉抑制背景并结合平衡探测，可突破探测器饱和对探测光功率的限制，实现按总功率平方根标度的EO信号增强，为高灵敏度电光采样提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In electro-optic (EO) sampling, increasing the probe power enhances the EO signal, but the usable power can be limited by photodetector saturation caused by the much larger probe background independent of the applied electric field. Here, we propose a method for suppressing this background through destructive interference between an EO-modulated probe and an unmodulated reference beam. Using a Jones-matrix model, we compare five detection configurations and identify balanced detection as suitable for maintaining a linear weak-field response under strong background suppression. This suppression allows higher input optical power to be used without saturating the photodiodes, thereby enhancing the EO signal. Under a per-photodiode saturation constraint, the optimized signal enhancement scales approximately as the square root of the total input power. For a total input power of 5 W and a saturation limit of 5 mW per photodiode, the optimized configuration yields an approximately 12-fold signal enhancement over conventional balanced detection.

</details>


### [171] [An optical-frequency-comb-enabled unified infrastructure for quantum networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16349)
*Yiting Lin,Zishuo Ren,Yunkun Zhao,Jiayu Ma,Zimeng Li,Yang Sun,Yufei Zhang,Yanhao Sun,Lei Yang,Xiangyu Wang,Kemi Xu,Ziyang Chen,Hong Guo*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文演示了一个基于光频梳的共享光纤基础设施，统一承载精密授时、经典通信与量子通信：在100 km链路上同时实现100 Mbit/s数据与1 s积分下0.85 fs的授时稳定度，并通过光谱整形与功率相关过量噪声建模将CV-QKD额外过量噪声降至0.0037 SNU、密钥率损失限制在6.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络需要精密授时与经典通信来协调分布式量子操作，但这两类功能通常由独立基础设施承担，导致系统复杂度上升、可扩展性受限。作者希望以单一物理层同时支撑授时、经典数据与量子信道，从而简化网络架构。

Method: 构建光频梳驱动的统一光纤链路，将经典数据编码于频梳强度上同时保留脉冲时域特性；提出光谱工程策略抑制数据调制与定时提取之间的耦合；建立共传播连续变量量子密钥分发（CV-QKD）信道中功率相关的过量噪声模型，并以光功率、波长分配与传播方向作为主要控制参数进行优化；进一步利用量子安全时间戳实现绝对时间同步。

Result: 在100 km光纤上同时实现100 Mbit/s数据传输与1 s积分下0.85 fs的授时稳定度；优化后系统仅引入0.0037 SNU的额外过量噪声，密钥率损失限至6.7%；量子安全时间戳传输额外实现1 s积分下0.26 fs稳定度的绝对时间同步。

Conclusion: 光频梳可作为集成量子网络多项服务的通用物理层，实现授时、经典通信与量子通信的共纤融合，为可扩展量子网络基础设施提供可行方案。

Abstract: Quantum networks require precision timing and classical communication to coordinate distributed quantum operations, yet these functions are typically supported by separate infrastructures, increasing system complexity and limiting scalability. Here we demonstrate an optical-frequency-comb-enabled infrastructure that unifies precision timing, classical communication and quantum communication over a shared fibre link. By encoding classical data onto the intensity of the comb while retaining its pulse timing, we simultaneously transmit data at 100 Mbit/s and achieve a timing stability of 0.85 fs at 1 s over 100 km. We develop a spectral-engineering strategy to suppress the coupling between data modulation and timing extraction. We further establish a power-dependent model of the excess noise induced in a co-propagating continuous-variable quantum key distribution channel, identifying optical power, wavelength allocation and propagation direction as the principal control parameters. After optimization, the system introduces only 0.0037 shot noise unit of additional excess noise and limits the secret-key-rate penalty to 6.7%. Furthermore, quantum-secured timestamp transmission additionally enables absolute time synchronization with a stability of 0.26 fs at 1 s. These results establish optical frequency combs as a common physical layer for integrated quantum-network services.

</details>


### [172] [Speckle Angle: An Intrinsic Geometric Metric for High-Precision Speckle Metrology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16438)
*Alexander C. Trowbridge,Gabriel Britto Monteiro,Kishan Dholakia*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出“散斑角”几何度量，将归一化散斑图视为高维单位球面点，实现小扰动下的线性响应与最大灵敏度，并给出噪声分解与采集极限分辨率界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有散斑计量用相似度相关指标量化散斑演化，但其梯度在相似度趋近1时消失，导致工作点附近局域灵敏度急剧下降。

Method: 将归一化散斑图映射为单位球面点，定义散斑角作为散斑图间的内禀几何坐标；据此区分扰动动力学与测量噪声，估计非相关噪声与固定图案噪声贡献，推导采集极限分辨率界；并用积分球散斑实验验证。

Result: 散斑角保留既有相似度方法的物理标定，同时在小扰动区呈线性响应并具最大灵敏度；实现噪声分量的直接定量分析，给出分辨率极限，并经实验验证。

Conclusion: 散斑角为不同散斑计量架构提供统一比较度量，并为基于散斑矢量空间轨迹的多维传感奠定基础。

Abstract: Speckle-based metrology exploits the extreme sensitivity of complex interference patterns to perturbations in wavelength, refractive index, displacement and other physical parameters. Existing approaches typically quantify speckle evolution using similarity-based correlation metrics, whose local sensitivity deteriorates near the operating point because their gradient vanishes as the similarity approaches unity. Here we show that normalized speckle patterns represent points on the surface of a high-dimensional unit sphere and introduce the speckle angle: the intrinsic geometric coordinate between speckle patterns. The speckle angle preserves the physical calibration of established similarity approaches while providing a linear response and maximal sensitivity in the small-perturbation regime. This geometric perspective enables discrimination of perturbation dynamics from measurement noise, enabling direct estimation of uncorrelated and fixed-pattern noise contributions and yielding a fundamental acquisition-limited resolution bound. Using speckle generated by an integrating sphere, we experimentally validate the framework and demonstrate quantitative noise analysis using angular coordinates. Beyond improving sensitivity characterization, the speckle angle establishes an original common metric for comparing disparate speckle metrology architectures and provides a foundation for multidimensional sensing based on trajectories through speckle-vector space.

</details>


### [173] [TARANG: an open-source, cost-controlled design workflow for silicon nitride microcomb chips](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16469)
*Divyanshu Tiwari,Esraaj Sarkar Gupta,Anshuman Kumar*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: TARANG：开源Python工具链，从折射率数据到GDSII掩模14步自动化设计氮化硅微环频率梳，含仿真计价/缓存/数值校验，并提出边界网格平均法使色散计算稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微环频率梳芯片设计是一连串耦合问题（反常色散波导、FSR与Q值、总线耦合间隙、光栅耦合器需单步光刻实现），缺乏端到端自动化流程；同时色散仿真中"网格吸附到芯层界面"的常规做法存在浮点舍入不稳定性。

Method: 构建TARANG开源Python包，基于Tidy3D的mode、FDTD、本征模展开求解器，14个阶段完成从表格化折射率到GDSII掩模的设计；云仿真运行前定价并限额，结果按完整仿真指纹缓存，每个数值附数值校验；对色散计算，改为分别取界面内侧与外侧网格求解并取平均。

Result: 证明网格对齐芯层界面会使界面场采样受浮点舍入支配；界面内外平均法使群速度色散D不依赖计算平台且网格间稳定约1 ps/(nm·km)。给出1550 nm（700×1000 nm，D=372.9±0.6 ps/(nm·km)）与780 nm（350×500 nm，D=196.4±0.9 ps/(nm·km)）两款空气包层设计，均含200 GHz微环、临界与过耦合间隙、光栅耦合器及光纤到光纤预算。预测色散与文献两款实测器件偏差1%与4%，独立Lugiato-Lefever求解器由导出参数生成稳定单孤子。

Conclusion: TARANG实现了氮化硅微环频率梳的可复现、可校验端到端设计流，边界平均色散法提升数值稳健性，两款设计经文献验证与孤子仿真确认，可直接流片。

Abstract: Designing a microresonator frequency comb chip means solving a chain of coupled problems: a waveguide with anomalous dispersion that survives fabrication errors, a ring with the right free spectral range and quality factor, a bus gap that sets the coupling regime, and grating couplers, all made in one lithography step. We present TARANG, an open-source Python package that takes a silicon nitride platform from tabulated refractive indices to a GDSII mask in fourteen stages, built on the Tidy3D mode, FDTD and eigenmode-expansion solvers. Every cloud simulation is priced before it runs and capped, every result is cached by a fingerprint of the full simulation, and every quoted number carries a numerical check. We show that snapping the grid onto the core's faces, a common way to steady dispersion calculations, leaves the field samples on the faces to floating-point rounding, and that averaging solves with the grid just inside and just outside the faces makes the group-velocity dispersion $D$ independent of the computer and steady to about 1 ps/(nm km) from mesh to mesh. Two complete air-clad designs are presented, at 1550 nm ($700\times1000$ nm core, $D = 372.9 \pm 0.6$ ps/(nm km)) and at 780 nm ($350\times500$ nm, $D = 196.4 \pm 0.9$ ps/(nm km)), each with a 200 GHz ring, critical and over-coupled gaps, grating couplers, and a fibre-to-fibre budget. The predicted dispersion agrees with two measured devices from the literature to within 1 and 4%, and an independent Lugiato-Lefever solver forms stable single solitons from the exported parameters of both designs, which are ready for fabrication.

</details>


### [174] [Design and optimization of multilevel zone plates for flying focus applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16497)
*Zhengkun Li,Yisong Zhou,Changbo Fu,Yugang Ma*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出2^M台阶调制宽度波带片(MWZP-2^M S)，通过正弦随机宽度的蒙特卡洛采样使MWZP-8S统计上近似Gabor波带片，将高阶焦点抑制四个数量级，并在M=3时把聚焦效率提升至73.7%，实现速度可在亚光速与超光速区间灵活调节、稳定超过100个瑞利长度的飞行焦点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 飞行焦点脉冲速度可与群速度解耦并编程调控，在高强度激光—物质相互作用中潜力巨大；但现有方案难以同时兼顾低成本制造、高阶焦点抑制与高效率。

Method: 设计具有2^M台阶的调制宽度波带片(MWZP-2^M S)：对正弦调制的随机宽度进行蒙特卡洛采样，使MWZP-8S在统计意义上模拟Gabor波带片；同时利用多级相位调制将不透明区域转化为相长相位调制区。

Result: 高阶焦点被抑制约四个数量级；M=3时聚焦效率达73.7%；数值模拟显示焦点速度可在亚光速与超光速区间灵活调节，并在超过100个瑞利长度范围内保持稳定。

Conclusion: 该方案可扩展、成本低，为EUV产生与X射线光学中的飞行焦点应用提供了稳健平台。

Abstract: Flying focus pulses, characterized by programmable velocities decoupled from the group velocity, offer remarkable potential for high-intensity laser-matter interactions. However, achieving low-cost manufacturing while simultaneously suppressing higher-order foci and maintaining high efficiency remains a key challenge. Here, we propose a modulated width zone plate with $2^{M}$ steps (MWZP-$2^{M}$S). By employing Monte Carlo sampling of sinusoidal random widths, the MWZP-8S design statistically mimics a Gabor zone plate, suppressing higher-order foci by four orders of magnitude. Furthermore, the multilevel phase modulation converts opaque regions into constructive phase-shifting zones, significantly boosting the focusing efficiency to 73.7$\%$ when $M=3$. Numerical simulations demonstrate that the focal velocity is flexibly tunable across subluminal and superluminal regimes while remaining stable over 100 Rayleigh lengths. This scalable and cost-effective scheme provides a robust platform for flying focus applications in EUV generation and X-ray optics.

</details>


### [175] [PISim: A Unified Photonic Inference Simulator for Hardware-Aware Cross-Layer Design-Space Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16522)
*Andreas Falkenberg*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: PISim是一个统一的光子推理仿真器，将预训练DNN执行与物理模型耦合，用于光子神经加速器的跨层设计空间探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子神经加速器的可用数值精度、可持续吞吐率和应用精度受电光接口与物理非理想性限制，而现有缺乏能在相同神经工作负载和物理假设下评估不同光学拓扑的跨层EDA框架。

Method: PISim耦合预训练DNN执行与基于物理的模型：6类加速器架构、6种电光调制器、4种光源组织、直接/相干读出方案；内嵌分级损伤算子（控制与ADC量化、有限消光比、散粒噪声、相关RIN、热漂移、传输损耗、通道串扰）及解析循环级接口时序模型。

Result: 在MNIST MLP基准上，识别多损伤下的最优精度工作点（MZM与微环调制器峰值6比特）；量化VCSEL阵列与并行扇出激光架构间52.5个百分点的精度差距（源于功率分配与相关源噪声）；建模32至128通道发射阵列宽度下接口受限的吞吐率缩放。

Conclusion: 该框架为流片前的精度约束Pareto优化提供了可扩展、可复现的基础。

Abstract: Photonic neural accelerators offer ultra-low latency linear algebra, yet their usable numerical precision, sustainable throughput, and application accuracy remain tightly bounded by surrounding electro-optical interfaces and physical non-idealities. Pre-fabrication design-space exploration (DSE) requires a cross-layer electronic design automation (EDA) framework capable of evaluating disparate optical topologies under identical neural workloads and physical assumptions. We present \textbf{PISim}, a unified Photonic Inference Simulator that couples pretrained deep neural network execution with physics-based models of six accelerator architecture families, six electro-optic modulator technologies, four optical source organizations, and direct or coherent readout schemes. PISim embeds stage-resolved impairment operators---covering control and analog-to-digital quantization, finite extinction ratio, shot noise, correlated relative-intensity noise (RIN), thermal drift, propagation loss, and channel crosstalk---alongside an analytical cycle-level interface timing model to expose electronic-optical bottlenecks. Using simulated MNIST multilayer perceptron (MLP) benchmarks, we demonstrate how PISim identifies optimal precision operating points under multi-impairment stress (peaking at 6 bits for Mach--Zehnder and microring modulators), quantifies a 52.5 percentage-point accuracy gap between VCSEL-array and parallel-fanout laser architectures arising, under the modeled conditions, from power partitioning and correlated source noise, and models interface-limited throughput scaling across emitter-array widths from 32 to 128 channels. This comprehensive framework establishes an extensible, reproducible foundation for accuracy-constrained Pareto optimization prior to photonic tape-out.

</details>


### [176] [Phonon-mediated visible upconversion luminescence in femtosecond 1030-nm pumped Tm3+ -doped silica fibres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16582)
*Nicolò Pisani,Raffaele Filosa,Alessia Cirimele,Lucas Zanaglia,Jan Aubrecht,Michael Kamrádek,Ivan Kašík,Ivo Bartoň,Ondřej Podrazký,Jan Mrázek,Mario Ferraro*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 研究掺铥铝硅酸盐光纤在1030 nm飞秒脉冲列激发下的多光子吸收可见发光，观察到457 nm蓝光被475 nm青光逐渐取代的光谱交叉现象及792 nm近红外发光的双线性增长拐点，并用宿主热力学率方程模型将其归因于纤芯累积热致多声子非辐射转移与声子辅助交叉弛豫。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高功率超快光纤系统中发光光谱随功率变化的演化机制尚不清晰，需区分永久性色心"日照"效应与热激活的瞬态光谱演化，以评估系统的热与光谱稳定性。

Method: 以1030 nm飞秒脉冲列（MHz重复频率）激发掺铥铝硅酸盐光纤，测量可见与近红外发光随平均功率的变化；采用定标的光谱带积分结合基于宿主热力学的结构信息率方程模型进行机理分析。

Result: 发现光谱交叉：457 nm深蓝发射带随注入平均功率增加逐渐被475 nm青色发射带超越；792 nm近红外发射呈持续单调增长并具有明确的双线性斜率转折。模型表明可见光谱演化由累积纤芯加热激活的多声子非辐射转移与声子辅助交叉弛豫主导，而非永久性色心损伤。

Conclusion: 掺铥铝硅酸盐光纤的多光子激发发光演化具有热驱动的可预测性，为高功率超快光纤系统的热-光谱稳定性提供了新的预测性认识。

Abstract: We investigate multiphoton absorption-driven visible luminescence in a thulium-doped aluminosilicate optical fiber excited by an ultrafast 1030-nm femtosecond pulse train in the {MHz domain}. We report the observation of an unusual spectral crossover: the predominance of an emission band centered at 457-nm (deep blue) is progressively overtaken by a second emission, centered at 475-nm (cyan), as launched average power increases. Concurrently, a third near-infrared emission around 792~nm exhibits persistent, monotonic growth characterized by a well-defined bilinear slope transition. By combining calibrated spectral band integration with a structure-informed rate-equation model based on host thermodynamics, we show that the visible spectra evolution is governed by non-radiative multi-phonon transfer and phonon-assisted cross-relaxation activated by cumulative core heating, rather than permanent color-center solarization. These results offer new predictive insights into the thermal and spectroscopic stability of high-power ultrafast fiber systems.

</details>


### [177] [Optical fiber shape sensing using picosecond spatiotemporal single-photon reflectometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16717)
*Harikumar K Chandrasekharan,Saba N. Khan,Muhammad Afnan Ansari,Robert R. Thomson*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出一种基于单端探测的光纤形状重构新范式：用时间分辨32×32 SPAD阵列探测多模光纤背向散射单光子的时空分布，将其映射到低维几何流形，实现连续弯曲角估计与未见二维/三维形状的重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅从可及的单端推断柔性光纤的三维构型是光子传感的基础性难题；现有方法依赖特种多芯光纤、相干干涉测量或分布式光谱编码，硬件复杂、成本高、适用范围受限，亟需一种无需特种光纤的单端几何传感方案。

Method: 采用激光工程化的多模光纤，用时间分辨32×32单光子雪崩二极管（SPAD）阵列在近端进行探测；利用光纤形变在背反射光子的空间分布与到达时间上留下的独特"指纹"，将高维光子返回状态映射到低维几何流形，从而由单端测量反演弯曲角与二维/三维形状。

Result: 像素级信噪比中位数约26 dB，重复性变化低于1%；可连续估计弯曲角，并能重构此前未参与训练的二维与三维形状，全部测量均在光纤近端完成。

Conclusion: 多模单光子时空动力学可作为信息丰富的分布式几何传感通道，建立了单端光纤形状重构与微创光子导航的新框架，摆脱了对多芯光纤、相干干涉等专用硬件的依赖。

Abstract: Inferring the three-dimensional configuration of a flexible optical fiber from a single accessible end remains a fundamental challenge in photonic sensing. Existing approaches typically require specialised multicore fibers, coherent interferometry, or distributed spectral encoding. Here, we demonstrate a fundamentally different sensing paradigm in which fiber geometry is reconstructed from the spatiotemporal dynamics of returning single photons. Using a laser-engineered multimode fiber interrogated by a time-resolved 32x32 single-photon avalanche diode array, we show that fiber deformation imprints unique signatures onto the distribution and arrival time of back-reflected photons. We map these high-dimensional photon-return states onto a low-dimensional geometric manifold, enabling continuous bend-angle estimation and reconstruction of previously unseen two- and three-dimensional shapes from measurements acquired entirely at the proximal fiber end. Measurements show a median pixel-wise signal-to-noise ratio of approximately 26 dB and below 1% repeatability variation. Our results establish multimode single-photon dynamics as an information-rich channel for distributed geometric sensing, introducing a new framework for single-ended fiber shape reconstruction and minimally invasive photonic navigation.

</details>


### [178] [Chiral high harmonic generation via coherent superposition of multi-channel interband transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16721)
*Ruoxuan Ding,Chen Qian,Zhilin Li,Tianchen Hu,Chunmei Zhang,Tao Dong,Dong Wu,Li Yue,Nan-Lin Wang*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文揭示了一种新机制：单个线偏振驱动场即可在纯净晶体（Weyl半金属NbP）中产生高椭圆率的手性高次谐波，其椭圆率源于多个带间跃迁谐波（不同线偏振与相对相位）的相干叠加，无需多维驱动场或材料/光路工程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 圆偏振光（含特定手性）是研究材料结构、电子、磁性与拓扑手性性质的重要工具，因此人们希望获得波长可调的圆/椭圆偏振相干光源。现有手性HHG方案通常依赖多维驱动场、材料工程或复杂光学架构，复杂度高，亟需寻找更简单、普适的机制。

Method: 采用单一的近红外线偏振场驱动Weyl半金属NbP，测量其高次谐波的偏振态（椭圆率）；并用半导体Bloch方程（SBEs）对谐波偏振的微观起源进行理论建模与解析，进一步将驱动光子能量移至中红外以检验机制。

Result: NbP在线偏振近红外驱动下产生具有显著椭圆率的高次谐波。SBE计算表明椭圆率来源于多个带间跃迁通道所产生谐波的相干叠加——各通道谐波具有不同的线偏振方向与相对相位。将驱动光子能量移至中红外后，可及带间跃迁通道数减少，椭圆率被强烈抑制，从而验证了该机制。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一种简单且普适的手性HHG机制：量子材料可将表观无手性的驱动场转化为偏振可调的超快手性辐射，无需复杂驱动场或结构设计，为手性光源与手性探测提供了新途径。

Abstract: Circularly polarized light, with its specific handedness, is a valuable tool for investigating the chirality related properties of materials, including their structural, electronic, magnetic and topological characteristics. This has led to efforts in creating wavelength-tunable coherent light with circular or elliptical polarization through a sub-cycle ultrafast process high-harmonic generation (HHG). Existing approaches to chiral HHG, however, typically rely on multidimensional driving fields, materials engineering or complex optical architectures. Here we uncover a distinct mechanism without such complexity, where a single linearly polarized driving field generates chiral HHG from a pristine crystal. We show that, in the Weyl semimetal NbP, one linearly polarized near-infrared driving field generates high harmonics with pronounced ellipticity. Semi-conductor Bloch equations (SBEs) reveal that this ellipticity originates from the coherent superposition of multiple interband transitions, each contributing harmonics with distinct linear polarizations and relative phases. We further substantiate this mechanism by shifting the driving photon energy into the mid-infrared, which strongly suppresses the ellipticity as the number of accessible interband transition channels is reduced. These results establish a simple and general mechanism for chiral HHG, and show that quantum materials can transform an apparently achiral driving field into polarization-tunable ultrafast chiral emission.

</details>


### [179] [Enabling Mie Resonances for Optomagnetism in Epitaxially Grown Garnet Nanodisks via Photonic Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16788)
*P. V. Zorina,D. O. Ignatyeva,D. M. Krichevsky,V. I. Belotelov*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出与GGG外延兼容的全石榴石光子晶体衬底，用于调控单个BiIG纳米盘的光磁响应，使逆法拉第效应的轴向光场贡献峰值提升约6.3–7.9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在石榴石体系中实现光磁（逆法拉第）效应的增强通常依赖改变纳米结构几何，缺乏通过光子环境在保持几何不变前提下对光磁响应幅值与空间分布进行调控的手段。

Method: 设计可与钆镓石榴石（GGG）外延生长的全石榴石光子晶体衬底，将单个铋铁石榴石（BiIG）纳米盘置于其上；通过改变光子环境修正电、磁偶极贡献的谱位置与相对强度，并重新分布盘内光场；与前驱均匀衬底上的参考纳米盘（在其多极响应叠加的宽峰附近评估）对比，计算轴向光场对逆法拉第效应的贡献。

Result: 在考察截面内，磁偶极主导与电偶极主导情形下峰值轴向光场贡献分别增强约7.3倍与6.3倍；在多极散射叠加响应最大值处增强约7.9倍。

Conclusion: 该工作证明可在不改变纳米盘几何的前提下，通过全石榴石光子晶体衬底的光子环境实现局域光磁响应幅值及其空间分布的谱调控，为可外延集成的光磁纳米光子器件提供途径。

Abstract: An all-garnet photonic-crystal substrate compatible with epitaxial growth on gadolinium gallium garnet is proposed for controlling the optomagnetic response of a single bismuth iron garnet nanodisk. The photonic environment modifies the spectral positions and relative strengths of the electric- and magnetic-dipole contributions and redistributes the optical field inside the disk. Compared with a reference nanodisk on a homogeneous substrate evaluated near the broad maximum of its summed multipolar response, the peak axial optical contribution to the inverse Faraday effect in the examined cross-sections is enhanced by factors of approximately 7.3 and 6.3 in the magnetic- and electric-dipole-dominated regimes, respectively. The enhancement reaches approximately 7.9 at the maximum of the summed multipolar scattering contributions. These results demonstrate spectral control of the magnitude and spatial distribution of the local optomagnetic response through the photonic environment, without changing the nanodisk geometry.

</details>


### [180] [Ultracompact and Nonvolatile On-chip Switch Based on DBS and PSO Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16855)
*Sanaz Zarei*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 基于SOI-Sb2Se3混合平台，用DBS与PSO逆设计出非易失1×2光子开关，尺寸6.3×31 μm，1550 nm处插损<0.96 dB、串扰<-12 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现片上非易失、小尺寸、低损耗的1×2光开关，利用相变材料Sb2Se3的非易失特性降低静态功耗，并通过逆设计提升器件性能。

Method: 在SOI-Sb2Se3混合平台上构建由最少可切换相变单元组成的器件结构，采用直接二进制搜索（DBS）与粒子群优化（PSO）进行逆设计；器件通过全部Sb2Se3单元在晶态与非晶态之间整体（en-masse）切换实现开关功能；用三维FDTD仿真评估性能。

Result: 器件尺寸仅6.3 μm×31 μm；在1550 nm波长下插损小于0.96 dB，3D FDTD仿真中串扰低于-12 dB。

Conclusion: 该工作表明SOI-Sb2Se3混合平台结合DBS/PSO逆设计可实现紧凑、低损耗、低串扰的非易失1×2光子开关，为可重构光子集成回路提供可行方案（性能数据目前基于仿真）。

Abstract: A 1x2 photonic switch based on silicon-on-insulator (SOI)-Sb2Se3 hybrid platform is inverse-designed using direct binary search (DBS) and particle swarm optimization (PSO) algorithms. The nonvolatile on-chip switch is designed to consist of minimum switchable phase-change units and operates based on the en-masse switching of all Sb2Se3 cells from crystalline to amorphous and vice-versa. The device has a small footprint of only 6.3umx31um. At the wavelength of 1550nm, it exhibits an insertion loss of less than 0.96dB, and its crosstalk is measured to be less than -12dB within the three-dimensional (3D) finite-difference time-domain (FDTD) simulations.

</details>


### [181] [Inverse Design of an On-chip Phase-Change Switch Composed of Sparse Heterogeneous Metasurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16863)
*Sanaz Zarei*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文逆向设计了一种基于片上异质一维超表面的1×2硅基光子开关，超原子由SiO₂或相变材料Sb₂Se₃构成，采用稀疏参数化结合三元搜索与粒子群优化，实现高消光比与小尺寸（6.3μm×31μm）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电光、热光光子开关存在功耗、速度或结构复杂等缺陷；将相变材料与硅光子开关集成可克服这些不足，但非晶态下Sb₂Se₃与硅折射率对比度小，导致输出消光比偏低，需要新的设计手段提升性能。

Method: 逆向设计1×2片上开关，采用一维超表面结构，超原子可为SiO₂或Sb₂Se₃形成异质超表面；为兼顾结构、制造与开关的简洁性，采用稀疏参数化，并联合三元搜索（TS）与粒子群优化（PSO）算法进行优化。

Result: 获得尺寸仅6.3μm×31μm的紧凑开关；异质超原子设计提升了开关两输出间的消光比，尤其改善了与硅折射率对比度较低的非晶态下的性能。

Conclusion: 异质超表面结合稀疏参数化逆向设计可在小尺寸下实现高消光比相变光子开关，为硅基可重构光路由提供简洁、易制造的设计路径。

Abstract: Photonic switches are exploited for optical signal routing and control within optical communication systems. The integration of phase change materials with silicon photonic switches overcomes the drawbacks of the state-of-the-art photonic switches based on electro-optic and thermo-optic effects. In this work, a 1x2 on-chip switch with a footprint of 6.3umx31um based on on-chip one-dimensional metasurfaces is inverse-designed. The on-chip metasurfaces are heterogeneous, which their meta-atoms can be composed of either SiO2 or the phase-change material Sb2Se3. This helps to the realization of higher extinction ratio between the switch outputs, especially at the amorphous state which has a small refractive index contrast to that of the silicon. To benefit from structural, fabrication and switching simplicity, sparse parameterization is followed in the inverse design of the switch using ternary search (TS) and particle swarm optimization (PSO) algorithms.

</details>


### [182] [Chlorophyll-a Fluorescence-Based Optical Temperature Sensor Using Food-Grade Chlorella Extract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16896)
*Sinei Felix Gonçalves Junior,Giovanna G. S. de Miranda,Leomar S. Marques,Flavio A. M. Marques,Alexandre A. C. Cotta,Jefferson E. Tsuchida,Julio C. Ugucioni,Lidia O. Rosa,Eric Fujiwara,Cristiano M. B. Cordeiro,Jonas H. Osório*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 基于食用级小球藻乙醇提取物中叶绿素-a荧光的温度传感器，在28.6–43.0 °C呈线性热猝灭响应，灵敏度约−8.9×10⁻³ °C⁻¹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索可持续、生物来源的微藻色素作为功能性光活性材料用于光学测温的可行性，替代合成荧光探针。

Method: 以市售食品级单细胞微藻（小球藻）为原料，经简易乙醇萃取获得含叶绿素-a的提取物；以叶绿素-a特征发射作为光学换能参数，利用热致荧光猝灭（升温致发射强度下降）实现温度传感；并进行连续升降温循环的动态测试与光漂白漂移表征。

Result: 28.6–43.0 °C范围内线性响应（R²=0.9963），灵敏度(−8.9±0.2)×10⁻³ °C⁻¹；动态测试验证其在亚度级热振荡下工作，并测得光漂白漂移3.65%·h⁻¹。

Conclusion: 结果证明利用可持续、生物衍生的微藻色素作为光学测温的功能性光活性材料是可行的。

Abstract: This letter reports the development of an optical temperature sensor based on the fluorescence of an extract obtained from \textit{Chlorella}, a unicellular microalga commercially available as food-grade biomass. Using a straightforward ethanolic extraction route, the sensing platform exploits the characteristic emission of chlorophyll-\textit{a} as the optical transducing parameter. The operating principle relies on thermal fluorescence quenching, which promotes a decrease in emission intensity with rising temperature. In the temperature range from $28.6$ to $43.0~^{\circ}\text{C}$, the sensor exhibited a linear response ($R^2 = 0.9963$) with a sensitivity of $(-8.9 \pm 0.2) \times 10^{-3}~^{\circ}\text{C}^{-1}$. Dynamic testing over consecutive heating--cooling cycles confirmed the sensor operation under sub-degree thermal oscillations and enabled the characterization of the system's photobleaching drift ($3.65\%\cdot\text{h}^{-1}$). The results reported herein demonstrate the feasibility of exploiting sustainable, bio-derived microalgal pigments as functional photoactive materials for optical thermometry.

</details>


### [183] [Enhancing optomechanical oscillation amplitude and frequency comb bandwidth by intensity-modulated driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16917)
*Menno H. Jansen,Ewold Verhagen*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 研究在有限腔线宽与有限功率约束下，如何通过强度调制驱动提升光机械自持振荡振幅并拓宽光频梳带宽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光机械自持振荡可作为集成光子电路中紧凑、低噪声的GHz信号源与光频梳，其振幅直接决定时钟功率与梳带宽；而仅靠腔反作用（backaction）驱动的机械振子振幅会因有限腔带宽而饱和，难以在功率受限平台上获得大振幅。

Method: 针对光驱动机械振子，在相同平均输入功率与腔线宽条件下，将输入光强在机械共振频率附近进行调制；采用周期平均的能量平衡分析识别使稳定周期轨道向大振幅移动的附加功率；并用完整运动方程的数值积分在跨两个数量级的协同度及0.1–10的边带分辨比范围内进行验证。

Result: 强度调制可将可达振幅提升至腔带宽所设定的极限附近；数值积分在宽参数范围内确认了增强效果，相对振幅增益在自振荡阈值附近及边带未分辨区最大，实现了反作用单独无法达到的无阈值大振幅振荡；振幅提升带来更大的光场调制深度，从而获得更宽的光频梳并改善梳齿间距稳定性。

Conclusion: 强度调制驱动为功率受限平台提供了一种简单的开环途径，以获得更大的机械振幅和增强的梳带宽。

Abstract: Self-sustained oscillations in optomechanical systems are attractive as compact, low-noise sources of GHz signals and optical frequency combs for integrated photonic circuits. For these applications, the achievable oscillation amplitude directly sets clock power and comb bandwidth. This work studies the maximum amplitude that can be reached through combinations of cavity backaction and modulated optical driving, with realistic resources. For an optically driven mechanical resonator, the oscillation amplitude saturates at a level set by the finite cavity bandwidth. We show that modulating the input intensity near the mechanical resonance frequency raises the achievable amplitude toward this limit at equal mean input power and cavity linewidth. We use a cycle-averaged energy-balance analysis to identify the added power that shifts the stable periodic orbit to larger amplitudes. Numerical integration of the full equations of motion confirms the enhancement across two decades of cooperativity and sideband-resolution ratios from 0.1 to 10. The relative increase in amplitude is largest near the self-oscillation threshold and in the sideband-unresolved regime, enabling threshold-free large-amplitude oscillation where backaction alone fails to do so. The enhanced amplitude leads to a larger modulation depth of the optical field, enabling broader frequency combs, with improved stability of the comb spacing. Intensity-modulated driving thus offers power-constrained platforms a simple open-loop route to larger mechanical amplitudes and enhanced comb bandwidth.

</details>


### [184] [Thulium- and Holmium-Doped Silica Fibres for 2 μm High-Power and Highly-Efficient Lasers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16948)
*Richard Svejkar,Pavel Peterka,Bara Svejkarova,Michal Kamradek,Petr Varak,Martin Grabner,Jan Aubrecht,Ondrej Podrazky,Ivo Barton,Ivan Kasik,Pavel Honzatko*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文综述了2 μm波段掺铥与掺钬石英光纤的研究进展，重点讨论通过热负载抑制与效率提升实现功率定标的材料与波导策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2 μm波段高功率光纤激光器在功率定标中面临热负载与激光效率限制，需要从光谱、材料组分与波导结构层面寻找解决方案。

Method: 综述吸收与发射截面的温度依赖性及其对光谱增益、放大自发辐射和高功率运转的影响；分析结构化纤芯掺铥光纤（高局部Tm³⁺浓度支持双光子-单光子交叉弛豫、降低平均掺杂浓度与纵向热负载、无基座波导）；总结纳米颗粒辅助MCVD制备的掺钬石英光纤，考察Al/Ho比对效率与寿命的影响。

Result: 掺钬光纤中Al/Ho比为决定激光效率的关键组分参数，高绝对氧化铝含量可维持长荧光寿命并支持高Ho³⁺浓度而不过度团聚；由此实现纤芯泵浦掺钬光纤激光器斜率效率达84%、输出功率接近100 W。

Conclusion: 通过调控光谱特性、材料组分与横向掺杂分布，可拓展石英光纤激光器在1.7–2.2 μm波段的性能。

Abstract: Silica optical fibres, originally developed as a low-loss transmission medium for telecommunications, have evolved into a versatile platform for compact and efficient high-power lasers. This review discusses recent progress in thulium- and holmium-doped active fibres emitting within the 2 μm spectral band, with an emphasis on material and waveguide approaches that address power scaling through thermal-load mitigation and enhanced laser efficiency. In particular, the temperature dependence of absorption and emission cross-sections are reviewed, and their implications for spectral gain, amplified spontaneous emission and high-power laser operation are discussed. Structured-core thulium fibres are then considered as a route to combining the high local Tm3+ concentration required for efficient two for- one cross-relaxation with a reduced average dopant concentration, lower longitudinal heat load, and a pedestal-free waveguide design. Finally, recent results on holmium-doped silica fibres prepared by nanoparticle-assisted modified chemical vapour deposition are summarised. Crucially, the Al/Ho ratio is identified as a key compositional parameter governing laser efficiency, while a high absolute alumina content supports a long fluorescence lifetime and enables high Ho3+ concentrations without excessive clustering. These advances have enabled core-pumped holmium fibre lasers with slope efficiencies reaching 84% and output powers approaching 100W. Collectively, these development illustrate how control of spectroscopy, composition, and transverse dopant distribution can extend the performance of silica fibre lasers across the 1.7-2.2 μm spectral region.

</details>


### [185] [Monolayer MoSe2 as an Attenuator of Spintronic Terahertz Emission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16958)
*Hatice Nur Koyun,Alexej Pashkin,Ruslan Salikhov,Artur Erbe,Jürgen Faßbender,Manfred Helm,Stephan Winnerl,Jürgen Lindner*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文用时域THz光谱研究MoSe2单层覆盖的Py/Au与Py/Pt自旋电子THz发射，发现约四倍的信号衰减，归因于自由载流子与三重子对THz的吸收，而非自旋-电荷转换效率下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁磁/二维界面超快自旋-电荷转换对THz自旋电子器件（超快存储、传感器、紧凑THz源）至关重要；但FM/TMD异质结构中的STE信号偏弱，与磁输运报道的显著自旋轨道力矩存在矛盾，需澄清其原因。

Method: 采用时域THz光谱测量单层MoSe2覆盖与未覆盖区域的Py/Au、Py/Pt自旋电子THz发射体，对比其发射幅度；其中Py/Pt裸发射体的THz辐射比Py/Au高约一个数量级，用以检验衰减是否依赖发射体强度。

Result: 单层MoSe2覆盖区域相比未覆盖区域THz发射幅度显著降低；Py/Au与Py/Pt两体系均出现一致的约四倍衰减，尽管二者本征发射强度相差一个数量级，说明该抑制与发射体强度无关。

Conclusion: 衰减归因于金属层电荷转移或激光脉冲光激发产生的自由载流子与三重子对THz的吸收，显著削弱可探测的STE信号；这解释了FM/TMD异质结构中STE信号弱与磁输运中较大自旋轨道力矩之间的矛盾，并提示解读二维TMD异质结构的超快自旋-电荷转换测量时须考虑光致THz衰减。

Abstract: Understanding ultrafast spin-to-charge conversion at ferromagnetic/2D interfaces is critical for developing THz-frequency spintronic devices such as ultrafast memories, sensors, and compact THz sources. We investigate this by studying spintronic THz emission (STE) from Py/Au heterostructures capped with transition metal dichalcogenide (TMD) monolayer MoSe2 using time-domain THz spectroscopy. We observe a pronounced reduction in emitted THz amplitude when a monolayer MoSe2 flake covers Py/Au or Py/Pt emitters compared with uncovered regions. A consistent, approximately fourfold attenuation occurs for both systems, despite an order-of-magnitude larger THz radiation from the bare Py/Pt emitter. These results indicate that the suppression is independent of emitter strength. We attribute the attenuation to THz absorption by free charge carriers and trions that are either present due to charge transfer from the metallic layer or photoexcited by the laser pulse, which can significantly diminish the detectable STE signal. This finding provides an explanation for the discrepancy between weak STE signals from FM/TMD heterostructures and magnetotransport reports of sizable spin-orbit torques in similar systems, and highlights the need to account for photoinduced THz attenuation when interpreting ultrafast spin-to-charge conversion measurements in 2D TMD heterostructures.

</details>


### [186] [Asymmetric Frequency Conversion and Violation of Kirchhoff's Law of Thermal Radiation in Phased Floquet Metasurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17062)
*Yao-Ting Wang,Emanuele Galiffi,Sander A. Mann,Mauro Antezza,Andrea Alù*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 研究三片紧密排列的导电薄片系统，利用Floquet时调制引发表面模式跃迁，实现大幅非对称频率转换，使热辐射显著违反基尔霍夫定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统热辐射受基尔霍夫定律约束（发射率与吸收率相等），实现非互易热辐射通常依赖外磁场，亟需无需磁场的非互易光子方案。

Method: 构建三片间距很近的导电薄片（超表面）系统；对其中一片超表面进行时间调制，借助Floquet物理使表面模式发生跃迁，从而产生非互易频率转换并破坏热辐射的互易对称性。

Result: 获得高度非对称的频率转换，热辐射明显违反基尔霍夫定律。

Conclusion: 该工作拓展了Floquet超表面在波转换与能量操控方面的能力，为无需磁体的非互易光子器件铺路，并可用于热管理与高效光子制冷。

Abstract: We study a system of three closely spaced conducting sheets, whose thermal emission largely violates Kirchhoff's law through nonreciprocal frequency conversion enabled by Floquet phenomena. We leverage surface mode transitions controlled by temporal modulation of one of the metasurfaces, resulting in largely asymmetric frequency conversion. Our findings broaden the capabilities of Floquet metasurfaces for wave conversion and energy manipulation, paving the way towards magnet-free nonreciprocal photonic devices with opportunities for thermal management and efficient photonic refrigeration.

</details>


### [187] [UV and deep-UV ultrafast harmonic generation enhanced by resonant gold nano-pillar metasurface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17037)
*Shroddha Mukhopadhyay,Yuxuan Chen,Hooman Barati Sedeh,Natalia M. Litchinister,Michael Scalora,Jose Trull,Maria Antonietta Vincenti,Crina Cojocaru*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 通过二维周期金纳米柱超表面实现近红外到紫外/深紫外的二次与三次谐波高效超快转换，转换效率分别提升两个和三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属纳米结构可增强非线性光-物质相互作用，但如何在无需复杂几何或混合结构（制造困难）的前提下实现高效、波长可调的紫外/深紫外超快光源仍是需求。作者希望利用简单方形金柱的周期阵列来达成这一目标。

Method: 实验观测结合全矢量数值模拟，研究对象为二维周期金纳米柱超表面；该表面在近红外支持角度可调的等离激元共振，改变泵浦入射角即可调谐共振波长。共振时纳米柱角部附近的强电磁场局域增强非线性作用；并用全波理论建模拟合线性与非线性过程。

Result: 相对平面金镜，纳米结构金属的二次和三次谐波产生效率分别提高约两个和三个数量级；理论与实验高度吻合，验证了共振增强与场局域机制。

Conclusion: 周期排列的方形金柱可通过结构设计调控共振波长，实现增强的二次与三次谐波产生，无需复杂几何或混合结构；该工作深化了对金属表面介导非线性光学过程的理解，并显示金属纳米结构作为紫外/深紫外超快光源的潜力。

Abstract: We report experimental observations and full-vector numerical simulations of second and third-harmonic generation from a two-dimensional periodic gold nano-pillar metasurface, for efficient ultrafast light conversion from near-infrared to the UV and deep-UV spectral ranges. The metasurface is designed to support an angle-tunable plasmonic resonance in the near-infrared spectral range, with its resonance wavelength shifting as a function of the pump angle of incidence. Under resonant conditions, strong electromagnetic-field confinement near the nano-pillar corners enhances the nonlinear light-matter interactions. Compared with a flat gold mirror, the nanostructured metal exhibits enhanced nonlinear frequency conversion, with conversion efficiencies of second and third-harmonic generation increased by two and three orders of magnitude, respectively. Full-wave theoretical modeling of linear and nonlinear light-matter interaction in nanoscale structures validates the experimental results. We demonstrate that periodically arranged square gold pillars can be engineered to achieve enhanced second and third-harmonic generation at wavelengths that can be tailored through the structural design, without requiring complex geometries or hybrid structures that pose fabrication challenges. The close agreement between theoretical predictions and experimental measurements further advances our understanding of surface-mediated nonlinear optical processes in metals and highlights the potential of metallic nanostructures as ultrafast light sources in the UV and deep-UV.

</details>


### [188] [Pseudovortex-assisted generation of first-order vector modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17043)
*L. Marques Fagundes,P. Schneider Lacerda,T. A. Petry Kock,N. Rubiano da Silva,P. H. Souto Ribeiro,R. Medeiros de Araújo*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 用伪涡旋光束替代高斯输入，可将涡旋波片产生的一阶矢量模式保真度从0.815提升至0.921，理论上界约8π/27≈0.931。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 涡旋波片是产生径向、角向偏振等一阶矢量模式（一阶矢量模式）的实用器件，但高斯入射与矢量模式所需的环形（doughnut）轮廓不匹配，会激发寄生高阶模式，限制生成模式的保真度。

Method: 以不含拓扑电荷的环形"伪涡旋光束"作为入射光，用空间光调制器（SLM）产生该输入模式；模式分析采用几何相位补偿的三角形腔，并结合理论建模与实验测量评估保真度及其随输入光束腰斑的变化。

Result: 矢量模式保真度达0.921±0.013，显著优于高斯输入时的0.815±0.003；理论推导给出保真度上界8π/27≈0.931，与实验结果吻合良好。

Conclusion: 以伪涡旋光束作输入可抑制寄生高阶模式，突破高斯输入下的保真度极限，提供一种可靠的高保真矢量模式生成方法。

Abstract: A practical method of generating first-order vector modes like radially and azimuthally polarized modes is using the so-called vortex waveplates. Shining these plates with Gaussian beams produces parasitic higher-order modes besides the target vector beam. The reason is that there is a mismatch between the incident Gaussian mode and the doughnut-shaped profile required for the vector beam. This imposes a limit on the fidelity of the generated mode with respect to the target vector beam. In this work we demonstrate theoretically and experimentally that this limit can be overcome if the input Gaussian beam is replaced with a pseudovortex beam, which is a doughnut-shaped mode without topological charge. We use a spatial light modulator to generate this input mode and a geometric-phase-compensated triangular cavity for mode analysis. We demonstrate vector mode fidelity as high as $0.921 \pm 0.013$, which is considerably better than $0.815 \pm 0.003$ obtained with Gaussian input. We also investigate the fidelity as a function of the input beam waist, leading to a theoretical upper bound for the fidelity of $8π/27 \approx 0.931$, in close agreement with the experimental results. This approach presents a reliable method of generating high-fidelity vector modes.

</details>
